1b1(phal chen): @#//rgya gar skad du/bud+d+hA a ba tang sa ka nA ma ma hA be pu l+yo sU tra//bod skad du/
1b2(phal chen): sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo'//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/
1b3(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das thog ma mngon par sangs rgyas pa/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad shin tu rnam par dag pa 
1b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas pa'i thugs mnga' ba/dus gsum mnyam pa nyid du thugs su chud pa'i ye shes kyi sku mnga' 
1b5(phal chen): ba||'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du skus shin tu khyab pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du dbyangs 
1b6(phal chen): kyi yan lag dang gsung gi ngor shin tu bsgrags pa/mkha' dang nam mkha' lta bu'i mtshan nyid mnyam pa nyid 
1b7(phal chen): du rab tu snang ba'i spyod yul can nam mkha' ltar 'gro ba thams cad kun khyab pa'i snying po byang chub thams cad la 
1b8(phal chen): bzhugs pa'i sku//byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho thams cad kyi nang na rnam par snang ba/'jig rten gyi khams thams
2a1(phal chen): @#//cad du nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ltar shar ba/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad yongs su skye ba bstan pa//rab tu spros pa dus gsum gyi bsod nams rgya chen po rgya mtsho lta bus spyod 
2a2(phal chen): yul longs su dag pa/'od kyi dkyil 'khor dang /'od gzer kha dog mtha' yas pa mnga' ba/chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa'i chos kyi sprin thams cad kyis rnam par 'phrul ba//
2a3(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du bsgribs pa med pa/skra'i rtse mo thams cad du mtha' dang dbus med par rnam par 'phrul ba/byin kyis rlob pa'i mthu mnga' ba/'gro ba thams cad yongs su 
2a4(phal chen): smin par bya ba dang /gdul ba la mngon tu gyur bar yongs su ston pa/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad du 'khor thams cad du 'phos pa med par gshegs pa/yongs su ston pas khyab par 
2a5(phal chen): mdzad pa dngos po med pa'i dbyings kyi mchan nyid thams cad thugs su chud ga 'od gzer gyi sgo thams cad du dus gsum rnam par sprul pa'i byin gyis rlabs yongs su ston pa/sangs 
2a6(phal chen): rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du bskal ba rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab par byin kyis rlabs shin tu bsgrubs pa/yul ma ga da'i chos kyi dgon pa snying po byang chub rdo rje rin po che thams cad ltar gzhom du mi 
2a7(phal chen): tshoms par sra ba'i sa phyogs nor bu rin po che thams cad kyi me tog gis gzhi brgyan pa nor bu'i rgyal po dri ma med par 'bar bas rab tu bsgrub pa/rin po che mang po thams cad kyis rnam pa sna tshogs 
2a8(phal chen): su brgyan pa/yon tan rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa'i rgyan rnams pa sna tshogs yang dag par bstan pa/nor bu'i rgyal po 'od gzer dang /sgra dbyangs kyi rgyal mchan thams cad kyis rab tu bsgrub pa/spos dang 
2b1(phal chen): me tog dang /rin po che'i 'phreng ba dang /dra ba mang po thams cad rnam par bres pa/rin po che'i kha dog zad mi shes pa'i char rab tu 'bab pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa yang dag par ston pa'i/nor bu'i rgyal pos 
2b2(phal chen): shin tu bsgrubs pa/me tog sna tshogs chu bo'i glong lta bus yongs su bstan pas brgyan pa/rin po che'i shing 'od gzer gyi kha dog sdug po dang ldan pa'i yal gas brgyan pa/rgyan thams cad du snang ba shin 
2b3(phal chen): tu 'bar ba'i byin gyis rlabs kyis rnam par sprul pa/yang dag par bstan pa'i sa'i snying po/rin po che'i 'bru thams cad 'byung bas phyogs kun sbyangs shing brgyan pa'i snying po byang chub pa'i shing nor bu 
2b4(phal chen): rin po che rdo rje'i rgyan sna tshogs kyi 'brus/bsgrubs pa'i sdong po dang ldan pa be dur+ya dang /nor bu thams cad kyi rigs 'byung ba'i snying po dang ldan pa'i lhun can/yongs su yon tan mdab mtha' yas pa'i nor bu 
2b5(phal chen): rin po che mang po'i 'bus shin tu brgyan cing rab tu 'phel ba'i yal ga can rin po che thams cad kyi lo ma shin tu 'od gsal bas phyogs kyi sprin tu gyur pa/yal gas thams cad kyi rtse mo las me tog dang 
2b6(phal chen): 'bras bu'i dra bas yon tan rnams pa sna tshogs can rab tu sbyangs pa/rin po che'i 'bras bu sna tshogs shin tu 'bar ba'i snying pos/shing gi rgyan me tog thams cad shin tu rgyas shing rnam par gsal ba/
2b7(phal chen): phyogs bcur 'od kyi dkyil 'khor rin po che rgya che zhing mnga' ba'i 'od gzer lta bur rnam par snang ba/nor bu'i yon tan zad mi shes pa'i char chen po rnams par sprul pa'i yongs su bstan pa/nor bu'i sgo 
2b8(phal chen): thams cad nas byang chub sems dpa'i sprin 'byung ba shin tu bstan pa/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba dang /byin gyi rlabs kyis zad mi shes pa'i dbyangs 'byung ba'i drung na/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
3a1(phal chen): @#//gzhal med khang phyogs thams cad du mngon par snang ba rab tu 'phel ba/rin po che'i rgyal po sna tshogs yon  tan mtha' yas pa'i ka ba bkye ba/lhun rin po che'i me tog rnam pa sna tshog+sa
3a2(phal chen): thams cad kyis brgyan kyis brgyan pa/rgyan mtha' yas pas shin tu klus pa'i 'od gzer snang bas sprin ltar khebs pa/pho brang gi mtshams thams cad nas 'od kyi rgyal mtshan rab tu bkye ba/byang chub
3a3(phal chen): sems dpa'i 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa/rjes su 'dod pa'i snying po can bsam gyis myi khya ba pa/gsung rgya chen po'i dbyangs dang/sangs rgyas kyi 'od nor bu'i rgyal po lta bu'i 
3a4(phal chen): dra bas thams cad yog pa/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis rnam par sprul pa'i 'phrul ba/dmigs pa thams cad du byung ba/sems can thams cad kyi khyim gyi gzugs brnyan rab tu ston pa/
3a5(phal chen): sems skad cig gis sangs rgyas kyi byin kyis rlabs thams cad chos kyi dbyings 'byam klas bar 'phro ba'i snying po can seng ge'i khri chen po sangs rgyas kyi gdan tu gyur pa/rin po che thams cad dang/yid
3a6(phal chen): du 'ong ba'i spos sna tshogs kyi snying po can pad mo'i dra ba 'od gzer mtha' yas pa 'byung pas brtan pa/rin po che dri ma med pa'i 'khor lo'i 'od kun tu 'dal ba/me tog gi phreng ba bzang zhing yid du 'ong ba kha 
3a7(phal chen): dog mtha' yas pas kun nas klubs pa/nor bu'i 'bru sna tshogs kyi shing gi yal ga las 'od shin tu bkye ba/rta babs dang khri 'khri 'apang dang/skar khung khad pa brtsegs pa/rnam pa sna tshogs
3a8(phal chen): mtha' yas legs par brgyan pa nor bu'i rgyal po 'od gzer 'bar ba'i snying po'i sprin thams cad du 'phros pa'i rgyan can/byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho rgya chen po'i gtsug gi rin po ches bzung 
3b1(phal chen): zhing 'od gzer rnam par snang ba/phyogs bcu'i mtshams thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi mtshams thams cad du sang rgyas kyi sprul pa'i 'od kyi snying po'i nor bu'i rgyal pos shin tu brgyan pa/sangs rgyas kyi 
3b2(phal chen): yul gyi sgra dbyangs rgya chen pos chos kyi dbyings khyab pa'i byin gyi rlabs dang /ldan pa la bzhugs shing /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sngon bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang 
3b3(phal chen): mdzad dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thabs gcig tu spyod spyod ba'i dge ba'i rtsa ba can /sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' semsdpa' 
3b4(phal chen): chen po kun tu bzang po dang /byang chub sems dad 'a sems dpa' chen po dge ba dri ma med pa'i gzi brjid kyi 'od yid du 'od ba dang /byang chub sems pa'i sems dpa' chen po yon tan 'od mchog kun tu sgron ma dang 
3b5(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po kun tu 'bar ba seng ge'i rgyal mtshan dang /byang chub sems 'das sems dpa' chen po dpal gyi 'od gzer kun tu 'od 'phro dang /byang chub sems dang sems dpa' chen 
3b6(phal chen): po yon tan rgya mtsho kun tu dbyangs rgyal mtshan dang /byang cub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul ye shes kun tu snang ba'i 'di dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa'i chen po 
3b7(phal chen): gtsug na rin chen kun tu rgyal mtshan dang /byang chu ba sems dpa' sems dpal chen po rgyal ba'i gsung kun tu 'jam dbyangs dang /byang chub sems  dpa' sems dpa' chen pa bsod nams mi zad dri myed kun tu gzi brjid 
3b8(phal chen): dang /byang chub sems dad 'a sems dpa' chen po mtshan gyi 'od gzer kun tu snang ba dang /byang chub sems pa' sems dpa' chen po 'od gzer rgya mtsho 'od chen dpal gyi zla ba dang /byang chub sem dpa' sems 
4a1(phal chen): @#//dpa' chen po sprin gyi nga ro rgya mtsho dri med 'od kyi snying po dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po yon tan rin chen 'byung ba ye shes dpal gyi gtsug phud dang /byang chub sems  dpa'
4a2(phal chen): sems dpa' chen po rgya chen 'od 'bar blo gros dbang phyug dang /byang chub sems pa'i sems dpa' chen po pad mo rnam par gnon pa 'od gzer gtsug phud dang /byang  chub sems dpa' sems  dpa' chen po kun tu ye
4a3(phal chen): shes sprin gyi nyi ma'i rgyal mtshan snang ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pomathu stobs brtson 'grus rdo rje yangs pa'i snying po dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po spos kyi 'od 'phro shin tu 
4a4(phal chen): 'bar ba'i 'od kyi rgyal mtshan dang /byang chub sems ngapa' sems dpa' chen po rnam par snang mdzad yon tan yangs pa'i rgyal mtshan dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bsod nams yangs pa shin tu
4a5(phal chen): 'phags pa'i 'od mchog yon tan 'byung ba'i blo gros dang //de dag lastsogs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba bsnyed kyi/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa
4a6(phal chen): rnam par snang mdzad dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba rgya mtsho gcig gis byung ba pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad kyi 'khor lo yongasu rgyas pas snang bar gyur ba/ye shes kyi mig yongs su dag pa/dus gsum gyi dbyings
4a7(phal chen): thams cad du snang bas gsal bar gyur ba/ting nge 'dzin gyi 'khor thams cad yongs su dag pas snang bar gyur ba/tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa/rgya mtsho yangs shing zad mi shes pa dang /sangs
4a8(phal chen): rgyas kyi yon tan dang mthus snang bar byas pa/'gro ba thams cad yongs su smin ba dang /gdul bar brtsom pa'i ye shes mnyam pa nyid kyis snang bar byas pa/chos kyi dbyings kyi snying po gcig tu tha mi dad
4b1(phal chen): pa'i ye shes kyi sos snang bar gyur ba/sangs rgyas kyi snang bar thar pa zab mo'i yul yangs pa la ye shes kyi spyod yul gyis snang bar byas pa /sa gcig las byung ba yongs su gnon pa'i rjes su 'brang 
4b2(phal chen): bas shin tu snang bar byas pa /smon lam rgya mtsho la 'jug pa phyi ma'i mtha'i byin gyis rlabs kyi ye shes thams cad kyis gnas pas snang bar gyur ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba rmad du byung ba'i 
4b3(phal chen): dkyil 'khor yangs pa yongs su shes pas snang bar gyur pa/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid la rjes su brtags pa'i ye shes kyis shin tu snang bar gyur ba/de bzhinagashegs pa'i sa 
4b4(phal chen): kun tu 'od kyi dbyings dang /gnas su rnam par spyod du dbang ba /ting nge 'dzin rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa'i sgo thams cad du rnam par rgyu bas snang bar gyur ba/ye shes snang ba thams cad kyi rjes su song 
4b5(phal chen): zhing 'gro ba thams cad kyi spyod pa rgya mtsho la shin tu gnas pa/chos rgya mtsho thams cad kyi gzungs dang /tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa bstan pa dang /yongs su bstan pa la mkhas pa/sangs rgyas kyi 
4b6(phal chen): yon tan rgya mtshor gtogs pa thams cad kyis khyab par byed pa'i lus can smon lam rgya mtsho mngon par bsgrub pas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa/sangs rgyas 
4b7(phal chen): thams cad mchod pa'i sbyor ba la phyi mi' mur thug pa'i bskul bar dga' ba'i shugs kyi snang ba rab tu thob pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi zhabs kyi drung du phyin cing snying po byang chub tu nye bar 'dod 
4b8(phal chen): pa yang dag par ston pa/kun tu bzang po'i smon lam rgya mtshos sems can thams cad ma lus pa thams cad kyi shes pa yongs su rgyas par 'gengs pa'i lus can rnams dang thabs gcig go//sangs rgyas 
5a1(phal chen): @#//kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi lag na rdo rje dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lag na rdo rje mthu chen kha dog bzang po'i 'od dang/lag na rdo rje nyi ma'i shugs kyis rgyal mtshan dang /
5a2(phal chen): lag na rdo rje ri rab me tog 'od dang/lag na rdo rje rnam dag sprin kyi dbyangs dang/lag na rdo rje dpa' po shin tu mda' ba dang/lag na rdo rje rdo rje'i 'od yid du 'ong ba dang/lag na rdo rje shing gi sgra dbyangs dang lag na rdo rje sed 
5a3(phal chen): seng ge'i 'od kyi rgyal po dang/lag na rdo rje 'od 'phro stug po'i dpal gyi mig dang/lag na rdo rje rin chen gtsug phud pad mo'i 'od dang/de dag lastsogs pa lag na rdo rje sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phrab snyed kun 
5a4(phal chen): kyang/sngon bskal ba mtha' yas par de bzhin gshegs pa mchod par smon pas zham 'bring na 'bring bar gyur pa/sangs rgyas bsnyen bkur ba'i smon lam gyi pha rol tu phyen pa yongs su rdzogs pa dge ba dang/bsod
5a5(phal chen): nams kyi dkyil 'khor mtha' yas pa yongs su dag pa/ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul yangs pa la rnam par spyod pa/rdzu 'phrul dang stobs dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul mtha' yas ba/khong du chud pa bsam
5a6(phal chen): gyis mi khyab pa'i rnam par thar ba'i yul la zhugs pa/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad kyis zil kyi mi non pa'i lus can rnam ji ltar 'dul bzhin du lus shin tu ston pa/sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa'i sku thams cad 
5a7(phal chen): kyi 'phrul ba rjes su rtogs pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rnam par spyod pa dang/gnas srung ba la shin tu brtson pa rnams dang thabs gcig go/lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba 
5a8(phal chen): snyed dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lha khrel dang dge bas rgyas pa'i 'od dang/'od gzer phyogs su snang ba dang/sgra 'dul ba rgya mtsho'i dpal dang /rgyan bzang po me tog gtsug phud dang/spyod yul mtha' 
5b1(phal chen): yas dpal dang/snang ba'i 'phreng ba 'dzin pa'i dpal dang /spos dri ma med pa'i sprin las char 'bab pa'i 'od dang/srung 'dzin yongs su sdud pa dang/sgo kun nas yongs su gnon pa la mngon tu gyur ba'i mig dang
5b2(phal chen): khrel dang dpal mi g.yo ba'i 'od dang /lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha de dag lastsogs pa kun kyang/de bzhin gshegs pa bsnyen bkur bar smon lam btab pa dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin
5b3(phal chen): tu phra ba snyed dang thabs gcig go//rkang gis 'gro ba'i lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdu la shin tu phra ba snyed dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpal gyi phyag rgya dang/rkang gis 'gro
5b4(phal chen): pad mo snang ba'i 'od gzer gi 'od dang/rkang gis 'gro me tog gtsug phud kyi dpal dri ma med pa dang/rkang gis 'gro shin tu mdzes par gnon pa dang/rkang gis 'gro rin chen dbal gyi rgyal mtshan dang/rkang
5b5(phal chen): gis 'gro dga' ba'i dbyangs snyan pa dang/rkang gis 'gro zhing tsan dan gyi 'od dang/rkang gis 'gro pad mo yid du 'ong ba'i 'od dang/rkang gis 'gror rdul gyi shugs kyi 'od dang/rkang gis 'gro mi tog mang po'i dkyil
5b6(phal chen): 'khor dang/de dag lastsogs pa rkang gis 'gro ba'i lha/sangs rgyas kyi zhing rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun kyang sngon bskal ba mtha' yas par de bzhin gshegs pa'i rjes su 'brang ba sha stag dang thabs gcig
5b7(phal chen): go/snying po byang chub kyi lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dag dang yang thabs gcig ste 'di ltar//snying po byang chub kyi lha dge ba dri ma med pa'i rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i rgyal mtshan dang/snying po
5b8(phal chen): byang chub kyi lha ri rab spo'i rin po che'i 'od dang /snying po byang chub gyi lha dbyangs kyi 'brug sgra sgrogs pa'i rgyal mtshan dang/me tog gi char sna tshogs 'bab pa'i mig dang/'od phro mang po shin tu dri ma med pa dang/
6a1(phal chen): @#//me tog gi phreng ba 'od kyi gtsug phud dang /rin po che'i rgyan kyis char rab tu 'bebs pa dang /mthu dam pa spos kyi myig dang /rdo rje sna tshogs kyi sprin dang /bad mo'i shing snad ba'i 'od dang /snying bo 
6a2(phal chen): byang chub kyi lha dge ba'i lta ba sna tshogs rnam par ston pa'i 'od dang /de dag la stsogs pa snying po byang chub kyi lha/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun kyang /sngon sangs rgyas rgya chen mchod pa'i 
6a3(phal chen): smon lam gyis yang dag par 'byung ba sha stag dang thabs gcig go/nam mkha'i lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul/shin tu phra ba snyed dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar grong khyer gyi lha rin chen rtse mo'i 
6a4(phal chen): 'od dang /rgyan rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung ba dang /rin chen dri med shin tu dga' ba dang /slos pa'i mya ngan thams cad spong bas rnam par snang ba dang /sgron ma shin tu rgyas pa'i mig dang /'od 'phro ba'i dpal mngon 
6a5(phal chen): par 'bar ba dang /bsod nams kyi shugs snang ba'i 'od dang /lus dri ma myed pas mnyam pa'i 'od dang /spos kyi rgyal mtshan gtsug pud kyi rgyan dang /grong khyer gyi lha rin chen dpal gyi 'od dang /de dag la stsogs 
6a6(phal chen): pa nam mkha'i lha/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gis rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du kun kyang /bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzhal med khang rab tu brgyan pa la brtson ba sha stag dang thabs 
6a7(phal chen): gcig go/sa'i lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing rdul shin tu phra ba dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar sa'i lha dpal gyi me tog kun nas shin tu rgyas pa dri ma med pa dang /bsod nams brtan pa shin tu 
6a8(phal chen): bsags pa'i 'od dang /shing gi yal ga sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang /sems can rin po ches rgyas pa dang /dus dus su dge ba'i mig rab tu ston pa dang /gser gyi mig gi dpal dang /spu las spos kyi 'od 
6b1(phal chen): rnam par 'gyed pa dang sgra dbyangs yid du 'ong ba sgrog pa dang/me tog gi glong sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dang/kun nas rdo rje'i rdul gyi rgyan dang/sa'i lha khyim dri ma myed pa'i 'od dang/de dag la stsogs 
6b2(phal chen): pa sa'i lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun kyang bsod nams kyi dbyibs gcig tu de bzhin gshegs pa'i sngon gyi smon lam dang rab tu 'brel ba sha stag dang thabs gcig go/ri'i lha tshad 
6b3(phal chen): med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar ri'i lha rin po che'i rtse mo me tog shin tu rgyas par gnas pa dang/nags tshal sna tshogs me tog shin tu rgyas pa'i gtsug pud dang/kun nas shin tu 'phags pas 
6b4(phal chen): rnam par snang ba'i rgyal mtshan dang/rdul thams cad dang/myi gnas pas dri ma med pa'i gtsug phud dang/snang bas phyogs su gsal ba'i 'od dang/stobs la thub pa med pas snang ba'i 'od dang/kun nas 
6b5(phal chen): gzi brjid dpal gyi dkyil 'khor dang/snang ba'i rdul chu bo'i dkyil 'khor dang kun tu mngon par lta ba'i mig dang/ri'i lha kun nas sra ba rdo rje'i mtshams kyi mig dang/de dag la stsogs pa ri'i lha tshad med 
6b6(phal chen): pa kun kyang/tshul dri ma med pa'i mig gi sgo rab tu thob pa sha stag dang/thabs gcig go/nags tshal gyi lha bsam gyis mi khyab pa dag dang yang thabs gcag ste/'di ltar nags tshal gyi lha yal ga sna 
6b7(phal chen): tshogs kyi sprin dang/nags tshal gyi lha 'od kyi phung po sna tshogs kyi mig yid du 'ong ba'i 'od dang/lo ma dri ma myed pa'i 'phreng ba'i dpal dang/yan lag rab tu 'phyang ba'i gzi brjid dang/dge ba'i 'od 
6b8(phal chen): sna tshogs dang sgra skad yid du 'ong ba'i dbyangs dang/kun nas spos mang po ldang ba'i 'od dang/kun na dpal gyi 'od rab tu 'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor dang/nags tshal gyi me tog dang 'bras bu dang ro'i 
7a1(phal chen): @#//'od dang /re dag la stsogs pa nags tshal gyi lha bsam gyis myi khyab pa kun kyang dga' ba'i shugs mtha' yas pas gsal bar gyur ba sha stag dang thabs gcig go/sman gyi lha mtha' 
7a2(phal chen): yas pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar sman gyi lha phun sum tshogs pa dang /tsan dan gyi nags tshal gyi shugs 'chad ba dang /'od rdul med pa dang /mig kun tu grags pa glang po'i rgyal mtshan 
7a3(phal chen): dang /spu'i klong kun nas 'od 'byung ba dang /mun pa rnam par sel cing rnam par sbyong dang /sgra dbyangs kun tu 'byung ba dang /nyi ma'i 'od 'gro ba'i mchog gi rgyal mtshan dang /phyogs bcur rmongs pa med 
7a4(phal chen): par lta ba dang /sman gyi lha dangs bzang po kun tu 'gyed pa'i mig dang //de dag la stsogs pa sman gyi lha mtha' yas pa kun kyang snying rje dri ma med pa'i dkyil 'khor gyis rab tu snang bar gyur pa sha stag 
7a5(phal chen): dang thabs gcig go/lo tog gi lha mtha' yas pa dag dang yang thabasagacig ste/'di ltar log tog gi lha dang 'jam pa'i dpal dang /dus kyi me tog rnam par mdzes pa'i 'od dang /rtsal dam pa'i stobs 
7a6(phal chen): dang mdangs kyi dang mtha' yas pa rab tu 'gyed pa dang /sa kun kyi rtsa ba 'bras bu dang /gtsug pud kha dog sna tshogs dang /yal ga dri ma med par grub par dang /spos sna tshogs shin tu grub pa dang /bzang 
7a7(phal chen): zhing yid du 'ong bar snang ba dang /lo tog gi lha kha dog rnam par dag cing 'od dri ma med pa dang /de dag la stsogs pa lo tog gi lha mtha' yas pakun kyang dga' ba dang mgu ba dang rang sa shin tu thob 
7a8(phal chen): pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/chu klung gi lha mu med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar chu klung gi lha chu bo kun dang ldan pa dang /ri dang chu kun la rten pa dang /rdul thams cad dri ma med par 
7b1(phal chen): rnam par dag pa'i mig dang /nga ros khyab par byed pa dang /srog chags kyi rgyud mtha' yas pa skyob pa dang /gdung ba med pa rgya mtsho gdung ba med pa'i 'od dri ma med pa dang /dga' ba de kun nas 
7b2(phal chen): 'byung ba dang /yon tan gyi shugs yangs pa'i rgyal mtshan dang /'jig rten thams cad kyi dpal rnam par snang ba dang /chu klung gi lha tshogs chen po rgya mtsho'i dpal gyi 'od dang /de dag la stsogs 
7b3(phal chen): bachu klung gi lha mu med pa kun kyang 'gro ba thams cad la phan pa'i don du yid la byed pa la khyed pa la brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/rgyu mtsho'i lha bsam gyis mi khyab dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar 
7b4(phal chen): rgya mtsho'i lha rin po che'i 'od 'byung ba'i dpal dang /rdo rje kun nas sra zhing mi choms pa'i rgyal mtshan dang ldan pa dang /rdul dang dri ma med pa'i gnas dang ldan pa dang /kun nas chu dang ri bo dang khyim gyi dkyil 
7b5(phal chen): 'khor dang /skyabs rin po che mig gi zla ba'i dpal dang /klu'i dbal pad mo sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dang /chu na lta bur ro mthun pa 'dzin pa'i 'od dang /rin po che'i 'od 'phro pa'i me tog gi tshon gyi 'od dang /ri 
7b6(phal chen): rab sra ba'i rdo rje sna tshogs dang /rgya mtsho'i lha 'brug sgra sgrogs pa rgya mtsho'i shugs kyi dbyangs dang //de dag la stsogs pa rgya mtsho'i lha bsam gyis mi khyad pa kun kyang sangs rgyas kyi yon tan rgya mtsho'i mthar 
7b7(phal chen): phyin par rgyas par byed pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/me'i lha grangs med pa dang yang thabs gcig ste /'di ltar me'i lha gzi brjid 'bar zhing phro ba'i 'od dang /kun tu 'bar zhing 'phro ba'i rgyal mtshan 
7b8(phal chen): gyi dkyil 'khor dang /'od rgya chen pos phyogs rgyas par byed pa dang /rgyal mtshan gyi rdul gyi sgos rgyas par byed pa dang gzi brjid mi zad pa'i gtsug phud dang /dka' thub sna tshogs kyi mig gi dpal dang /
8a1(phal chen): @#//phyogs khyim du gyur pa'i ri rab kyi spos dang /'od drag shul can gyi dbang phyug dang /mun pa dang rab rib sel cing phyogs rnam par snang bar byed pa dang /me'i lha 'od 'phro ba'i sprin gyi dbang phyug
8a2(phal chen): dang /de dag la stsogs pa me'i lha grangs med pa kun kyang sems can thams cad kyi gdung ba sel ba'i snang ba ston pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/chu'i phung po'i lha tshad med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste 
8a3(phal chen): 'di ltar chu'i phung po'i lha sprin dang na bun kun tu grub pa'i rgyal mtshan dang /sprin sgra'i shugs rgya mtsho'i sprin dang /chu sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dang /bzang por 'khyil ba grub pa'i dbal dang /kun nas 
8a4(phal chen): spos pa dri ma med pa'i myu gu'i dpal dang /dpal rlabs kyi sgra bsod nams stegs kyi 'od dang /tshim par bya ba la dbang byed pa yongs su sgrub pa dang /sa'i rdul snyed kyi sgo nas dbyangs 'byung ba dang /'od kun 
8a5(phal chen): tu 'gro zhing rgyas par byed pa dang /chu'i phung po'i lha rgya mtsho'i dbyangs kyi nga ro'i 'od dang //de dag la stsogs pa'i lha tshad med pa kun kyang /'gro ba thams cad la phan pa dang bde ba'i skyabs su brtson pa sha stag 
8a6(phal chen): dang thabs gcig go/rlung gi phung po'i lha tshad med pa de dag dang yang thabs gcigaste 'di ltar rlung gi phung po'i lha chags pa med par 'gro ba'i 'od dang /kun tu rgyas par 'byung ba'i mthu'i dpal dang /kun tu reg par 
8a7(phal chen): byed pa'i sprin gyi rgyal mtshan dang /rgyan yid du 'ong zhing bzang ba'i 'od dang chu drag po'i snying po skems pa dang /sgra chen pos 'gro ba'i dbyangs dang /shing gi rtse mo me tog rab tu 'phyang ba'i gtsug phud dang /thogs 
8a8(phal chen): pa med par phyogs kun tu rgyas par 'gro ba dang /rlung gi phung po rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung ba'i sprin gyi dkyil 'khor dang /rlung gi phung po'i lha 'jig rten thams cad kyi mun pa kun nas snang bar byed pa dang /
8b1(phal chen): de dag la stsogs pa rlung gi phung po'i lha tshad med pa kun kyang sems can thams cad kyi sngon gyi mtha'i nga rgyal rnam par gtong ba la brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/nam mkha'i lha mu med pa dag dang yang 
8b2(phal chen): thabs gcig ste/'di ltar nam mkha'i lha kun nas snang ba dri ma med pa'i 'od dang /mtha' yas shing zag pa kun tu rgyas par byed pa dang /rlung kun nas 'byung ba'i dpal dang /bsgribs pa thams cad dang mi gnas 
8b3(phal chen): pa'i dkyil 'khor dang /rgya chen po kun tu gnon pa sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dang /'od gzer snang ba'i 'od thogs pa med pa dang /stobs kyi rgyun thogs pa med pa'i dpal dang /phyogs dri ma med par snang ba'i 
8b4(phal chen): 'od dang /dbyangs shin tu 'phags shing zab pa dang /nam mkha'i lha phyogs bcur mngon tu ston pa'i 'od dang /de dag la stsogs pa nam mkha'i lha mu med pa thams cad kyang sems rgya mtsho dri ma med cing yongs 
8b5(phal chen): su dag pa sha stag dang thabs gci go/phyogs kyi lha mu med pa dag dang /yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar phyogs kyi lha phyogs kyi dbyibs na rab tu gnas shing kun tu rgyas par 'gengs pa dang /mnyam 
8b6(phal chen): pa nyid la mngon tu lta ba dang /snang ba spyod pa'i rgyan dang /kun tu rgyu zhing rjes su 'brang ba dang /rmongs pa med pa'i gtsug yongs su 'grub pa dang /nam mkha'i rgyud kun tu spyod pa dang /rnam par spyod 
8b7(phal chen): pa'i sprin rgya chen po'i rgyal mtshan dang /mig tha dad pa med pa'i gtsug phud dang /'jig rten gyi bya ba kun nas spyod pa dang /phyogs kyi lha phyogs su rnam par lta ba phreng ba'i 'od dang /de dag lastsogs pa

9a1(phal chen): @#//lha dpal dri ma med cing kun nas zab pa'i 'od dang /kun nas skyob pa'i mdangs kyi dpal dang /rab tu dga' ba'i mig gis 'gro ba la rnam par lta ba dang /sgras zhing rgya mtsho dang ldan pa dang /
9a2(phal chen): mtshan mo thams cad 'byung ba'i dpal dang /shing thams cad rab tu rgyas par byed pa'i 'od dang /kun nasaskyong zhing srung ba'i dpal dang /dge ba thams cad kyis dga' ba la dbang sgyur ba dang /mdud par 
9a3(phal chen): 'dzin pa thams cad zhi bar byas dbang po rab tu dga' ba dang /mtshan mo'i lha dge ba'i bsod nams 'byung ba'i dpal ston pa dang /de dag la stsogs pa mtshan mo'i lha tshad med pa kun kyang chos kyi dga' ba 
9a4(phal chen): 'byung ba la mos par gnas pa la brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/nyin mo'i lha gzhal du med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar nyin mo'i lha khyim dang /gzhal med khang gi gzugs shin 
9a5(phal chen): tu ston pa dang /brtsam pa'i blo gros mtshams sbyor ba'i dkyil 'khor dang /kun nas dga' ba'i dpal gyi rgyan dang /me tog dang spos yid du 'ong ba'i 'od dang /kun nas yid du 'ong ba'i sman ston pa sgrub 
9a6(phal chen): pa'i dpal dang /dga' ba'i mig bzang pos tshim par byed pa dang /yul rgya chen po'i phyogs kun nas byung ba'i dpal dang snying rje rgya chen po'i gzi brjid kyi 'od dang /snang ba'i rgya chen po'i dge ba'i rtsa dang 
9a7(phal chen): mi 'bral ba'i 'od dang nyin mo'i lha me tog gi phreng ba kun nas 'phyang ba'i dpal dang /de dag la stsogs pa nyin mo'i lha gzhal du med ba kun kyang mos pa'i chos gzhal du med pa'i rgyan la rab tu brtson pa sha stag 
9a8(phal chen): dang thabs gcig go/lha ma yin gyi dbang po mtha' yas pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lha ma yin gyi dbang po sgra gcan dang /lha ma yin gyi dbang bo thag zangs ris dang /lha ma yin gyi dpang po 
9b1(phal chen): bde ba'i mchog dang /lha ma yin gyi dbang po mtshon cha chen po dang /lha ma yin gyi dbangs po stobs can dang /lha ma yin gyi dbangang po rnam par snang ba dang /lha ma yin gyi dbad po rdo rje sra ba sna tshogs kyi yan lag dang 
9b2(phal chen): lha ma yin gyi dbang po stobs rgya chen po'i rgyu'i blo gros dang /lha ma yin gyi dab dpo dpal gyi be'u rab tu 'byung ba dang /lha ma yin gyis dbang po brtul zhugs bzang po'i dbyangs dang /de dag lastsogs pa lha ma 
9b3(phal chen): yin gyi dbang po kun kyang sgyu dang /nga rgyal dang kun nas nyon mongs pa rab tu zhi bar bya ba la brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/nam mkha' lding gi dbang po bsam gyis mi khyab pa dag dang yang thabs gcig 
9b4(phal chen): ste/'di ltar nam mkha' lding gi dbang bo mthu stobs chen po'i shugs dang /nam mkha' lding gi dbad po mi shigs pa'i sems can rin chen gtsug phud dang /nam mkha' lding ga dbang po shugs dri ma med 
9b5(phal chen): pa'i dpal dang /nam mkha' lding dbang po phyir mi ldog pa'i sems kyi rgyan dang /nam mkha' lding gyi dbang po rgya mtsho chen po'i 'od kyi tshul 'chang ba dang /nam mkha' lding gis dbang po chos brtan pa dri ma 
9b6(phal chen): med pa'i 'od ba'i 'od dang /nam mkha' lding go dbang po cod pan sna tshogs dpal gyi gtsug phud dang /nam ma kha'i lding gi dbang po bzhin kun tu khyab par ston pa'i shugs dang /nam mkha'i lding gi dbang po dge 
9b7(phal chen): ba rgya mtsho kun nas lta ba dang /nam mkha' ldid gi dbang po klu'i sgra yangs pa kun tu lta ba'i dkyil 'khor dang /de dag lastsogs pa nam mkha' lding gi dbangapo mtha' yas pa kun kyang sems can thams cad

10b1(phal chen): srung po dang/dri za'i rgyal po shing mi 'am ci'-i 'od dang/dri za'i rgyal po mig gtsang ma dga' rab tu skye ba dang/dri za'i rgyal po me tog dang/shing shin tu rgyas ba'i cod pan dang/dri za'i rgyal po dga' bas
10b2(phal chen): bas spyod pa kun tu sgrog pa dang/dri za'i rgyal po rab tu dga' ba rgyun tu gnas ba'i mig bzang bo dang/dri za'i rgyal po yid du 'ong ba'i dbyangs seng ge'i rgyal mtshan dang/dri za'i rgyal po 'od gzer

12b2(phal chen): par thar pa rab du zhi bar sems can thams cad gdul ba rab tu brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/lha'i rgyal po dbang po sgyur lastsogs pa bgrang du med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig 
12b3(phal chen): ste/'di ltar lha'i rgyal po dbang sgyur dang/lha'i rgyal po mig bzang po'i blo gros dang/cod pan sna tshogs kyi rgyal mtshan dang /btson pa'i blo gros dang /go 'phang rnam par gnon pa'i sgra dbyangs
12b4(phal chen): sna tshogs dang/'od yid du 'ong ba'i rgyal mtshan dang /spyod yul zhi ba'i sgo dang/'khor lo'i ri mo bzang po'i rgyal mtshan dang /me tog blo gros seng ge'i dbang phyug dang/lha'i rgyal po dbang po'i stobs
12b5(phal chen): ri mo bzang po'i 'od dang de dag lastsogs pa dbang sgyur gyi lha'i rgyal po bgrang du med pa kun kyang /chos kyi dbang rgya chen po la rab tu brtson pa sha stag rang thabs gcig go/bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i 
12b6(phal chen): tshangs pa chen po dag dang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar tshangs pa chen po rab tu snang ba dang /blo gros kyi 'od dang/blo gros bzang po'i sems snang ba dang/kun tu dbyangs kyi sprin dang/'jig rten dbang 
12b7(phal chen): po'i sgra dang/mig rab tu zhi zhing 'od shin tu zab pa dang/phyogs su snang ba 'gyed pa dang/sbrul pa'i sgra dbyangs dang/mig gi 'od gzer mngon par snang ba dang/tshangs pa'i chen po dbyangs rgya mtsho
12b8(phal chen): yid du 'ong ba dang/de dag lastsogs pa tshangs pa chen po bsam gyis mi khyab pa kun kyang /sems can thams cad kyi sems snang bar bya ba dang /sim par bya ba la byams pa chen po la gnas pa sha
13a1(phal chen): @#//stag dang thabs gcig go/lha'i bu gya nom snang ba'i ris su gtogs pa tshad med pa dag dang /yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lha'i bu snang bas dga' ba dang /dge ba sna tshogs kyi 'od 
13a2(phal chen): dang /dbang po'i dbyangs dang /dran pa'i blo 'di med pa dbang ga' ba sna tshogs sam par dag pa'i dbyangs dang /legs par bsam pa'i dbyangs dang /'od 'gyed pa'i dbyangs rnam par snang ba dang /dbyangs 
13a3(phal chen): zab mo'i 'od dang /grags pa dri ma med pa'i 'od dang /lha'i bu 'od dri ma med pas shin tu 'phags pa dang /de dag la stsogs pa gya nom snang ba'i ris su btogs pa'i lha'i bu tshad med pa kun kyang zhi ba 
13a4(phal chen): dga' ba rgya chen po'i rnam par thar pa la gnas pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go//dge rgyas kyi lha'i bu grangs med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di la ltar lha'i bu dge bar grags pa'i blo gros dang 
13a5(phal chen): mchog tu snang ba dang /rab tu zhi ba'i dpal dang /dbyangs kyi ri bo dang /dran pa dge ba'i mig dang /dga' ba dam pa rnam par snang ba dang /'jig rten dbyangs kyi blo gros dang /gzi brjid 'bar ba'i bdag po dang 
13a6(phal chen): chos kyi dga' bas 'phrul pa'i sems dang /'phrul bas shin tu 'phags pa dang de dag la stsogs pa dge rgyas kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha'i bu grangs med pa kun kyang sgo yongs pa la gnas pas 'gro bar 
13a7(phal chen): 'byung zhing rnam par 'dul ba la rab tu brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gci ga go//lha'i bu 'bras bu chen po'i ris su gtogs pa tshad med pa dag dang yang thams gcig ste/'di lta bu lha'i bu chos kyi dpal gzi brjid 
13a8(phal chen): dga' ba dang /dri med sna tshogs rgya mtsho dang /shes rab mchog gi 'od dang /ye shes dbang po'i dpal dang /dga' bzhi pa dang /sgo kun nas shes pa'i mig dang /dga' ba'i gtsug gi blo gros dang /yon tan blo gros 
13b1(phal chen): bzang po'i 'od dang 'od zhi zhing dri ma med pa dang/lha'i bu snang ba chen po dri ma med pa dang/de dag lastsogs ba 'bras bu chen po'i ris su gtogs pa'i lha'i bu tshad med pa kun kyang zhi ba'i chos kyi gzhal 
13b2(phal chen): med khang la kun nas rab tu gnas pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/lha'i bu dbang phyug chen po'i ris su gtogs ba'i grangs med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lha'i bu shin tu 'od 'phro rgya 
13b3(phal chen): mtsho dang/lha'i bu dbang phyug grags pa rab tu snang ba dang /lha'i bu dge ba'i mig dri ma med pa dang/lha bu shes rab chen pos dga' zhing spro ba dang/lha'i bu 'od mi sgul ba'i dbang phyug dang/lha'i bu mig 
13b4(phal chen): shin tu mnyam par gzhag pa dang/lha'i bu snang pa shin tu sems pa dang/lha'i bu ye shes chen pos dga' ba dang/lha'i bu sna tshogs kun tu dbyangs kyi rgyal mtshan dang/lha'i bu grags pa rnam par snang ba'i brtul
13b5(phal chen): zhugs bzang po dang/de dag lastsogs pa dbang phyug chen po'i ri sa su gtogs pa'i lha'i bu grangs med pa kun kyang spyod yul mtshan ma med pa'i mnyam pa nyid la rab tu brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig ste/
13b6(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho de ni sems can gyi ris thams cad yang dag par gzhol ba'o//sems can yon tan mtha' yas pa 'dus pa las yongs su byung ba'o//phyogs kyi 
13b7(phal chen): dbyings kun na shin tu gnas pa'o//la bor gyi dkyil 'khor sna tshogs kyi dbyings su rnam par gnas pa'o//phan tshun 'dres pa med cing ji lta 'ongs pa'i phyogs kyi dbyings la rab tu gnas pa'o//de bzhin
13b8(phal chen): gshegs pa lta ba la mngon par phyogs pa'o//nyon mongs pa'i pag chags dang/sems kyi dri ma thams cad dang/bral ba'o//bsgrib pa thams cad kyi ri rnam par 'thor ba'o//sangs rgyas lta ba la bsgrib 
14a1(phal chen): pa med pas snang bar gyur ba ste/de ni 'di ltar bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyis sngon bskal pa rgya mtsho nas/byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe/bsdu 
14a2(phal chen): ba'i dngos po bzhis yongs su bsdus te/de bzhin gshegs pa de dang de dag gi drung du bzhud pa'i tshe/dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su bzung zhing thabs kyi sgo de dag gis yongs su smin par byas te rnam 
14a3(phal chen): pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi lam la bkod cing /dge ba'i rtsa ba chad med pa rab tu bskyed pas bsod nams chen po'i stobs dang shugs rab tu thob pa dag go/smon lam gyi tshul rgya mtsho gzhan dang 
14a4(phal chen): gzhan la rab tu zhugs shing spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor gzhan dang gzhan yongs su dag pas/yang dag par byung ba 'byung ba'i lam gzhan dang gzhan gyis shin tu 'byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa 
14a5(phal chen): gzhan dang gzhan mthong bas snang bar gyur ba dag ste/de dag ni mos pa'i stobs gzhan dang gzhan gyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan rgya mtsho la 'jug go/sangs rgyas blta ba'i rnam par thar 
14a6(phal chen): ba'i sgo gzhan dag gzhan gyis rnam par 'phrul lo//de ni 'di lta ste lha'i bu dbang phyug chen po'i ris su gtogs pa shin tu 'od 'phro rgya mtsho'i rnam par thar pa ni chos kyi dbyings dang /nam mkha' ltar zhi 
14a7(phal chen): ba'i tshul gyi mthu zhes bya'o//lha'i bu dbang phyug grags pa rab tu snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni chosathams cad la dbang ba'i stobs la yang dag par gzhol zhing kun nas rjes su 'gro bas rnam par spyod pa zhes 
14a8(phal chen): bya'o//lha'i bu dge ba'i mig dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/chos thams cad 'byung ba med pa dang /'gro ba med pa dang /brjod du med cing sbyar ba med pa zhes bya'o//lha'i bu shes rab chen pos dga' zhing 
14b1(phal chen): spro ba'i rnam par thar pa ni chos kyi tshul thams cad la mngon sum du gyur ba'i shes rab rgya mtshos rnam par dpyod pa la rab tu snang ba zhes bya'o//lha'i bu 'od mi sgul ba'i dbang phyug gi rnam par thar pa ni/
14b2(phal chen): sems can la bsam gtan gyi dga' ba dang bde ba mtha' yas pa la mos pa bstan pa rgya chen po'i tshul yang dag par 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lha'i bu mig shin tu mnyam par gzhag pa'i rnam par thar pa ni mi shes 
14b3(phal chen): pa'i mun nag dang /'jigs pa rnam par zlog ba'i spyod pa rab tu zhi ba zhes bya'o//lha'i bu snang ba shin tu sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa ni bsam ba'i yul mtha' yas ba'i las srid pa thams cad pa la 'jug 
14b4(phal chen): pa zhes bya'o//lha'i bu ye shes chen pos dga' ba'i rnam par thar pa ni tshos thams cad kyi phyogs su 'gro zhing yongs su gnan pa dang /brten pa'i phyogs kyi mthu zhes bya'o//lha'i bu sna tshogs kun tu dbyangs 
14b5(phal chen): kyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni sangs rgyas kyi yul rab tu zhi ba la 'jug pa/kun nas khong du chud ba'i gtsug rab tu snang ba zhes bya'o//lha'i bu grags pa rnam par snang ba'i btul zhugs bzab 
14b6(phal chen): po'i rnam par thar pa ni rang gi gzhal med khang dang /blo'i lus nas dmigs pa tshad med ba'i sangs rgyas kyi yul rgya chen po 'byung ba zhes bya'o//de nas dbang phyug chen po'i ris su gtogs pa'i lha'i shin 
14b7(phal chen): tu 'od 'phro rgya mtshos sangs rgyas kyi mthus lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor chen po yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//mi zad chos kyi dbyings ni mtha' 
14b8(phal chen): yas ste/kun kyang sangs rgyas sku la rab tu gsal//de ni gzugs med dngos med rab tu zhi//'gro ba'i skyabs su 'byung ba skyes shing byung //chos kyi sgron ma sangs rgyas shin tu ston//thos la mnga' 
15a1(phal chen): @#//rab mdzad pa 'jig rten byung //de'i spyod yul mtha' yas mi zad de//dbang phyug grags pa 'di 'dra'i chos la 'jug/bsam gyis mi khyab sangs rgyas snang ba med//de'i mtshan nyid phyogs na 
15a2(phal chen): med pa ste//rnam dag lam ni skye bo dag la ston//shin tu dri med mig gis 'di 'dra mthong //'gro ba kun gyis bsam du mi nus ste//rgyal ba'i shes rab mtha' yas gang mnga' ba gti mug bsgribs pa'i sems 
15a3(phal chen): can rnam par spyod/de la shes rab chen po rnam par gnas//skye dgu kun gyi sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//mthong ba tsam gyis nyon mongs zhi bar 'gyur//'gro ba'i dga' ba rgya chen rab tu skyed//mi sgul 
15a4(phal chen): dbang phyug lha yis de mthong ngo //sems can rgya mtsho gti mug mun bsgribs la//sangs rgyas chos nyid rab tu zhi ba ston//shes rab sgron ma 'gro ba 'di brjod de//dge ba'i mig ni 'di'i tshul la 'jug/
15a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kun kyang rnam dag sku ston cing //thams cad phyogs su shin tu 'phags pa'i sku//de yang dngos med 'jig rten mi gnas te//kun snang sems dpa' 'di ni de ltar lta//mtha' yas chags med rgyal 
15a6(phal chen): ba rnams kyi gsung //snod du gyur ba'i sems can de dag nyan//sangs rgyas rnams la 'pho bar mi snang snang ba ste//'di ni shes pas dga' ba'i rnam thar to//thams cad phyogs sgo kun nas sangs rgyas 
15a7(phal chen): te//mi'i dbang po rnam thar zhi la bzhugs//sangs rgyas 'jig rten snang zhing rnam par mdzes//'di ni sna tshogs rgyal mtshan rnam thar to//rgyal ba bskal pa rgya mtsho mtha' yas su//'gro ba kun don rnam 
15a8(phal chen): par byang chub cing //sna tshogs rnam par 'phrul bas sems can 'dul//rnam par snang bas rnam thar 'di mthong ngo //de la lha'i//rgyal po chos kyi dpal gzi brjid dga' ba'i rnam par thar pa ni//sems 
15b1(phal chen): can thams cad kyi dbang po dang the tsom rnam par lta ba'i chos kyi sprin la rab tu gzhol ba zhes bya'o//lha'i rgyal po dri med sna tshogs rgya mtsho'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i rnam pa thams 
15b2(phal chen): cad yid la byed pa ston pa sbyor ba zhes bya'o//lha'i rgyal bo shes rab mchog gi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni chos kyi rang bzhin mnyam pa nyid la gnas pa lus kyi rgyan ces bya'o//lha'i rgyal po ye shes dbang 
15b3(phal chen): po'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni 'jig rten gyi chos thams cad rnam par sgom pa'i sems skad gcig tu bsam gyis mi khyab rgyan rgya mtsho la rab tu gnas pa zhes bya'o//lha'i dbang po dga' ba zhi 
15b4(phal chen): ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/skra'i sbubs gcig tu sangs rgyas kyi zhing bsam gyis mi khyab pa yod dgu 'dzud pa zhes bya'o//lha'i rgyal po sgo kun nas shes pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni chos 
15b5(phal chen): kyi dbyings la sgo kun nas rnam par lta bas 'jug pa zhes bya'o//lha'i rgyal pa dga' ba'i gtsug gi blo gros kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi skye ba dang byed pa bskal pa mtha' 
15b6(phal chen): yas pa mngon par 'byin pa zhes bya'o//lha'i rgyal ba yon tan blo gros bzang po'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni dmigs pa thams cad 'jig rten gyi dgos pa thams cad chos gzhal du med cing bsam 
15b7(phal chen): gyis mi khyab pas rnam par dpyod pa zhes bya'o//lha'i bu 'od zhi zhing dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni sems can dang chos dang 'byung ba thams cad kyi sgo la mngon sum du yang dag par spyod pa 'byung ba zhes

18a2(phal chen): pa ni/'phags pa'i chos thams cad la dga' ba/mtshungs pa med pa'i shugs rnam par spel bas rab tu tshim par byed pa zhes bya'o//lha'i bu legs par bsam pa'i dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/tshul
