40b1(phal chen): mthong bas 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal 'grol//kun tu mngon par lta ba'i spyod yul lo//sems can rgya mtsho mun pas bsgribs pa la//sangs rgyas chos kyi sgron ma snang ba ston//rnam par snang ba'i sangs rgyas 'od kyang 
40b2(phal chen): mthong //snang ba spyod pa rnam thar 'di la dga'//chos kyi 'khor lo thams cad kun tu skor//sems can nyon mongs zhi mdzad thogs pa med//dbyangs kyi yan lag thams cad yongs su rgyas//kun nas 'khyil pa'i dpal 
40b3(phal chen): gyis rab tu thos//'gro ba'i ming gi 'khor lo ji snyed pa//sangs rgyas mtshan gyis de dag thams cad 'thon//sems can rnams kyi gti mug rnam par zlog/'di ni gti mug rnam dag spyod yul lo//sangs 
40b4(phal chen): rgyas mngon sum sems can gang 'gro ba//de dag thams cad sangs rgyas gsung snyan ni//dga' ba rgya chen shin tu yangs pa 'thob//'di ni nam kha rgyud kyi rnam thar to//skad gcig sems rgyud kun la tshad med pa'i//
40b5(phal chen): chos kyi sprin char rgyal ba 'bebs pa mdzad//'gro ba'i nyon mongs thams cad rab tu 'jil//'di la sprin gyi rgyal mtshan lha 'jug go//sems can kun gyi rgya mtsho'i las rnams kun//sangs rgyas rnam pa 
40b6(phal chen): gzhan min yang dag ston//sems can nyon mongs las la rab tu dgrol//'jig rten bya ba spyod bdag spyod yul lo//sangs rgyas sngon chad spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe//sems can don tu thugs rjes rab bskyed de//
40b7(phal chen): pha rol phyin pa thams cad rgyas par bkang //sna tshogs phreng ba'i 'od ni 'di la dga'//phyogs rnams sems can rgyal bas snang mdzad pas//bsam pa rgya mtsho rnam pa sna tshogs kyis//de dag mtha' 
40b8(phal chen): yas phyogs kyi chos la 'jug/mig bzangs gtsug phud tshul 'dir rab tu 'jug/de la nam mkha'i lha kun nas snang ba dri ma med pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa'i bsam 
41a1(phal chen): @#//pa la rnam par lta pa zhes bya'o//nam mkha'i lha mtha' yas shing rang ba kun tu rgyas par byed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/chos kyi dbyings rgya chen po dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes zab mo la yang dag par 
41a2(phal chen): 'jug pa zhes bya'o//nam mkha' lha rlung kun nas 'byung ba'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/yul mtha' yas par rnam par bsgom pa'i mtshan nyid kyi lus zhes bya'o//nam mkha'i lta bsgribs pa thams cad 
41a3(phal chen): dang mi gnas pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni /sems can thams cad kyi las dang nyon mongs pa'i sgrib pa yongs su sbyangs pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//phyogs kyi lha rgya chen po kun tu gnon pa sna tshogs 
41a4(phal chen): kyi gtsug pud kyi rnam par thar pa ni /'gro ba rgya mtsho rgya chen po la rnam par lta zhing rnam par spyod pa zhes bya'o//nam mkha'i+i lta 'od gzer snang ba'i 'od thogs pa med pa'i rnam par thar pa na//'gro ba log par 
41a5(phal chen): ltung ba thams cad snying rje'i 'od kyi skyob pa zhes bya'o//nam mkha'i lha stobs kyi rgyun thogs pa med pa'i dpal gyi rnam par thar ba ni//bsod nams thams cad kyi stobs dang /dga' ba bsdu ba la yang dag par 
41a6(phal chen): gzhol ba zhes bya'o//nam mkha'i la phyogs dri ma med par snang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni //sems can thams cad dri ma med pa'i sems kyis sgrib pa rnam par zlog par gnas pa'i dbang po zhes bya'o//nam mkha'i
41a7(phal chen): lha dbyangs shin tu 'phags shing zab pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor gyi 'od rab tu 'phags pas phyogs kun tu rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//nam mkha'i lha phyogs bcur mngon tu ston pa'i 'od kyi rnam par
41a8(phal chen): thar pa ni/sgo thams cad nas phyogs thams cad du 'pho ba med ma'i dkyil 'khor ces bya'o//de nam mkha'i lha kun nas snang ba dri ma med pa'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus nam mkha'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor 
41b1(phal chen): thams cad la rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba'i spyan ni rgya chen yangs pa yis//sems can rgya mtsho ma lus kun gzigs te//'gro ba kun la mngon par rab tu bzhugs 
41b2(phal chen): sangs rgyas dri med nam mkha' rab tu 'dra//sangs rgyas sku'i rgya chen 'od kyi rnams//phyogs bcu'i 'gro kun khyab cing rnam par mdzes//thams cad phyogs su mngon par rab tu gnas//kun tu 
41b3(phal chen): rgyas pa'i 'od kyis 'di mthong ngo //rgyal ba nam mkha'i mtshan nyid 'byung ba med//'di la gzung ba snang ba gang yang med//thob pa med cing rang bzhin med pa ste//rlung gi 'od kyis rnam thar 'di mthong ngo //
41b4(phal chen): bskal pa rgya mtsho brgya phrag mang por yang /sangs rgyas sems can bsgrib pa sel bar mdzad//lam yangs rnam par thar pa rab tu ston//'di ni dpal po dkyil 'khor 'od kyi'o//sngon cad sangs rgyas byang 
41b5(phal chen): chub spyod pa'i rnams//'gro ba kun la phan dang bde don tu//ji ltar byung ba de dag bdag gis mthong //'di ni sna tshogs gtsug phud spyod yul lo//'khor ba rgya mtsho kun tu rgyu ba yi//sems can log par 
41b6(phal chen): ltung zhing 'khor ba la/sangs rgyas 'od ni sdug bsngal zhi ba'i rgyu//snang ba'i 'od kyis rnam thar 'di ma thong ngo //bsod nams dge ba thams cad snying po ste//chags med stobs dang ye shes rgya chen mkhyen/
41b7(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad bsod nams zhing gyur pa//sangs rgyas de ltar chags med stobs sems so//mun pa stug po spyod yul rnam rgyu pa'i//sems can bsgribs pas rab tu slob pa la//de bzhin gshegs 
41b8(phal chen): pa snang ba rab 'gyed de/dri med phyogs snang lhas ni de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas ye shes shin tu 'phags pa'i//mtha' dang dbus yang dmigs pas mi rung ste//zhing kun 'gro la mngon par 'od kyang 
42a1(phal chen): gtan gyi sgo rab tu zhi ba'i shugs kyis gti mug rab tu zhi bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha 'jig rten thams cad kyi mun pa kun nas snang bar byed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi 
42a2(phal chen): spyod pa'i rjes snyogs pas/thogs pa med pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//de nas rlung gi lha chags pa med par 'gro ba'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus//rlung gi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams 
42a3(phal chen): cad la rnam par bltas ste/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba kun gyi chos nyid zab mo 'dzin pa ste//chags med thams cad tshul gyis de la 'jug/'gro ba kun la sangs rgyas 
42a4(phal chen): mngon tu bzhugs/de ni mtshan med gzugs brnyan yod pa smin//sangs rgyas thams cad mthu la rnam par ltos//byang chub spyad pa mtha' yas spyod pa'i tshe//skad gcig cig la sangs rgyas mchod cing 
42a5(phal chen): bstod/de la rgyas byed 'od kyi lha 'jug go//sangs rgyas bsam gyis mi khyab 'gro ba'i mgon//tshul rnams kun kyis sbyor ba chung zad med//sdug bsngal kun las sems can ma lus skyob//
42a6(phal chen): rgyal mtshan reg pas rnam thar 'di mthong ngo //'gebs dang sgrib pa shin tu stug po yi//mi des bsgribs shing bsod nams med pa'i//sems can sdug bsngal kun las dkrol bar 'gyur//
42a7(phal chen): sman kyis brgyan pa'i lhas ni 'di mthong ngo //rgyal ba'i rdzu 'phrul rnam par 'phrul la ltos//rnam pa kun gyis bdud rnams shin tu 'joms//tshul gyis 'dul ba de la rab tu sgrog/gzi brjid drag po'i 
42a8(phal chen): mthus ni 'di mthong ngo //rgyal ba'i pa spu'i sgo rnams thams cad nas//'gro ba kun 'gengs sgra'i dbyangs 'byung ba/de dag sdug bsngal 'jigs pa thams cad med//'di ni sgra bsgrog lha'i rab tu shes//
42b1(phal chen): @#//snang /zab mo'i 'od kyis de ltar sangs rgyas mthong //phyogs rnams rgya mtsho kun na sngon spyod pa'i//sems can thams cad yongs su dgrol ba'i phyir//sangs rgyas smon lam rgya che 
42b2(phal chen): shin tu yangs/mngon par ltabs rnam thar 'di mthong ngo //de la rlung gi phung po'i lha chags pa med par 'gro ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa//'jig rten gyi bya ba thams cad sangs rgyas kyi chos su yang dag par 
42b3(phal chen): 'jug pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha kun tu rgyas par 'byung ba'i mthu'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas mchod pa'i yul mtha' yas pa rgya chen po mngon par sgrub pa ston pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi lta kun 
42b4(phal chen): tu reg par byed pa'i sprin gyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni /sems can thams cad kyi nad rab tu zhi bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi la rgyan yid du 'ong zhing bzang po'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni//'gro 
42b5(phal chen): ba thams cad kyi bsgribs pa'i ri 'joms pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha chu drag po'i snying po skems pa'i rnam par thar pa ni//bdud kyi 'khor lo sgo kun nas 'joms pa zhes 
42b6(phal chen): bya'o//rlung gi lha sgra chen pos 'gro ba'i dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni|/sems can mtha' yas pa'i 'jigs pa rnam par zlog cing zhi bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha shing gi rtse mo'i me tog rab tu 
42b7(phal chen): 'phyang ba'i gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni//chos thams cad kyi tshul tha dad pa yang dag par shes rgya mtsho la 'jug pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha thogs pa med par phyogs kun tu rgyas par 'gro ba'i rnam 
42b8(phal chen): par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad mnyam par dgod pa'i tshul gyis 'dul ba'i snying po zhes bya'o//rlung gi lta rlung gi phung po rnam pa sna tshogs 'byad pa'i sprin gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni bsam 
43a1(phal chen): @#//'gro ba kun mnyam rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams su//tha dad yang dag mkhyen pas bde bar gshegs//mtha' yas bskal par rang byung yang dag ston//shing gi gtsug phud 'byung ba de la 'jug 
43a2(phal chen): rgyal ba'i ye shes thabs rnams kun sgo nas//kun tu chags shing thogs pa med pa ste//spyod yul gzhan yas kun tu mtha' yas med//'di ni chags med 'gro ba'i rnam thar to//rgyal ba'i spyod yul 
43a3(phal chen): mtha' yas dag la lta//rab zhi sku mnga' mtshan med sangs rgyas te//thabs sgo kun nas 'od kyang rab tu snang //phung po sna tshogs bdag pa'i rnam thar to//mi mngon bskal ba mtha' yas spyod pa'i tshe//
43a4(phal chen): de 'dra'i chos kyis 'gro kun tshim mdzad pas//thams cad stobs la sangs rgyas rab tu bzhugs//'jig rten stobs 'dzin lha'i sa 'di mthong ngo //de la me'i lha gzi brjid 'bar zhing 'phro ba'i 'od kyi rnam 
43a5(phal chen): par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i dpa' rlabs dang rab tu gdung ba rnam par zlog pa zhes bya'o//me'i lha kun tu 'bar zhing 'phro ba'i rgyal mtshan gyi dkyil 'khor kyi rnam par 
43a6(phal chen): thar pa ni/sems can tham sa cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i pa'i dpa' rlabs dang rabs tu gdung ba rnam par zlos pa zhes bya'o//me'i lha 'od rgya chen pos phyogs rgyas par byed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/bsod nams kyi stobs 
43a7(phal chen): mi sgul ba dang /snying rje ba dang //byams pa'i snying po zhes bya'o//me'i lha rgyal mtshan gyi rdul gyi sgos rgyas par byed pa'i rnams thar pa ni/nyon mongs pa'i rdul gyis g.yog pa rnam par sel ba zhes bya'o//
43a8(phal chen): me'i lha gzi brjid mi zad pa'i gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni nam mkha' mtha' yas pa snang bas gsal bar byed pa'i 'od ces bya'o//me'i lha dka' thub sna tshogs kyi mig dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/
43b1(phal chen): dge ba dang /bsod nams sna tshogs rnam par snang ba'i 'od rab tu zhi ba zhes bya'o//mi'i lha phyogs khyim du gyur pa'i ri rab kyi spo'i rnam par thar pa ni/'jig rten gyi rgyud thams cad me ltar 'bar ba'i sems can sdug 
43b2(phal chen): bsngal rab tu zhi bar byed pa zhes bya'o//me'i lha 'od drag shul gyi dbang phyug gi rnam par thar pa/'jig rten thams cad la dbang sgyur ba'i brtsom pas rab tu gnon pa zhes bya'o//me'i lha mun pa dang rib rab sel 
43b3(phal chen): cing phyogs rnam par snang ba'i rnam thar pa ni/chags par lta ba'i gti mug thams cad rnam par spong ba'i 'od snang ba zhes bya'o//me'i lha 'od 'phro ba'i sprin gyi dbang phyug gi rnam pa thar pa ni/chags pa thams 
43b4(phal chen): cad la mi gnas pa'i stobs 'byung ba yang dag par bsgrub pa'i nga ro zhes bya'o//de nas me'i lha gzi brjid 'bar zhing 'phro ba'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus//me'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams 
43b5(phal chen): cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba'i brtson 'grus stobs la rnam par ltos//bskal ba bye ba rgya chen bsam yas par//'gro ba'i don du phan pa rab 
43b6(phal chen): brtsams te/sems can kun kyi mun pa rnam par bsal//nyon mongs dpa' rlabs ldan pa'i 'gro ba rnams//lta la gnas shing gti mug bsgribs pa'i//nyon mongs me ni mgon pos zhi bar mdzad//
43b7(phal chen): de la rgyal mtshan dkyil 'khor rab tu dga'//rgyal ba'i spyod yul rgya chen bdag gis mthong //phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal ba rgya mtshor yang //rgyal ba'i byin kyis rlabs rnams rab tu 'jug/mngon nas snang 
43b8(phal chen): ba'i 'od ni rab tu dga'/rgyal ba'i bsod nams mi zad nam mkha' bzhin//de'i mtha' ni kun tu mi rnyed de//rgyal ba'i thugs rjes bskyod du yod mi nus//'di la dpal gyi sprin sgra rab tu 'jug/bskal ba 
44a1(phal chen): @#//bye bar spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe//chos tshul gang rnams bstan pa de dag gi/mtha' ni nam yang rnyed par sla ba min//rnam thar 'di ni gzi brjid gtsug pud kyi'o//phyogs bcu'i 'khor rnams 
44a2(phal chen): shin tu yangs pa dag/sangs rgyas mgon po thams cad mngon tu lte//'gro ba kun la thub pa rnam par mdzes//'di la bka' thub 'od 'phro dpal 'jug go/'gro ba kun na thub pa rab tu 'phags//khang khyim 
44a3(phal chen): thams cad dag na bzhugs par snang //rnam par yangs pa'i chos kyi char yang 'bebs//'di ni phyogs snang byed pa'i spyod yul lo//rgyal ba nyid kyi chos rnams zab pa ste//thams cad chos kyi 
44a4(phal chen): 'gro ba rab byung zhing /ngo bo nyid kyi chos nyid ston pa mdzad//de la drag shul 'od ni rab tu dga'//lta ba'i mun nag gti mug bsgribs pa'i//'jig rten bdag med 'khrugs pa thams cad la//rgyal bas chos 
44a5(phal chen): ni rab tu bstan mdzad de//de la rnam par snang ba'i lha dag la//rgyal ba'i smon lam sngan cad byung ba'i rnams//ci 'dra bsam gyis khyab par mi rung ste//stobs dang pha rol phyin pas rnam par sbyangs//'od 
44a6(phal chen): 'phro sprin ni 'di la 'di ltar 'jug/de la chu'i lha sprin dang na bun kun tu grub pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad la byams pa'i tshul la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes/
44a7(phal chen): bya'o//chu'i lha sprin sgra'i shugs rgya mtsho'i sprin gyi rnam par thar pa ni//chos kyi sprin mtha' yas pa 'byung ba'i rgyan ces bya'o//chu'i lha chu sna tshogs kyi gtsug pud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba snod du 
44a8(phal chen): gyur pa thams cad la rnam par lta zhing /sdud pa'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//chu'i lha bzang por 'khyil ba grub pa'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi yul zab mo'i 'od kyi char ba'i byin gyi rlabs 
44b1(phal chen): shes bya'o/chu'i lha kun nas sbos dri ma med pa'i myu gu'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni//sangs rgyas kyi 'od dri ma med pa rnam par lta ba la yang dag par 'dug pa'i byin gyi brlabs shes bya'o//chu'i lha 
44b2(phal chen): dpa' rlabs kyi sgra bsod nams stegs kyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/chos kyi dbyings mtshan nyid med cing dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//chu'i lha tshim par bya ba la dbang byed pa yongs su sgrub pa'i rnam par 
44b3(phal chen): thar pa ni/snying rje rgya mtsho mi zad pa'i sgor 'jug pa zhes bya'o//chu'i lha sa'i rdul snyed kyi sgo nas dbyangs 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du 
44b4(phal chen): dga' ba'i shugs 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya'o//chu'i lha 'od kun tu 'gro zhing rgyas par byed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/bsod nams chags pa med pa shin tu rgyas par kun tu snang bar byed pa'i dkyil 
44b5(phal chen): 'khor ces bya'o//chu'i lha rgya mtsho'i dbyings kyi nga ro'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa na/'gro ba thams cad 'dul ba'i sbyor ba nam mkha' lta bus 'dul bar spyod pa zhes bya'o//de nas chu'i lha sprin dang na bun 
44b6(phal chen): kun tu grub pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi mthus lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba'i mtshan rnams re re thams 
44b7(phal chen): cad kyang /sangs rgyas zhing gi rdul dang mnyam pa yi//byams pa mngon du gyur pas byung ba bzang //sems can kun gyis bltas zhing mi ngoms so//'gro ba kun tu rgyal ba rnams drung du//skyo ba med par spyad cing 
44b8(phal chen): rnams sbyangs te/rnam par sna tshogs chos kyi sgo yang ston//rgya mtsho shugs ni 'di'i tshul la 'jug/rgyal ba rnams ni 'pho bar mi snang ste//phyogs rnams bcu nas byon par snang ba med//snod kyi 
45a1(phal chen): @#//tshul bzhin sems can rnams kyang lte//chu'i gtsug phud dkyil 'khor nga la lta//de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul ni gzhal du med//sems can kun gyis bsam par mi nus te//
45a2(phal chen): phyogs rnams kun tu khyab par sgra bsgrog go/'di ni snyan pa'i gtsug dpal yul yin no//sangs rgyas 'od ni mi zad gnas pa ste//bsam gyis mi khyab chos kyi dbyings kyis kyang //sems can thams 
45a3(phal chen): cad 'dul ba rab tu ston//spos kyi phung po sna tshogs lha yis mthong //de bzhin gshegs pa nam mkha' mnyam pa ste//phyogs rnams kun tu dri med mtshan nyid med//phyogs rnams kun na'ang bzhugs 
45a4(phal chen): par rab tu snang /dpa' rlabs sgra'i bsod nams 'od kyis mthong //snying rje'i sgo ni rgya mtsho lta bu ste//sems can kun dang mnyam par snon bshad do//sprin dang 'dra bar 'gro bar mngon sum mo//de ltar 
45a5(phal chen): de ni dga' ba 'byung bas mthong //phyogs bcu zhing rnams ci snyed gang yod pa//de dag kun na sangs rgyas gdan bzhugs snang //shin tu rgyas pa'i byang chub mngon sangs rgyas//dga' 
45a6(phal chen): ba rdul med dbyangs kyis de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas spyod yul chags pa med pa ste//shin tu rgya che phyogs rnams kun tu gnas//rnam par 'phrul ba rgyal ba ston mdzad do//de la 'phro ba'i 'od 
45a7(phal chen): kyi lha 'jug go/sbyor ba'i spyod pa rnams la mtha' med de//'gro ba kun dbyings thub pa med par 'jug/rgyal ba'i byin gyi rlabs ni 'di 'dra ste//nga ros 'gengs pa rnam thar 'di la 'jug/de la rgya mtsho rin po che'i 'od 'byung ba'i 
45a8(phal chen): dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni//'gro ba thams cad dang mnyam pa'i bsod nams rgya mtsho rin po che 'byung ba'i rgyan ces bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lha rdo rje kun nas sra zhing mi choms pa'i rgyal mtshan dang ldan pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/dge ba'i rtsa ba 
45b1(phal chen): thams cad bsrung ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lha rdul dang dri ma med pa'i gnas dang ldan pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa rgya mtsho mang po rnam par sel ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lta kun nas 
45b2(phal chen): chu dang ri bo dang khyim gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi ngan song dang ngan 'gro'i lam rnam par zlog pa zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lhas skyabs rin po che mig gi zla ba'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/shugs chen pos 
45b3(phal chen): gti mug rnam par sel ba zhes bya 'o//rgya mtsho'i lha klu'i dpal pad mo sna tshogs kyi gtsug pud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/rgyud thams cad kyi sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar byed cing bde ba 'byung bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i la chu lta 
45b4(phal chen): bur ro mthun pa 'dzin pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni /sems can thams cad kyi lta ba dang /rmongs pa'i rang bzhin dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho lha rin po che 'od 'phro ba'i me tog gi tshon gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa na/rin po che'i 
45b5(phal chen): rigs thams cad 'byung zhing byang chub kyi sems rab tu skye ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lha ri rab sra ba'i rdo rje sna tshogs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems rgya mtsho mi sgul ba'i bsod nams la rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i
45b6(phal chen): lha 'brug sgra sgrog pa rgya mtsho'i shugs kyi dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/ting nge 'dzin gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad chos kyi dbyings su yang dag par 'jug pa'i rgyal mtshan ces bya'o//de nas rgya mtsho'i lha rin po che'i 'od 'byung 
45b7(phal chen): ba'i dpal gyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/rgya mtsho'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//bskal pa rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab par/sangs rgyas 
45b8(phal chen): rgya mtsho rgya chen mad mchod cing //'gro ba dag la bsod nams mtsho spel bas//de phyir rgyal ba lha me lha nge 'o//'gro ba kun tu rgyal ba mngon par mdzes//sems can kun gyi dbang po bsam rgyud du//chos kyi rgya mtsho rgya chen ston pa mdzad//
46a1(phal chen): @#//
46b1(phal chen): chu klung gi lha rdul thams cad dri ma med par rnam par dag pa'i mig gi rnam par thar ba ni/sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i rdul dang 'grogs pa'i snying rje'i tshul gyis kun tu rgyas par 'gengs pa zhes bya'o/
46b2(phal chen): chu klung gi lhang ros khyab par byed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams can kyi sgra yid du mi 'ong ba zlog cing phan pa skyed pa zhes bya'o//chu klung gi lha srog chags kyi rgyud mtha' yas pa skyob pa'i rnam par thar ba ni/sem can 
46b3(phal chen): thams cad la 'tshe ba med pa'i byams pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//chu klung gi lta gdung pa med pa rgya mtsho gdung pa med pa'i 'od dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad gdung par mi nus pa'i sgo kun nas ston pa zhes 
46b4(phal chen): bya'o//chu klung gi lha dga' bde kun nas 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni//sems can thams cad kyi 'jungs pa sel cing gtong ba phun sum tshogs pa yongs su spyod pa zhes bya'o//chu klung ga lha yon tan gyi shugs yangs 
46b5(phal chen): pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/thams cad tshim par byed pa'i bsod nams kyi zhing rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya'o//chu glung gi lha 'jig rten thams cad kyi dpal rnam par snang ba'i rnam par thar pa na/sems can 
46b6(phal chen): thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa rnam par byang bar byed cing //'khon du 'dzin pa dam po dang khro ba'i shugs dang bar byed pa zhes bya'o//chu klung gi lha tshogs chen po rgya mtsho'i dpal gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa 
46b7(phal chen): na 'gro ba thams cad bde ba phun sum tshogs pa myong bar byed cing thar par rgya mtsho la 'jung pa zhes bya'o//de nas chu klung gi lha chu bo kun dang ldan pas sangs rgyas kyi mthus//chu klung gi lha'i 'khor 
46b8(phal chen): kyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//chos rnams rgya mtsho mtha' spyod dag/don cad rgyal bas sems can don du sbyangs 
47a1(phal chen): @#//'jig rten bkang zhing nyon mongs gdungs ba'i rnams//sprin chen char bzhin rab tu zhi bar mdzad//bskal pa brjod du med par rgyal bas sngon//smon lam 'od kyis sems can dbyings shes 
47a2(phal chen): shing /'gro ba yongs smin dbang po chos kyang mkhyen//'khyil ba zhes bya chu klung lha dga' bo//'gro dang mnyam pa'i snying rje'i tshul bsgoms shing //mgon po sems can nyon mongs dri ma sbyong //yongs smin 
47a3(phal chen): sems can rgyal ba mngon tu lta//de la dri med mig gi lha yang mos//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyangs snyan rgya chen ni//kun tu bsgrags pas 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal zhi/sangs rgyas dbyangs snyan thos nas 
47a4(phal chen): dga' mgur yid/rnam thar 'di ni sgra dbyangs sgrog pas thos//bskal pa rgya mtshor 'gro la phan don tu//sngan cad byang chub spyod pa spyad mthong na//de phyir de ni phyogs bkang 'jig rten mdzes//
47a5(phal chen): lam rgyud stobs pa rjes su dran zhing dga'//rgyal ba sngan cad spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe//sna tshogs sgo nas sems can smin par mdzad//de dag bsod nams rgya mtshos sdug bsngal 'jil//zhi byed chu klung 
47a6(phal chen): lhas ni ngas dga' bo//sbyin pa'i sgo mo tshad med mi zad mang //'gro ba 'dod pa kun la yongs btang zhing //rgyal bas 'gro ba'i 'jungs ba yongs su bstsal//kun nas dga' dpal rnam thar 'di mthong ngo//
47a7(phal chen): thabs tshul rgya mtsho thams cad bsgoms pas na/yon tan mang po brgya phrag mang pos byung //rgyal bas 'gro ba thams cad tshim mdzad de//rgya chen sgra dbyangs shugs ldan lha yang dga'//sgrib pa'i glong 
47a8(phal chen): dang 'gro ba'i nyon mongs dri/'gro ba rnams la byams pa bskyed sbyangs bas//mkha' mnyam 'od kyis sangs rgyas rnam par mdzes////rnam par mdzes pa'i chu klung lha dga' 'o//sangs 
47b1(phal chen): rgyas rang byung yon tan rgya mtsho ste/bsod nams zhing ni 'dus byas sdug bsngal 'dor//byang chub lam la rim gyis yong smin mdzad//rgya mtsho'i tshogs dpal rnam thar 'di yin no//de la lo tog gi lha dang 
47b2(phal chen): 'jam pa'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/ro dang mdangs thams cad skyed cing //sems can thams cad sangs rgyas kyi gzugs kyi sku rab tu 'thob pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lha dus kyi me tog rnam par 
47b3(phal chen): mdzes pa'i 'od kyi rnams par thar pa ni//'gro ba thams cad nye bar 'tsho bas dga' ba chen po'i shugs kyi tshim pa skyed pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gyi lha bstsal dam pa'i stobs kyi rnam par thar pa ni//chos 
47b4(phal chen): thams cad kyi dbyings yongs su rgyas pas shin tu dag pa'i spyod yul zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lham dangs kyi dang mtha' yas pa rab tu 'gyed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/snying rje mtha' yas pa'i sgo nas//sangs 
47b5(phal chen): rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba rab tu 'gyed cing rjes su dran pa zhes bya'o//lo tog ga lha kun gyi rtsa ba 'bras bu'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas thams cad mngon tu lta zhing //sangs rgyas rnams la yon 
47b6(phal chen): tan yang dag par bskyed pa ston pa zhes bya'o//lo tog go lha gtsug bud kha dog sna tshogs kyi rnam par thar pa ni /'gro ba thams cad kyi mos pa'i me tog yongs su sbyangs ba zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lta 
47b7(phal chen): yal ga dri ma med par grub pa'i rnam par thar pa ni /sems can la phan pa'i byams pa dang /bsod nams rgya mtsho rgya chen po rnam par 'phel bar byed pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lga spos sna tshogs shin tu 
47b8(phal chen): grub pa'i rnam par thar pani//chos thams cad la spyod pa kun tu gnon pas rtsom pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gyi lha bzang zhing yid du 'or bar snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi zhum pa dang /
48a1(phal chen): @#//yid du mi 'ong ba rnam par zlog cing chos kyi dbyings thams cad yongs su dag par byed pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lha kha dog rnam par dag cing /'od dri ma myed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/'gro 
48a2(phal chen): ba thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba la rnam par lta zhing chos kyi 'khor gyi dbang po tshim par byed pa zhes bya'o/de nas lo tog gi lha dang 'jam dpal gyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus//lo tog gi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 
48a3(phal chen): 'khor thams cad la rnams par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rang byung ye shes sgron ma skyod pa ni//'gro ba kun kyi skyabs dang mgon gyur te//ma lus 'gro ba'i bde 
48a4(phal chen): ba yang dag skyed//bsod nams rgya mtsho sangs rgyas bla na med//sangs rgyas mnyan na don yod yon tan sprin//'gro ba kun kyi sdug bsngal zhi bar mdzad//chos rgya chen po mi zad rab tu skyed//dus kyi dkyil

48a6(phal chen): de mthong ngo//sems kyi skad cig kun la 'gro mnyam ba'i//thugs rjes rgya mtsho sngan cad rnam sbyangs pas//de phyir rdzu 'phrul rnams ni mtha' yas so//de ni mdangs mthong lha yis rab tu mthong //thabs rnams 
48a7(phal chen): kun gyi sgo rnas don yod par/sangs rgyas 'jig rten kun tu khyab gyur cing //'gro ba kun kyi nyon mongs dag par mdzad//'di ni thams cad sgo mo'i rnam thar to//'gro ba 'di ni mos yangs gang yod pa 
48a8(phal chen): de dag thams cad rgyal ba'i mthus byung ste//de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho'o//nags tshal rgyan gyis gtsug bud de la 'jug/'jig rten rnams la byams pa che bskyed de//sems can 
48b1(phal chen): rnams la phan phyir rang byung skyes//lam rab dge ba zhi ba rab tu ston //zhi ba sna tshogs dpal gyi rnam thar to//phyogs su sems can yongasu smin byed pa'i //bde gshegs spyod pa ji ltar rnam dag pa//
48b2(phal chen): byang chub shing las de dag sgra yang 'byung //spos zhim dpal gyi lha yis de thos so//rgyal ba 'jig rten yid du 'ong ba spong //dga' ba ma lus rnams kyang rab tu skyed//'gro ba kun gyi dbang po rnam par
48b3(phal chen): sbyong /dge bar snang ba'i lha yis de mthong ngo//'gro ba kun na sangs rgyas shin du 'phags//skye bo kun kyi bsam pa des mkhyen te//sna tshogs sgo nas 'gro ba yongs smin mdzad//rnam dag kha dog
48b4(phal chen): 'od kyi rnam thar to//de la sman gyi lha phun sum tshogs pa'i rnam par thar pa ni//bsam pa kun nas rnam par lta ba'i gnon pas 'gro ba rab tu gnon pa zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha tsan dan gyi nags tshal
48b5(phal chen): gyi shugs 'chang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/blta ba yod pas sems can 'dren ba'i 'od ces bya'o//sman gyi lha 'od rdul med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni /sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs

48b7(phal chen): ba zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha spu'i glong kun nas 'od byung ba'i rnam par thar pani/nad thams cad la snying rje'i dkyil 'khor gyi shugs kyi rgyal mtshan zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha mun pa rnam par sel cing rnam
48b8(phal chen): par sbyong ba'i rnam par thar pa ni //'gro ba mdongs pa lta bur thams cad kyi ye shes kyi mig rnam par dag par byed pa zhes bya'o//sman gyi lta sgra dbyangs kun tu 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa nisangs

51a8(phal chen): rdul chu bo'i dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par ther pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bstan pa'i yon tan snang ba yang dag par ston pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lta kun tu mngon bar lta ba'i mig gi rnam par 
51b1(phal chen): thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi rmi lam gyi mthar yang dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams bar 'phel bar byed pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lha kun nas sra ba'i rdo rje'i mtshams kyi mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/don rgya mtsho 
51b2(phal chen): rgya chen po mtha' yas pa dang /'grogs pa zhes bya'o//de nas ri'i lha chen rin po che'i rtse mo me tog shin tu rgyas par gnas pas sangs rgyas kyi mthus//ri'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad 
51b3(phal chen): la/rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba'i rnam 'phrul mi zad tshad med ltos//sngon tshe spyod rab mtha' yas rgyal bas spyod//rnam thar zhing gi rdul snyed bsgom mdzad 
51b4(phal chen): pas/'gro ba ma lus dga' ba'i shugs kyang skyed//sangs rgyas spu nas dri ma med dpal bkye g.yan lag mtshan brgyan 'gro ba kun bkang zhing //byams tshul mtha' yas 'jig rten kun la ston//nags tshal 
51b5(phal chen): rgyas pa'i gtsug ni rnam thar to//sangs rgyas sku ni mtha' yas 'gro bar mngon//'jig rten kun tu phyogs bcu bkang zhing 'phags//dbang po dang ba'i sems can rangs shing dga'//rnam thar 'di la 
51b6(phal chen): 'phags pa'i rgyal mtshan 'jug/rang 'byung mkha' mnyam 'jig rten kun ma chags//bskal pa kun spyod brtson 'grus stobs mi skyo//sna tshogs mkhas 'gro sbyor bas ma lus 'dul//dga' 
51b7(phal chen): ba dri med gtsug phud rnam thar to//'jig rten kun ni mdongs gyur mun stug zhugs//thugs rjes der bskyed sangs rgyas 'od bkye zhing //'gro khams yun rings nyal ba sad par mdzad//rig nas dga' 'od 
51b8(phal chen): lha'i rnam thar to//srid pa'i rgya mtshor spyad pa sngon spyod tshe//zhing rdul mnyam pa'i sems can gtso mchod pas//de dag dgyes bzhin sems can byang chub bkod//zhi ba'i sa 'di stobs 'chad lha 'jug go/
52a1(phal chen): @#//las kyi sprin sgrib g.yogs shing bsgribs pa'i rnams//srid par sems can sdug bsngal 'khor gzigs nas//de dag mun pa ye shes 'od kyis bstsal//gzi brjid dpal 'od rnam thar 'di 
52a2(phal chen): sems so/rgyal ba'i spu las sangs rgyas yon tan kun//phyogs dbyings sems can bsam pa bzhin du grags//rdzu 'phrul rnam pa bskal pa kun tu gnas//'od ma rdul kyi chu bo'i lha 'jug go/sems can kun spyod 
52a3(phal chen): dge ba rgya mtsho kun//de la phyogs bkang sangs rgyas mngon par snang //sna tshogs thabs kyis 'jig rten chos kyang ston//tshul mngon mig gi lha yis de thos so//chos sgo rgya mtsho mi zad tshad med pa//sangs rgyas gsung 
52a4(phal chen): gcig sems can kun la gsung //bskal pa kun rjod 'jig rten mang la ston//rdo rje tshul mig 'di'i gnas la 'jug/de la sa'i lha dpal gyi me tog kun nas shin tu rgyas par dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/
52a5(phal chen): skad cig skad cig cing //sems can thams cad la byams pa dang /snying rje'i dkyil 'khor gyis rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//sa'i lha bsod nams brtan pa shin tu bstsags pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/
52a6(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi stobs dang shugs kyi rgyan ces bya'o//sa'i lha sems can rin po ches rgyas pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/ting nge 'dzin sna tshogs bsgoms pa rnam par dpyod pa dri ma med pa 
52a7(phal chen): zhes bya'i/sa'i lha dus su dge ba'i mig rab tu ston pa'i rnam par thar pa ni /sems can thams cad dga' zhing bde ba dang rtse ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//sa'i lha gser gyi mig gi dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni//lus thams 
52a8(phal chen): cad yongs su dag par byed pa'i tshul stan pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha spu las spos kyi 'od rnam par 'gyed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yon tan rgya mtsho thams cad rnam par sgom pa brjod pa zhes 
52b1(phal chen): bya'o//sa'i lha sgra dbyangs yid du 'ong ba bsgrog pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad smra ba'i sgra rgya mtsho byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha me tog gi klong sna tshogs kyi gtsug bud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs 
52b2(phal chen): rgyas kyi zhing phun sum tshogs pa'i rang bzhin dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha kun nas rdo rje'i rdul gyis rgyud kyi rnam par thar pa ni /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyi 'khor lo byin gyi rlabs kun nas rgyu zhing 'byung ba zhes 
52b3(phal chen): bya'o//sa'i lte khyim dri ma med pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi tshul la yang dag par 'jug pa'i chos kyi rgyan bsgom pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//de nas sa'i lha dpal gyi me tog kun 
52b4(phal chen): nas shin tu rgyas pa dri ma med pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus sa'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sngan cad skad cig skad 
52b5(phal chen): cig bde gshegs kyis/brjod du med pa'i snying rje'i sgo rnams dang //byams pa'i sgo yang mi zad sgoms pas na//de'i tshe sangs rgyas sku ni mi shigs sra//dus gsum gtogs pa'i sems can bsod nams 
52b6(phal chen): dang //snying rje byang chub sems dpa'i bsod nams dag/rgyal ba'i spu gcig de snyed bsod nams 'od//bsod nams de mthong rab tu dga' bar 'gyur//'byung ba med cing byon pa gang yang med//ting 
52b7(phal chen): 'dzin zhi ba rgya mtsho rab tu zhugs//sems can rgyan kyi sa 'di rab tu ston//'di ni sna tshogs shing gi ze ba'i sa yin no//sngan cad thub pas spyod pa spyad pa dang //'gro ba'i sgrib pa rnam par zlog pa ni//rin 
52b8(phal chen): chen bkram pa'i 'od kyi sa yin te/rnam thar de la dga' bar lha lta'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa ni mtha' yas te//'jig rten kun tu skad cig cig la ston//dus tshod yang dag ston pa'i sa'i lha//dga' 
53a1(phal chen): @#//bar sangs rgyas yul la rnam par lta//sangs rgyas gsung ni mtha' yas bsam mi khyab//sems can kun bsgrags nyon mongs zhi bar mdzad//gser 'od mig gi dpal ni rab rga bar sangs 
53a2(phal chen): rgyas yon tan mtha' yas mang la lte//sangs rgyas sprul pas rnam par kun gzugs su//phyogs kun chos kyi dbyings su rgyas par mdzad//sku las dri zhim 'od kyi sa'i lha//sangs rgyas sems 
53a3(phal chen): can 'dul ba de la lta//phyogs rnams yid 'ong dbyangs kyis gang byas shing //'gro ba 'di ni phyi mtha'i bskal par brjod//yid du 'ong ba'i sgra dbyangs sa'i lha//rab tu dga' bar sangs rgyas dbyangs 
53a4(phal chen): la nyan/rgyal ba'i spu las sems can mnyam pa'i//spos kyi 'od bkye 'jig rten kun bkang zhing //sems can ma lus yongs su smin mdzad me tog gtsug pud 'byang bas yang dag mthong //de bzhin 
53a5(phal chen): gshegs pa sku rdo rje lta bu ste/mtshams su mi phyed sra ba'i ri bo 'dra//'gro ba kun gyis sangs rgyas bskyed mi nus//rdo rje rdul gyi lha ni des dga' 'o//de la grong khyer gyi lha rin chen rtse mo'i 'od kyi rnam par 
53a6(phal chen): thar pa ni/sems can la phan pa yang dag par sbyar ba 'byung ba zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha rgyan rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba'i dbang po yongs su smin par byed pa'i 'dul ba 
53a7(phal chen): ba 'byung ba zhes bya'o/grong khyer gyi lha rin chen dri med shin tu dge' ba'i rnam par thar pa ni /sems can thams cad bsod nams dga' ba myong bar byed pa zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lta slos pa'i mya ngan thams 
53a8(phal chen): cad spong bas rnams par snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/skyob pa dang mi 'jigs pa dang /snying rje snying po zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lga sgron ma shin tu rgyas pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/shes rab kyis kun tu 
53b1(phal chen): rnam par 'byed pa zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha 'od 'phro ba'i dpal mngon par 'bar ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/thabs kyi sgo kun nas ston pa'i mthu zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyis lha bsod nams kyi shugs snang 
53b2(phal chen): ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba kun gyis bsod nams 'byung ba'i snang ba zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lta lus dri ma med pas mnyam pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/sems can rmongs pa thams cad 
53b3(phal chen): la rmi lam rnam par ston pa'i 'od ces bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha spos kyi rgyal mtshan gtsug phud kyi rgyan gyis rnam par thar pa ni/nyon mongs pa dang mi dge ba dri rnam par sel cing thams cad mkhyen pa 
53b4(phal chen): nyid kyi dri yang dag par bskyed pa zhes bya'o//grong khyeragyi lha rin chen dpal gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pani/'gro ba thams cad kyi sgrib pa'i ri'i rnam par 'jom+sa pa'i 'od ces bya'o//de nas grong khyer 
53b5(phal chen): gyi lha/rin po che'i rtse mo'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus//grong khyer gyi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//mgon 
53b6(phal chen): po'i chos nyid bsam gyis mi khyab ste//snang ba brnyes shing phyogs su rnam par mdzes//sems can mtha' yas yongs su smin mdzad pa'i//sangs rgyas 'gro ba kun la mngon tu snang //
53b7(phal chen): sems can thams cad dpang po sna tshogs te//de dag ma lus rgyal bas 'gro bar mkhyen//rnam thar 'di ni rab tu thob pa'i phyir//sna tshogs 'od 'byung lha yang rab tu dga'//rgyal bas bskal 
53b8(phal chen): pa mtha' yas yang dag spyad//sngon chad rgyal bas chos kyang yang dag bzung //dga' ba'i yid kyis rtag tu de dag blangs//rnam thar 'di ni dga' ba'i 'od kyis thong //'gro ba 'dir ni 
54a1(phal chen): @#//thugs rje byams bskyed cing/de bzhin gshegs pas sngan cad sems can rnams//kun gyi mya ngan mtha' yas rnam par bzlog//nyon mongs yongs su spyod pa 'di la dga'//rgyal
54a2(phal chen): ba'i shes rab rgya che mtha' yas te//tshad med nam mkha' 'dra zhing mtshungs pa yin//de bzhin gshegs pa'i shes rab rnam spyod pas//mar me'i me tog 'od kyi lha dga'o//de bzhin gshegs pas
54a3(phal chen): 'gro mnyam sku gzugs rnams//'gro ba kun gyi bsam pa rnams la bstan//de 'dra'i tshul la rab tu bsgoms pa yis//mngon 'od 'phro ba'i lha de rab tu dga'//bsod nams rgya mtsho rgya chen mtha'
54a4(phal chen): yas pa/sngon chad de bzhin gshegs pas yongs su dbyangs//de 'dra'i rnam par thar la rnam lta bas//bsod nams dpal gyi 'od de rab tu dga'//srid par rab tu rmongs pa'i sems can rnams//
54a5(phal chen): 'khor gyur rab rib mdongs pa gzigs pas na/de dag phan phyir sangs rgyas mngon tu byung //dri med 'od kyis rnam thar 'di thob po//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdzu 'phrul stobs la ltos//'gro la sprin 'dra
54a6(phal chen): mtha' dang dbus yang med//de dag bstan cing 'gro ba rnam par 'dul//spos kyi rgyal mtshan 'od kyis 'di mthong ngo //sems can rmongs pas mun pas mdongs gyur cing//rnam pa sna tshogs sgribs
54a7(phal chen): pas bsgribs gyur pa/sangs rgyas 'od kyis rnam par kun shes 'gyur//rtse mo'i 'od kyi lha ni 'di la 'jug/de la snying po'i byang chub kyi lha dge ba dri ma med pa'i rgyun kyis brgyan pa'i rgyal mtshan
54a8(phal chen): gyi rnam par thar pa ni//sangs rgyas mang po mchod pa yang dag par 'byung zhing//sngon gyi smon lam gyi rgyan ston pa zhes bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha ri rab spo'i rin po che'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni//
54b1(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad dang mngon sum du byang chub kyi spyod pa yang dag par sgrub cing 'byung ba zhes bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha dbyangs kyi 'brug sgra bsgrog pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni 
54b2(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa la sangs rgyas ston cing rmi lam gyi mthar//sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi 'brug sgra sgrog pa zhes bya'o//snying po byang cub kyi lha me tog gi char sna tshogs 'bab pa'i mig 
54b3(phal chen): gi rnam par thar pa ni//dka' ba thams cad yongs su gtong zhing /rin po che'i char 'bebs pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//snying po byang chubakyi lha 'od 'phro mang po shin tu dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/'dul 
54b4(phal chen): ba'i sems can yongs su smin par byed cing snying po byang chub kyi phun sum tshogs pa rnams pa sna tshogs ston pa zhes bya'o//snying po bya chub kyi lha me tog gi phreng ba 'od kyi gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems 
54b5(phal chen): can thams cad kyi dbang po bzhin du chos kyi tshul zab mo dran pa ston pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha rin po che'i rgyal gyi char rab tu 'bebs pa'i rnam par thar pa ni //dga' ba yang dag par 
54b6(phal chen): myong ba mtha' yas pa la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes bya'o//snying po byed chub kyi lha mthu dam pa spos kyi mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan dang legs par rab tu rjod pa'i byin gyi rlabs shes 
54b7(phal chen): bya'o//snying po byang chub gyi lha rdo rje sna tshogs kyi sprin gyi rnam par thar pa ni//snying po byang chub rnam pa mtha' yas pa yang dag par ston pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//snying po byang chubakyi lha pad mo'i shing snang ba'i 'od kyi rnam 
54b8(phal chen): par thar pa ni/byang chub kyi shing yongs su zhi bas phyogs rgyas par byed pa zhes bya'o//snying po byang cub kyi lha dge ba'i lta ba sna tshogs rnam par ston pa'i/'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin gshegs 
55a1(phal chen): ba'i stobs sna tshogs yang dag par rgyu ba zhes bya'o//de nas snying po byang chub kyi lha dag pa dri ma med pa'i rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/snying po byang chub kyi lha'i 'khor 
55a2(phal chen): gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//bskal pa mang po mtha' dang dbus med par//de bzhin gshegs pas ji ltar sngon spyad dran//sangs rgyas 
55a3(phal chen): thams cad mchod cing bsgrubs pas na//de phyir de'i bsod nams mkha'i tshad//zhing rnams mtha' yas tshad med 'dra med par//mi zad gtong ba'i sgo nas sngon btang bas//ri bo sbo'i 'od kyi lha de ni//bde gshegs dran zhing 
55a4(phal chen): rab tu dga' bar 'gyur//bde gshegs sprul pa rnam pa mtha' yas pa//de dag zhing rnams kun tu rab tu rgyu//'gro bar sangs rgyas rmi lam bzhin ston cing //sprin gyi sgra dbyangs lha de rab tu dga'//mtha' yas bskal 
55a5(phal chen): par bka' ba rab spyad de//dang ba'i spyan gyis 'gro la phan pa'i phyir//rgya mtsho'i spyan yang yongs su sngon btang ste//de la me tog mig gi lha dga' bo//yon tan rin chen 'od 'phro mtha' yas sprin//'gro ba kun tu snying 
55a6(phal chen): po byang chub nas//rab tu bkye ba'i sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul gyis//'od 'phro 'gyed pa'i lus kyi lha yang dga'//sems can rnams kyi spyod pa rgya mtsho ste//de las rgyal ba chos kyi char yang 'bebs//ji ltar mos par 
55a7(phal chen): nem nur rnam par sel//de yis gtsug bud 'phyang ba dga' bo thob//rgyan kyi char 'bebs mig bzang lha de ni//sems kyi skad cig gcig gis tshad med pa'o//chos don dbyings kyi bye brag mtha' yas pa//rgya mtsho 
55a8(phal chen): tha dad yang dag shes pas 'jug/zhing rnams brjod du med pa thams cad du//'gro ba kun la sangs rgyas legs brjod pas//de phyir yon tan sangs rgyas zhing kun grags//de ni rjes su spos mig lnga yang dran//
55b1(phal chen): mtha' yas shing gi rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs//byang chub shing la kun kyang ma lus bstan//rdo rje sna tshogs sprin gi lha de ni//byang chub shing la dga' bar rnam par lta//sangs rgyas ye shes snying po byang chub 
55b2(phal chen): la/phyogs rnams kun tu mtha' dang mu med de//pad mo snang zhing gsal ba'i lha de ni//rnam thar rnam yangs 'di la rab dga' bo//sangs rgyas stobs rnams yongs dag bsam mi khyab//snying po byang chub 'dir 
55b3(phal chen): ni kun kyang grag/yang dag bsgrubs pas byung ba de dag ni//thams cad sna tshogs snang ba'i 'od nyan to//de la rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpal gyi phyag rgya'i rnam par thar pa ni//dga' ba yangs pa rin po che'i char gyis 
55b4(phal chen): kun tu khyab pa zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'gro ba dmo snang ba'i 'od zer gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi sku 'od gzer gyi kha dog pad mo'i dri ba thams cad du ston pas dga' bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gi 
55b5(phal chen): 'gro me tog gtsug phud kyi dpal dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems kyi skad cig thams cad la de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi dbyibs dang rgyan 'byung ba zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'gro
55b6(phal chen): shin tu mdzes par gnon pa'i rnam par thar pa ni /gom pa 'dor ba thams cad mtha' yas pa 'dul ba'i rgyan ces bya'o//rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpa' gyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/skad cig gcig gis 
55b7(phal chen): pad mo'i dri ba sna tshogs sprul pa byed cing rin po che'i char ba dang dbyangs dang snang ba kun tu 'gyed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gis bgro dga' ba'i dbyangs snyan pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/rkang gis 'gro bad mo yid 
55b8(phal chen): du 'ong ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/pad mo'i gseb thams cad nas yid du 'ong ba'i chos kyi dbyings 'byung bas snang ba skyed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gi 'gro rdul gyi shugs kyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa'i lus 
56a1(phal chen): kyi 'od gzer rnam pa sna tshogs thams cad kyi dra bas snang ba byed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'gro me tog mang po'i dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad la dge ba'i rtsa 
56a2(phal chen): ba rgya mtshos yang dag par skul ba 'byung ba zhes bya'o//de nas rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpal gyi phyag rgya sangs rgyas kyi mthus//rkang gis 'gro ba'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam 
56a3(phal chen): par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//dga' ba rgya mtsho'i tshul gyis shugs bskyed cing //bskal pa mtha' yas sngon chad spyod pa mdzad//zhing rnams kun du rdul shin tu phra mnyam 
56a4(phal chen): pa'i//sangs rgyas rnams kyang dgyes pa'i thugs kyis mchod//skad gcig sems la pad mo brjod yangs pa//dri zhim sna tshogs mngon par sprul mdzad nas//zhing rnams kun tu rgyal ba gshegs mdzad 
56a5(phal chen): pa//snang ba'i 'od gzer lhar des rab tu mthong //'dren pa rnams kyi chos nyid bsam mi khyab//'khor rnams kun tu 'byam klas mtha' yas pa'i//rnam par 'phrul pa mi zad rab tu ston//me tog gtsug 
56a6(phal chen): phud lha des de mthong ngo //phyogs bcu'i zhing gi dbyings rnams ma lus pa//thams cad kun tu gshegs shing bzhud pa yis//sems can mtha' yas yongs su smin par mdzad//shin tu mdzes pas dga' 
56a7(phal chen): bar de mthong ngo //sems can snyed kyi sprul pa kun 'phros pas//re re'i sku'i chos kyi dbyings kyang 'gengs//rin chen dri med 'od kyang rab tu gtong //rkang gro dpal gyi mtshan gyis 'di mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs 
56a8(phal chen): pa'i spyod yul mtha' yas te//'khor du rgyal ba chos kyi char yang 'babs//bde gshegs de'i 'khor na dgar gnas shing //dga' sgra dbyangs kyis rnam thar de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas dbyangs ni mkha' dang 
56b1(phal chen): mnyam pa ste//dbyangs kyi yan lag yon tan kun dang ldan//'jig rten kun bkang sems can rnam par 'dul//tsan dan shing gi 'od ni de la nyan//spu rtse kun nas rgyal ba rnam 'phrul dang //dus gsum gshegs pa'i 
56b2(phal chen): rgyal ba'i mtshan gyi rnams/sgra bsgrags thos shing dga' ba skyer 'gyur te//de ni rdo rje 'od kyi lhas mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs sku bsam gyis mi khyab ste//gom pa gom par yon tan rgya mtsho ston//de dag sems 
56b3(phal chen): can ji ltar mos pas mthong /rdul gyi 'od kyi lhas ni de mthong ngo //'jig rten mgon po rnam 'phrul rnam mang gis//phyogs bcur sems can yang dag skul ba mdzad//me tog 'khor mang rkang pas 'gro ba'i lha//
56b4(phal chen): mthong zhing dga' ba yangs pa rab tu 'thob//de la lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha khrel dang dge bas rgyas pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sngon gyi smon lam rjes su dran pas rnam 
56b5(phal chen): par dpyod pa zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtogs pa lha 'od gzer phyogs su snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams su med par sangs rgyas kyi 'od rnam par ston pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//lus kyi 
56b6(phal chen): ris su gtogs pa'i lha sgras 'dul ba rgya mtsho'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi gsung 'dul ba'i sems can thams cad la rdod cing yang dag par skul bas rgal 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lus kyi 
56b7(phal chen): ris sugatogs pa'i lha rgyan bzang po'i me tog gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku nam mkha'i mtshan nyid kyi byin gyi rlabs kun nas rab tu gnas pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//luskyi ris 
56b8(phal chen): su gtogs pa'i lha spyod yul mtha' yas dpal gyi rnam par thar ba ni/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yul 'gro ba rnams la yang dag par ston pa'i mthu zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris gtogs pa'i lha snang 
57a1(phal chen): ba'i phreng ba 'dzin pa'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad gsol ba dang 'dres pa'i mdangs kyis dga' ba zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lta spos dri ma med 
57a2(phal chen): pa'i sprin las char 'bab pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sem can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i dri ma mngon par sel ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//lus kyi ri su gtogs pa'i lha srung 'dzin yongs su 
57a3(phal chen): sdug pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/semasacan thams cad kyi bdud kyi las dang rmongs pa rnam par zlog pa zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris gtogs pa'i lha sgo kun nas yongs su gnon pa la mngon du gyur pa'i mig 
57a4(phal chen): gi rnam par thar pa ni/'jig rten gyi dbang po thams cad kyi gnas su rgyal ba rnam par dmigs par mngon du ston pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//lus kyi ris su btogs pa'i lha khrel dang dpal mi g.yo ba'i 'od 
57a5(phal chen): kyi rnam par thar pa ni//sems can thams cad kyi dge ba'i shugs thams cad yang dag par 'dzin cing yang dag par spyod pa zhes bya'o//de nas lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha khrel dang /dge bas rgyas 
57a6(phal chen): pa'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/lus kyi ris su gtogs pa lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ri rab 
57a7(phal chen): rdul phran shin tu phra snyed kyi//bskal pa de dag pha snga'i bskal pa ni//rdul ces bya bar 'od mdzes 'od gzer rgyal//mchod de smon lam btab pa bdag gis dran//sangs rgyas sku las 'od 
57a8(phal chen): 'phro rab bkye ba//de'i 'od gzer chos kyi dbyings su rgyu//rgyal ba'i 'od gzer dra ba sems can 'dul//'od 'phro 'od gzer lha yis de mthong ngo //dbyangs kyi yan lag yon tan kun rgyas 
57b1(phal chen): pa'i/sangs rgyas gsung ni zhing rnams kun tu sgrog/'gro ba ma lus thams cad yang dag bskul//dbyangs kyis 'dul ba de la dga' bas nyan/rgyal ba'i sku ni rab tu zhi ba la ltos//sku gzugs 
57b2(phal chen): du ma'ang mchan nyid mi mnga' ste//'gro ba kun na'ang bzhugs par rab tu snang //me tog gtsug gis ci bzhin dus mthong //'dren pa'i chos nyid bsam gyis mi khyab ste//sems can de dag dad pa ci bzhin 
57b3(phal chen): du//la la bzhugs dang bzhengs dang gshegs par mthong //de yang spyod lam 'byung ba'i lha'i mthong ngo //rgyal ba'i tshems kyis mtshams rnams thams cad nas//dri zhim 'od 'phro sgron ma'i sprin bkye 
57b4(phal chen): pas/'gro ba kun kyi nyon mongs zhi bar 'gyur//dri med spos kyi sprin gyis de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas mgon po 'gro ba'i skyabs su byung //nyon mongs shin tu sdug bsngal gzir ba rnams//mgu par 
57b5(phal chen): mdzad cing nyon mongs thams cad bsil//snang ba'i phreng ba 'chad pas 'di mthong ngo //'dren pa bskal pa brgyar yang shin tu dkon//rang byung 'jig rten don mdzad skyes pa ni//'jig rten kun gyi dbul 
57b6(phal chen): ba rnam par sel/khrel yod dpal gyi 'od snang de skad smra//sems can nyon mongs sgrib pas nyon mongs shing //bdud kyi las kyis bkrugs te 'phyin pa'i rnams//de dag rgyal ba rnam thar lam ston to//
57b7(phal chen): srung zhing skyong ba'i dpal gyis de mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba ltos//sangs rgyas sprin gyis chos kyi dbyings mnan cing //'jig rten dbang po gnas su sems can 'dul//
57b8(phal chen): kun sgo'i mig gi dpal gyi spyod yul lo//de la lag na rdo rje mthu chen kha dog bzang po'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/yon tan mtha' yas pa dang /gzugs kyi sku rnam par dpyod pa yang dag par ston pa zhes 
58a1(phal chen): @#//bya'o//lag na rdo rje nyi ma'i shugs kyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi ba spu las nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi sprin sna tshogs kyi rgan 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje 
58a2(phal chen): ri rab me tog 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/rnam par 'phrul ba rgya chen po bstan pas/yon tan mtha' yas pa'i lus su 'phrul ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdorje rnam dag sprin gyi dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin 
58a3(phal chen): gshegs pa'i dbyangs mtha' yas tshad med pa'i rjes su song ba zhes bya'o//lag na rde rje dbang po shin tu mang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'jig rten gyi dbang po thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul ba ston cing sangs 
58a4(phal chen): rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis yang dag par skul ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje yid du 'ong ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi dbyings rab tu phye ba thams cad ma lus pa la yang dag par spyod pa 
58a5(phal chen): zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje shing gi sgra dbyangs kyi rnam par thar ba ni/shing gi lha thams cad 'dren pa'i rgyan yid du 'ong bas brgyan pa zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje seng ge'i 'od kyi rgyal po'i rnam par thar pa ni/de 
58a6(phal chen): bzhin gshegs pa'i bsod nams kyi dkyil 'khor yangs pa'i tshogs kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i yod pa ces bya'o//lag na rdo rje 'od 'phro stug po'i dpal gyi mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can ma rungs pa'i 
58a7(phal chen): rgyud la lus 'jigs 'jigs ltar rab tu ston cing kun tu rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje rin chen gtsug phud pad mo'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/byang chub sems dpa'i rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan 
58a8(phal chen): pa'i gtsug phud kyi nor bu rin po che thams cad kyi char mngon par 'bebs pa bzhes bya'o//de nas lag na rdo rje mthu chen kha dog bzang po'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus//lag na rdo rje'i 'khor gyi dkyil 
58b1(phal chen): 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//chos rgyal chos kyis mdzes pa'i//chos nyid de la rnam par ltos//yon tan mtha' yas sku gzugs 
58b2(phal chen): kyis/'jig rten kun tu rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas ba spu thams cad nas//'od gzer dra ba bsam mi khyab//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 'dra ba ste//phyogs bcu kun tu rnam par mdzes //sangs 
58b3(phal chen): rgyas rnam 'phrul de dag gis/chos kyi dbyings su khyabs byas shing //'gro ba kun la mngon sum du//mi zad sku ni shin tu ston//sangs rgyas gsung gi dkyil 'khor ni//zhing rnams kun tu rab 
58b4(phal chen): tu sgrog/gsung gis sems can thams cad kyis//bsam pa thams cad rab tu 'gengs //'jig rten dbang po kun gnas na//thub pa rab tu bzhugs par snang //de dag thams cad 'khor rnams 
58b5(phal chen): la/'dren pa dag gi chos ston mdzad//rgya mtsho chos kyi sgo bsgul bas//gnan dang gzhan gyi tshul dag gis//thams cad chos kyi bye brag don//rgyal ba zad med rab tu ston //rgyal chen mtha' med tshul 
58b6(phal chen): dag la/rgya mtsho 'khor rnams rab tu 'jug/sangs rgyas 'od gzer rab 'dres bas//kun kyis 'dren pa mthong bar 'gyur //zhing rnams bye ma'i rdul snyed du//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kun mchod 
58b7(phal chen): pas/bsod nams rgya chen nam mkha' bzhin//kun kyis 'dren pa rab tu mthong //sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul ma 'dres pa//thams cad zhing du ston pa mdzad//'gro la sangs rgyas mngon sum du//byang chub 
58b8(phal chen): snying por rnam par mdzes//'od gzer sna tshogs dkyil 'khor gyi//'od 'phro sprin ni kun tu snang //chos dbyings kun tu khyab byas shing //sangs rgyas spyod yul rab tu ston//de la byang chub sems dpa' 
59a1(phal chen): @#//kun tu bzang po'i rnam par thar pa ni//tshul rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab pas//de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan rgya mtsho la 'jug ste//'di ltar sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad yongs 
59a2(phal chen): su dag par bya ba dang /'gro ba gdul ba la mthar thug par mngon par sgrub pa zhes bya ba'i rnam par thar pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi zhabs kyi drung du phyin pas//yon tan gyi tshogs thams cad 
59a3(phal chen): kyi yul zhes bya ba'i rnam par tharapa dang //byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis nas sar smon lam rgya mtshos rab tu gnas pa 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i rnam par thar/sgo thams cad nas chos 
59a4(phal chen): kyi dbyings su bdag gi lus kyi sprin rdul snyed mtha' yas pas rgyas par 'gengs pa zhes bya ba'i rnam par thor pa dang //zhing thams cad du rang gi ming bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i 'khor lo sgrog pa'i byin 
59a5(phal chen): gyi rlabs shes bya ba'i rnam par thar ba dang //rdul shin tu phra ba'i thams cad kyi sbubs su byang chub sems dpa'i yul dang /rnam par 'phrul ba mtha' dang dbus med pa yang dag par ston pa zhes bya ba'i rnam 
59a6(phal chen): par thar pa dang /dus gsum gyi bskal pa skad cig gcig tu ston pas 'byung ba dang /'jig pa rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i rnams par thar pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' thamasacad kyi dbang po rgya mtsho'i 
59a7(phal chen): yul tshun du 'jug pa yang dag par ston pa'i zhes bya ba'i rnam par thal ba dang /gang 'di chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med par rang gi lus kyi sprul pas rgyas par rnam par 'phrul ba'i byin gyi rlabs shes 
59a8(phal chen): bya ba'i rnam par thar pa dang /gang 'di byang chub sems dpa'i chos thams cad kyi tshul phan tshun du ston pa rgya chen pos thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi tshul la 'jug pa zhes bya ba'i rnam par thar ba dag go/de 
59b1(phal chen): nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po rnam par thar pa'i tshul rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab pa dag gis//de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan rgya mtsho la 'jug cing sangs rgyas kyi mthus 'khor 
59b2(phal chen): rgya mtsho yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhing rnams rgyal bas dri med rnam par sbyangs//mkha' yangs rdul bzhin yon tan tshad kyang 
59b3(phal chen): med/sngon gyi rgyal ba'i sras po dri med gde la bsam yas chos sprin char yang phab//ji ltar rgyal ba gdan la bzhugs snang ba//de bzhin rdul sbubs ma lus kun nang skye//'on kyang bde 
59b4(phal chen): gshegs gshegs dang 'pho ba med//zhing rnams kun du yul yang rab tu ston//sa'i tshul la 'jug pa tshad med de//spyod pa thub pas byang chub sems dpa' bstan//bsam gyis mi khyab 
59b5(phal chen): tshul ni kun gsungs pas//rgyal sras rnams ni chos kyi dbyings la 'jug/chos kyi dbyings tshul rnam par rgya cher ste//mtha' yas phyogs su mtha' dang dbus med sgo//rdul phran sbubs su 
59b6(phal chen): rgyal ba 'phrul pa spros/'gro ba'i bsam pa kun kyang rab tu mkhyen//phyogs rnams thams cad zhing rnams kun tu yang //'jig rten gtso mtshan 'gro dang mnyam par byung //thams cad tshul sgor sangs 
59b7(phal chen): rgyas don yod de/'dul bas sems can thams cad rnam par spyod////rdul sbubs kun tu mtha' dang dbus med pa'i//rnam 'phrul yangs pa rang byung shin tu ston//sngon cad thub pa'i byang chub spyod pa rnams//
59b8(phal chen): ci bzhin kun kyang snying po byang chub brjod//yangs dus bskal pa ji snyed yod pa'i rnams//skad cig skad cig sangs rgyas ma lus ston//der ni sems can 'byung zhing 'jig pa'ang bstan//'jig rten 
60a1(phal chen): @#//gtso bo ye shes mtha' yas ltos//'byam klas mi zad mtha' yas lo grangs bas//rgyal sras thams cad sangs rgyas de lta ste//thams cad sgor yang sangs rgyas mtha' dbus med//rgyal 
60a2(phal chen): sras kun gyis shes par sla ba min//sangs rgyas mtha' 'dra rnam par mi rtog ste//chos nyid mi gnas chos kyi sprin dag gis//sprul pas spyod tshe kun tu rgyas mdzad cing //shing mchog kun drung byang chub 'tshang 
60a3(phal chen): rgya bstan/sangs rgyas gsung de rab tu brjod pas na//chos gnas thams cad ma lus brjod pa yin//'gro ba thams cad re re'i sgo dag nas//sangs rgyas chos rnams kun la rab tu 'jug/de la byang chub 
60a4(phal chen): sems dpa' dge ba dri ma med pa'i gzi brjid kyi 'od yid du 'ong ba'i rnam par thar pa ni//chos kyi dbyings thams cad du mngon par byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du 'gro ba'i rgyan byed pa zhes bya'o//byang 
60a5(phal chen): chub sems dpa' yon tan 'od mchog kun tu sgron ma'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems skad cig gis mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa'i sgo zad mi shes pa ston cing /chos ston kyi dbyings bsam gyis mi khyab pas 
60a6(phal chen): yongs su smin pa dang rnam par 'dul bar byed pa'i 'od ces bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' kun tu 'bar ba seng ge'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi tshogs 
60a7(phal chen): dang zhing yang dag par 'byung ba la rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//byang chub sems pa'i dpal gyi 'od gzer kun tu 'od 'phro'i rnam par thar pa ni/rdzu 'phrul gyi yul gyis rnam par spyod pa don yod pa zhes bya'o//byang 
60a8(phal chen): cub sems dpa' yon tan rgya mtsho kun tu sgra dbyangs rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thal ba ni/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan thams cad 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du yang dag par ston pa zhes bya'o//byang chub sem dpa' re 
60b1(phal chen): bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul ye shes kun tu snang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rjes su kun nas song zhing chos kyi dbyings la rnam par dpyod pa'i sprin chen po'i snying po zhes bya'o//byang chub 
60b2(phal chen): sems dpa' gtsug na rin chen kun tu rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi spyod pa la rab tu zhugs shing byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa mtha' yas pa shin tu 'byung ba zhes bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' 
60b3(phal chen): rgyal ba'i gsung kun tu 'jam dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas thams cad bsnyen bkur zhing mchod par nye bar 'gro ba'i snying po zhes bya'o//byangs chub sems dpa' bsod nams mi zad dri med kun tu gzi brjid 
60b4(phal chen): kyi rnam par thar pa ni/rdzu 'phrul dang rnam par 'phrul pa thams cad kyis byin gyi rlabs rgya chen po 'byung ba zhes bya'o//byangs chub kyi sems dpa' mtshon gyi 'od gzer kun tu snang ba'i rnam par thar ba ni/de bzhin gshegs 
60b5(phal chen): pa'i sku med pa las sangs rgyas kyi yul thams cad 'byung ba zhes bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dge ba dri ma med pa'i gzi brjid kyi 'od yid du 'ong bas sangs rgyas kyi mthus//byang chub sems 
60b6(phal chen): dpa'i rnam par thar pa rgya mtsho'i phyogs thams cad la rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ji snyed gang dag phyogs rnams kun gyi zhing //rgyal bas de kun skad cig thugs kyi sbyangs 
60b7(phal chen): 'gro ba kun tu zhi ba med pa'i gsung //sangs rgyas chos kyi 'khor lo rgya chen bskor//skad cig thugs kyis chos dbyings kun bkang ste//mtha' yas spyod yul dbyings rnams rgyal ba sten//'gro ba kun 
60b8(phal chen): las snying po byang chub tu//byang chub 'tshang rgya rnam par 'phrul ba mdzad//bskal pa brgya phrag stong rnams tshad med du//de bzhin gshegs pas sngan cad spyod spyad de//nam mkha' 'dra bar byas nas rab bkye 
61a1(phal chen): @#//zhing /sangs rgyas zhing 'di sngon tshe rab tu sbyangs//mtha' dang dbus med bskal par la la yis//skye ba med par rgyal ba'i yul bsams kyang //sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul stobs la dbus med pa/phyi 
61a2(phal chen): ma'i mtha'i bskal pa kun tu 'gengs/rgyal ba'i rdzu 'phrul yul la rnam par ltos//phyogs bcu'i zhing rgyan de dag rnam dag pa//rgyal bas 'dir ni kun kyang rab tu bstan//skad cig skad cig sna tshogs 
61a3(phal chen): gzugs kyang snang /bskal pa brgya stong tshad med pa dag tu//rgyal ba'i pa spu gcig mtha' shes bar bka'//tshul rnams kun skor sangs rgyas chags pa med//'gro ba 'di ni rgya chen bsam yas mdzes/sngon tshe'i 
61a4(phal chen): bskal par rgyal bas 'gro kun tu/sangs rgyas rgya mtsho rgya chen bsnyen bkur bas//de bas 'jig rten gtso la chos dbyings rnams//kun nas mchod pa mang po rgya chen 'byung //phyogs rnams bkang ste sangs 
61a5(phal chen): rgyas 'byung ba dag/rdul gyi sbubs su tshad med zhing yang ston//de dag yul du mi zad mtha' yas pa'i//bskal par rgya mtshor byin kyis rlom pa mdzad//sngon tshe'i bskal par rgyal bas 'gro don spyad//thugs 
61a6(phal chen): rje'i rgya mtsho tshad med rab skyed cing /'gro ba rgya mtsho kun nas 'khor ba'i//'khor rnams rgya mtsho mtha' yas kun kyang sbyangs//sangs rgyas mtshan med sku med rdul dang bral//de bzhin nyid gnas chos dbyings snying 
61a7(phal chen): po ste/'gro ba kun gyi mtha' yas gzugs su snang //'gro ba kun kyi sdug bsngal kun zhi mdzad//de la byang chub sems dpa' 'od gzer rgya mtsho 'od ces dpal gi zla ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/pha rol tu phyin pa 
61a8(phal chen): dang /sa'i shugs kyis sems can gyi khams kyi tshul dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnams par dag pa yang dag par 'byung ba zhes bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' sprin pa bo'i rgya mtsho dri med 'od kyi snying po'i rnam par 
61b1(phal chen): thar pa ni/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi sems skad cig la chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul gyis khyim sna tshogs la rnam pa lta ba zhes bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' yon tan rin chen 'byung ba ye shes dpal 
61b2(phal chen): kyi gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni//skal ba bsam gyis mi khyab par 'gro ba rnams la mngon sum du yon tan dri ma med pas yongs su dag pa sgrub pa zhes bya'o//byang chub sem dpa' rgya chen 'od 'bar blo 
61b3(phal chen): gros dbang phyug gi rnam par thar pa ni/byang chub sem dpa' thams cad kyi snying po byang chub tu thog ma 'gro ba'i ye shes kyi phyogs yang dag par 'byung ba'i rgyun ces bya'o//byang chub sems pa' pad mo 
61b4(phal chen): rnam par gnon pa 'od gzer gtsug phud kyi rnam par thal ba ni/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad tha mi dad par 'gro ba thams cad kyi dbang po dang /mos pa rgya mtsho'i 'od ces bya'o//byang chub semams dpa' 
61b5(phal chen): kun tu ye shes sprin gyi nyi ma'i rgyal mtshan snang pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas ye shes pa sgrub par//bskal pa mtha' yas bdag tu byin gyis rlob cing rnam par spyod pa zhes bya'o//byang chub
61b6(phal chen): sems dpa' mthu stobs brtson 'grus rdo rje yangs pa'i snying po'i rnam par thar pa ni/chos thams cad kyi phyag rgya mtha' dang dpus med pa la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes bya'o//byang chub semasad pa' spos kyi 'od 'phro 
61b7(phal chen): shin du 'bar ba'i 'od kyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni//sangs rgyas de ltar byung ba thams cad kyi thog ma'i byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa shes pa'i dkyil 'khor yang dag par sgrub pa zhes bya'o//byang chub 
61b8(phal chen): sems dpa'i rnam par snang mdzad yon tan yangs pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thal ba ni/rnam par snang mdzad kyi smon lam rgya mtsho thams cad kyi dbyibs la rab tu gnas pa zhes bya'o//byang chubasems dpa' 
62a1(phal chen): @#//bsod nams yangs pa shin tu 'phags pa'i 'od mchog yon tan 'byung ba'i blo gros kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi yul zab mo chos kyi dbyings thams cad la kun nas rab tu gnas pa rnam par 
62a2(phal chen): spyod pa zhes bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'od gzer rgya mtsho 'od chen dpal gyi zla bas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus byang chub sems dpa' rnams par phye ba rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa la rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs 
62a3(phal chen): su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//pha rol phyin pa rgya mtsho bsam yas dang //sa'i dkyil 'khor rgya chen kun kyang yongs su rgyas//tshad med sems can rnams par 'dul ba'i tshe//sangs rgyas zhing yangs rnams kyang 
62a4(phal chen): rnam par sbyangs/phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas zhing kun rgyas byas te//sangs rgyas sems can khams sbyin 'dul mdzad pa//skad cig thugs kyis chos kyi 'khor lo dag/mtha' yang 'gro mnyam bsam 
62a5(phal chen): pa tshim par ston/bskal pa rgya che mi zad mu med par//rgyal ba 'gro la mngon sum kun nas bzhugs//mgon pos sngon cad ji ltar sbyangs tshul du//rgyal bas spyod yul rnam dag rab tu bstan//chos rgyal de 
62a6(phal chen): dag ji ltar rnam 'phrul ba/phyogs rnams rgya mtsho ma lus kun na snang //snying po byang chub kun na'ang rgyal ba dag/'khor kyis bskor cing chos rnams gsung ba snang //'gro ba'i bsam pa thams cad tshim 
62a7(phal chen): mdzad cing /'jig rten dag na sangs rgyas tshul kun mdzes//dbang po ji bzhin chos kyi char yang 'bebs//sangs rgyas chos sku yangs pa rnam par mdzes//de bzhin nyid ltar mtshan nyid lus med de//rgyal 
62a8(phal chen): ba dri med 'od gsal chos kyi sku//ye shes zhi ba'i sku ni tshad med de//phyogs dbyings kun bkang chos kyi char yang 'bebs /chos rje thams cad stobs kyis rnam par dag/bde gshegs ye shes mkha' 'dra 
62b1(phal chen): mtha' yas te/rgyal chen 'gro ba mtha' dbus med bkang zhing //rgyal ba stobs la 'jug pa rab tu ston//sangs rgyas 'od kyis chos dbyings kun bkang zhing //sangs rgyas spyod pa thams cad rdzogs par 
62b2(phal chen): bstan/rgyal bas sngon tshe ji ltar rnam sbyangs pa//de bzhin ye shes sa rnams rgyal bas bstan//phyogs rnams kun tu rang byung rnam par mdzes//smon lam rnam par 'phrul ba ma lus ston//ji ltar sngon 
62b3(phal chen): tshe rgyal bas yongs sbyangs pa//rgyal ba'i 'od gzer dra ba kun nas sgrog/sangs rgyas yul ni phyogs su zad pa med//dbus dang mtha' med gzhal med mtha' yas dbyings//chos dbyings 
62b4(phal chen): kun tu chags med brnyes pa na//sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun na shin tu mdzes//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyi seng ge'i khri dang /pad mo'i ze ba'i dri thams cad dang /nor bu'i rgyal po thams cad kyi 
62b5(phal chen): ngos dang /'khor lo'i lte ba thams cad dang //me tog gi ze ba'i dri thams cad dang/stegs bu'i ngos thams cad dang //rta babs kyi sgo thams cad dang /nor bu gzugs kyi ngos thams cad dang /
62b6(phal chen): rin po ce 'od 'phro ba'i sgo thams cad dang /'od gzer sgron ma'i sgo thams cad dang /seng ge'i khri'i rgyan kyi sgo thams cad nas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi byang chub sems dpa' mang po 
62b7(phal chen): 'thon par 'gyur te/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho'i blo gros rnam par rol ba'i ye shes mngon par shes pa'i rgyal po zhes bya ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' sgra dbyangs kun tu sgrog pa dang /byang 
62b8(phal chen): chub sems dpa' nor bu rin po che sna tshogs kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gtsug phud dang /byang chub sems pa'i ye shes chen pos nyi ma'i sa rab tu gnon pa'i blo gros dang /byang chub sems dba' yon tan rin 
63a1(phal chen): @#//po che bsam gyis mi khyab pa ye shes rgya mtsho'i dpal dang /byang chub sems dpa' mig rgya mtsho pad mo'i sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dang /byang chub sems dpa' nam mkha'i tshul pad mo sna tshogs 
63a2(phal chen): gyi gtsug phud dang /byang chub sems dpa' gser gi 'od 'phro gzi brjid dkyil 'khor gyi 'od dang //byang chub sems dpa' kun tu sgra chos kyi dbyings kyi dbyings dang /byang chub sems dba' sprin yid du 'ong ba dri 
63a3(phal chen): med pa'i zla ba dang /de dag la stsogs pa byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed mchod pa'i sprin rnam pa sna tshogs rab tu 'gyed cing //'di ltar nor bu rin po che'i me tog gi sprin thams 
63a4(phal chen): cad ni rab tu 'byin/spos kyi sprin thams cad ni shin tu 'gyed//rin po che'i 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyi sprin thams cad ni mngon par 'byin//yul mtha' yas pa'i spos kun tu 'phro ba'i bdug pa'i sprin ni//
63a5(phal chen): rnam par 'gyed cing nor bu nyi ma'i 'khor lo'i 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gi sprin ni rab tu gtong /sil snyan kyi sgra dbyangs yid du 'ong ba'i sprin ni rab tu sgrog//rin po che 'od 'phro ba kha dog mtha' yas pa'i sgron ma 
63a6(phal chen): ma'i sprin gyi sa ni rab tu snang bar byed/nor bu sna tshogs kyi shing gi yal ga las me tog gi kha dog pa ni rnam par spyod//rin po che'i 'od dri ma med pa zad mi shes pa'i nor bu'i rgyal po'i char ni rab tu 'babs//
63a7(phal chen): rgyan mdzes pa thams cad yang dag par ston pa'i nor bu'i rgyal po'i sprin gyi char ni rab tu 'bebs shing /de lta bu dag la stsogs pa byang chub sems dpa' cher zhing yang sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu 
63a8(phal chen): phra ba snyed kyi mchod pa'i sprin kyi char rab tu 'bebs pa byed cing /lhags par gyur te//bcom ldan 'das la lan brgya stong du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas te//'khor gyi dkyil 'khor 
63b1(phal chen): rgya mtsho yod pa thams cad du mchod pa'i sprin rgyun mi 'chad pa'i char mngon par phab nas gang nas 'ongs pa'i phyogs su brten te/de bzhin gshegs pa la mngon tu bltas nas seng ge'i khri rin 
63b2(phal chen): po che sna tshogs du ma'i pad mo'i snying po can dag mngon par sprul te//skyil mo krung bcas te 'khod par gyur to//byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad kyang spyod yul rgya mtsho cha chen po yongs 
63b3(phal chen): su dag pa rnams so//sgo kun nas chos kyi snang ba rab tu thob pa rnams so//de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul chags pa med pa'i rjes su 'brang ba rnams so//tha dad pa yang dge bar shes ba thams cad 
63b4(phal chen): chos rgya mtsho la zhugs pa rnams so//rnam bar thar pa bsam gyis myi khyab pa'i snang ba rab tu thob pa rnalas so//sgo kun nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyings rab tu thob pa rnams so//gzugs 
63b5(phal chen): kyi sgo rgya mtsho thams cad kyis chos rgya mtsho rab tu 'dzin pa rnams so//dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes kyis la rab tu gnas pa rnams so//dga' ba'i shugs rgya chen po rab tu thob pa rnams 
63b6(phal chen): so||bsod nams dkyil 'khor mu med pa yongs su dag pa'i rnams te//chos kyi dbyings dang nam mkha' la rnam par rgyu zhing sangs rgyas thams cad mchod pa mngon par bsgrub pa la rab tu 
63b7(phal chen): brtson pa sha stag go/de nas byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho'i blo gros rnam par rol ba'i ye shes mngon par shes pa'i rgyal bos sangs rgyas kyi mthus 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho thams cad 
63b8(phal chen): cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas rnam par sangs rgyas mnyam nyid mkhyen//chags med rnyed pas nam mkha' lta bu ste//phyogs 
64a1(phal chen): rnams zhing kun rab tu snang byas nas//'byam klas 'khor na bzhugs pa rnam par mdzes//bde gshegs rang byung mtha' dang dbus med de//thams cad phyogs 
64a2(phal chen): kyi chos dbyings kun rgyas par//dbang phyug chen po kun kyis mchod bskor cing //shing gi rgyal po kun kyi drung na bzhugs//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdzu 'phrul stobs dag gis//skad cig sems la 
64a3(phal chen): zad med yon tan gzugs//rnam thar gzhan dang gzhan dag thob pas mthong //de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul ni mtha' yas so//bskal pa rgya mtshor sngon chad spyod pa'i tshe//de bzhin gshegs pas srid 
64a4(phal chen): pa rgya mtshor 'dir//sna tshogs thabs kyis sems can smin byas pa//de dag rgyal ba de'i chos 'dzin to//yongs dag dri med 'khor rnams 'khod pa dag/thams cad sangs rgyas lta zhing rnam par gnas 
64a5(phal chen): pad mo'i snying po pad mo gang na bzhugs pa//tshogs kyi nang na rgyal ba mngon par mdzes//sangs rgyas gang na bzhugs pa'i seng ge'i khri//rin chen nor bu'i rgyal po'i 'od 'phro zhing //spos 'phro sprin rnams mtha' 
64a6(phal chen): yas rab tu 'byung //snying po du ma'i me tog phreng sbyangs pa'o//rin chen 'od 'phro'i sprin ni rtag tu 'gyed//'od gzer sgron ma rgya chen rab tu 'bar//rta babs rnam pa sna tshogs rnams kyis brgyan//thub pa de bzhugs

64a8(phal chen): blags rin chen mang pos yongs su bzung //rdo rje 'od 'phro snang ba'i snying pos brgyan//gtsug na nor bu'i byang chub sems dpa'as bzung //sangs rgyas de bzhugs pa rnams par shin tu mdzes//phyogs bcur 
64b1(phal chen): sprul pa'i dra ba rab bkye zhing /de bzhin gshegs pa'i smon lam sgra yang sgrog/gzugs brnyan bzhin du zhing rnams kun na snang //thub pa de 'dra'i seng ge'i gdan la bzhugs//de nas byang chub sems dpa'

64b4(phal chen): gdan gyi 'khor/rgyan rnams thams cad gzugs brnyan gyur ba dang //'khor rnams de dag thams cad kun gyang te//skad cig skad cig rgyan kyi kha dog kun//rnam pa du mar sangs rgyas gdan las snang /sems can ji 
64b5(phal chen): ltar mos pa de dag kyang /'khor cing sangs rgyas tshad med gdan la lta//rin cen ldang ldi pad mo mang btags pa//phye zhing byang chub sems dpa' mang po rnams//sgra dbyangs yid du 'ong bar ba 'byin cing //
64b6(phal chen): sangs rgyas gdan la bzhugs la ston pa byed//sangs rgyas bsod nams tshad myed nam mkha' bzhin//'di dag rgyan gun de las rab tu byung //sa rnams re re'i rgyan rnams gang yod pa//'gro ba kun gyis 
64b7(phal chen): de dag bsam mi nus//mi phyed mtshams kyi rdo rje sna tshogs pa//'khor yangs rnam dag shin tu mnyam pa ste//nor bu'i dra ba sna tshogs kun tu bkram//byang chub shing gi sa'i 'khor kun to//sa phyogs 
64b8(phal chen): gdan rgyal ba'i gdan yod pa//kha dog mtha' yas rgya chen sna tshogs ste//me tog mngon gtor rin chen rnam par bkram//gser mtshon mdzes pas de ni rab tu brgyan//sa'i lha mo de dag rab dga' 
65a1(phal chen): @#//zhing /skad cig sems kyis mi zad sa yang ston//kun kyang rgyal ba'i zhal du mngon lta zhing /kha dog thams cad rgyan gyis sprin gtong ngo //rin chen sgron ma mang po gsal bar 'bar//spos 
65a2(phal chen): yang dri zhim mang po rtag tu 'byung /dus dang chod kyis rnam pa mang ston cing //lha rnams kun kyang sangs rgyas mchod pa byed//phyogs rnams bcu na zhing rnams ji snyed pa//de dag sa rgyan phun

65b1(phal chen): dag ltos/rin chen mang po tshon bzang rgyan tu bkye//'gro ba dag la chos kyi rgya mtsho sgro ba//de dag chos nyid nam par dag pa mthong //ma lus phyogs na sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//byang chub shing gi phun 
65b2(phal chen): sum tshogs pa dag/sa'i dkyil 'khor 'di na kun snang ste//de dag chos nyid rnam par dag pa rjod//sangs rgyas gdan las chos rnams rgya mtsho yi//chos sgo bde bar gshegs pas gang gsungs pa//sems 
65b3(phal chen): can kun kyis bsam pa ji bzhin du//sa 'khor rnams kyang kun nas rab tu 'byung //dri zhim 'od rnams rtag tu 'byung bas na//de dag ji bzhin snod kyi 'gro ba 'gengs//srog chags rnams kyi nyon mongs 
65b4(phal chen): zhi bar byed//sa 'khor de nas de 'dra bar du sgrog/sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul phun sum gang dag dang //dri med sa ni gang gis mdzes gyur ba'i//phun sum tshogs pa'i rgyan rnams re re zhing //bskal pa bye ba dag tu'ang brjod

65b7(phal chen): med khang na mdzes/'byam klas phyogs rnams kun tu mngon sum du//sangs rgyas 'gro gtso gzhal med khang na snang //rin chen kun gyis snying po rab tu brgyan//rgyan rnams kun gyis de bzhin mdzes shing 'bar 
65b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas pho brang 'ong ni bsam mi khyab//de na bzhugs shing sangs rgyas rab tu mdzes//ka'i rgyan ni nor bu sna tshogs ste//phyogs rnams kun sgor rta babs sprin kyang bres//stegs bu'i phreng ba kun nas 
66a1(phal chen): @#//rgyan tu bkod/gser gyi dril bu'i sprin kyang rnam par rgyu//me tog bla re rnam par sna tshogs ltos//rin chen shing sdong dar gyi bla re bres//nor bu thams cad sgo nas phreng ba 'brul//de nas ye shes 
66a2(phal chen): rgya mtsho rab tu mdzes/spos dri rgyal mtshan rin chen dra ba ste//'od gzer sgron ma'i sprin gis kun tu g.yog rgyan rnams sna tshogs kun nas shin tu bres//de na sangs rgyas rab tu 'phags pa bzhugs//phyogs rnams 
66a3(phal chen): kun sgo sprul pa'i sprin kyi rnams//dbyangs kyis 'jig rten khyab par grags byed cing //'byam klas phyogs kyi sems can rnam 'dul ba//sangs rgyas pho brang 'di na de dag grag//me tog shing dang rin chen 
66a4(phal chen): spong ba spal ba/'phags par phyogs rnams kun tu mngon par mdzes//de la dus gsum zhing rnams thams cad kyang //gzugs brnyan gyur bar de dag rab tu snang //phyogs rnams kun tu rin chen ma bkye pa//

66a6(phal chen): pa bsam yas te/gzhal med khang mang thams cad de la snang //de dag kun na'ang rgyal ba bzhugs shing mdzes//phyogs rnams kun na 'khor rnams ji snyed pa//rgyal po'i pho brang 'dir ni kun zhugs kyang //mtha' 
66a7(phal chen): med dbus med mu'ang de bzhin du//rang byung sangs rgyas pho brang de na bzhugs//de nas byang chub sems dpa' yon tan rin po che bsam gyis mi khyab pa ye shes rgya mtsho dpal gyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus//'khor

68b7(phal chen): kyi mthar phyin pa//bskal pa gzhal yas mang por yongs su sbyangs//de phyir rang byung stobs kyi pha rol phyin//phyogs kyi zhing rnams kun na de phyir lags//thams cad mkhyen nyid nam mkhas mtshungs
