83b4(phal chen): med rnams kyi'o//dus gsum bskal pa gang yod ma lus par//skad cig gcig gis thams cad rab tu ston//sgyu ma lta bu lus med dngos kyang med//de 'dra'i chos nyid chags med blo rtogs so//bzang 
83b5(phal chen): po'i spyod pa mchog la zhugs pa rnams//'gro ba kun la yid du 'ong ba'i lus//rgyal sras de dag chos nyid 'di yin te//'od gzer kun gyi sgo nas dbyangs kyang sgrog/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang 
83b6(phal chen): chub sems dpa' dang dbus med pa de dag/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la rnam par spyod pas rnam par 'phrul ba thob par bya ba'i phyir//smin mtshams kyi mdzod spu nas 'od gzer chen po byang chub 
83b7(phal chen): sems dpa' thams cad ye shes kyi snang bas phyogs rnams par ston pa'i snying po zhes bya ba rab tu bkye'o//'od gzer rin po che thams cad kha dog sgron ma'i sgrin du 'dal ba//sangs rgyas kyi 
83b8(phal chen): zhing thams cad du rab tu snang ba de byung nas/tshul thams cad kyi phyogs 'byam klas pa rab tu snang bar byas te/phyogs thams cad kyi dbyings rgya mtsho rnams rab tu snang bar byas nas sems can rgya mtsho 
84a1(phal chen): @#//rnams rab tu snang bar byas te/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i thams cad rab tu bstan nas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing mang po thams cad rab tu bskyod pa'i rgyan gyis tshul thams cad kyis rab tu 
84a2(phal chen): bskyod nas/sangs rgyas kyi sprin 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed/rdul shin tu phra ba thams cad dang mnyam pa dag tu rab tu spros nas/sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang /mos pa 
84a3(phal chen): ji lta ba rgya mtsho dag tu de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo'i sprin kyi char dus gsum gyi rdul shin tu phra ba dang mnyam pa phab ste/dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rgya 
84a4(phal chen): mtsho rab tu bstan nas/sems can thams can ma lus par 'khor ba thams cad las rab tu gdon par bya ba'i phyir/mos pa ji bzhin du byung ba dang 'thun pa'i sprin gi char rab tu dbab pa dang /de bzhin gshegs 
84a5(phal chen): pa thams cad kyi smon lam ma lus pa'i sprin gyi char rab tu dbab pa dang/'jig rten gyi khams thams chad du byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i 'khor dag tu dga' bar 'phrad par byas nas//bcom 
84a6(phal chen): ldan 'das la g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas te//bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar pad mo chen po chu ba rin po che thams cad las bsgrubs pa/nad rim nor bu'i rgyal po'i mdzod sna tshogs las byas pa/lo ma 
84a7(phal chen): rin po che thams cad kyi rigs kyi char 'bebs pa/ze ba'i phreng ba'i rgyan spos rin po che thams cad la byas pa/snying po'i 'dzam bu chu klung gser gyi tshon kyis rab tu mdzes par bris pa/'od kyi 'khor rnam par 
84a8(phal chen): dag cing 'od gzer gyi dra bas rnam par snang bar byas pa'i sprin dang ldan pa/rgyan thams cad rab tu 'byung ba/sems kyi skad cig la sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba mtha' yas ston pa'i sgra rab tu 'byung ba/
84b1(phal chen): nor bu'i rgyal po mtha' yas pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi sku'i gzugs brnyan gi sprin gyis thams cad du gang bar byed pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang/smon lam thams cad rab tu ston ba'i dbyangs 'byin 
84b2(phal chen): par gyur te byung ngo /pad mo mtha' yas chen po byung ba'i rjes su byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad la pham pa med pa'i dbyangs shes bya ba/byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul 
84b3(phal chen): shin tu phra ba snyed dang lhan cig tu/bcom ldan 'das kyi mdzod spu'i de nyid nas byung ste/bcom ldan 'das la lan du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor byas nas/bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal te/pad mo 
84b4(phal chen): chen po de'i snying po la 'dug go//byang chub sems dpa' 'jigs rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rtul shin tu phra ba snyed po de dag la 'khod do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chosathams cad la pham pa medapa'i dbyings 
84b5(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings rgya che zhing tshad med pas rab tu dga' ba'i shugs shin tu skyes pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyod yul gi rjes su song ba'i ye shes can/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi sku rgya mtsho bsam 
84b6(phal chen): gyis mi khyab pa la rab tu zhugs pa'i mthu' dang/ldan pa/zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi zhabs kyi drung du phyin pa dang /pa spu thams cad las sprul pa'i sprin zad mi shes bas kun tu 
84b7(phal chen): khebs bar byed ba/sems kyi skad cig thams cad la dmigs pa/thams cad chos kyi dbyings thams cad la dus gsum khyab par byed ba'i spyod yul gyis rnam bar spyod pa/phyogs thams cad kyi de bzhin bshegs 
84b8(phal chen): pa'i byin gyis rlabs 'chang pa/ting nge 'dzin thams cad la rab tu 'jog cing phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal ba'i mur thugs bar ting nge 'dzin dang/rnam par thar pa la rnam par spyod pa//sangs rgyas kyi yon tan rgya 
85a1(phal chen): @#//mtsho mtha' yas pa thams cad dang chos kyi dbyings kyi lus dang ldan pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdzu 'phrul dang /rnam par 'phrul ba mtha' dang dbus med pa la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs 
85a2(phal chen): su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas ku ni chos kyi dbyings kyis gsal//'gro ba kun la rgyal ba mngon sum ste/ji ltar mos pa'i sems can rgyas bkang nas/snying po byang chub de la rgyal ba zhugs//zhing 
85a3(phal chen): rnams kun kyi rdul phran mchog snyed kyi//rgyal ba spu rtsa kun la rab bzhugs te//byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor kyis kun nas bskor//kun kyang bzang po'i spyod pa mchog ston to//spu gcig rtse la zhing mang 
85a4(phal chen): rgya mtsho ste/byang chub snying po pad mo'i gdan la bzhugs//chos dbyings rgya chen thams cad kun bkang ste/spu rnams kun la rnam par 'phrul ba ston//zhing rnams re re dag na rab bzhugs shing //
85a5(phal chen): zhing rnams 'byam klas kun tu rgyas mdzad pas/phyogs bcu'i byang chub sems dpa'i sprin kyi rnams//kun kyang snying po byang chub drung du 'dong //zhing rnams kun kyi rdul phran mtshog snyed kyi//byang chub 
85a6(phal chen): sems dpa'i yon tan rgya mtsho'i 'od//sangs rgyas kun kyi 'khor du nye bar 'dong //chos dbyings 'byam klas rtag tu rgyas par 'gengs//zhing rnams thams cad gzugs brnyan rab tu ston//chos 
85a7(phal chen): dbyings rdul rnams ye shes spyod yul can//kun kyang bzang po'i sbyod la rab tu gnas//sangs rgyas kun kyi 'khod du yang dag byung /byang chub sems dpa' ye shes mchog yul can//sangs rgyas 
85a8(phal chen): chos sprin nyan cing rab tu mkhas//sangs rgyas kun gyi 'khor rnams kun na gnas//zhing rnams kun tu bskal pa rgya mtshor spyod//rgya mtsho'i chos la rnam par snang bar spyod//de dag 
85b1(phal chen): rgyal ba'i sa rnams rgya mtshor bzhugs//sangs rgyas chos rnams gzhan dang gzhan byung ba//de dag smon lam rgya mtshor rab tu 'jug/sangs rgyas yon tan legs ma rgya mtsho dang /kun tu bzang po'i 
85b2(phal chen): spyod pa'i de dag shes/sangs rgyas chos kyi dbyings rnams kun bkang zhing //de dag rnams 'phrul rgya chen kun la zhugs//zhing rnams kun kyi rdul phran mang snyed kyi/lus kyi sprin ni rtag par rab tu ston//
85b3(phal chen): sems can kun bkang 'od kyis kun la skyob//bsam pa yid bzhin chos kyi char yang 'bebs//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dpal pad mo rnam par lta ba'i rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin 
85b4(phal chen): gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas ye shes shin tu zab//dus rnams kun tu de 'byung ste/chos dbyings thugs su chud pas na//
85b5(phal chen): 'jig rten mgon zhes rab tu brjod//yang dag pa sangs rgyas chos kyi sku//'dres pa med cing gnas pa med//sems can thams cad yod bzhin du//gzugs kyi skur yang rab tu mthong na//chos 
85b6(phal chen): rnams thams cad mngon sangs rgyas//chos rnams kun la rab tu zhugs//thams cad mkhyen pa thams cad gzigs//zhing rnams kun na rnam par mdzes//kha dog bsam gyis mi khyab par//sems 
85b7(phal chen): can thams cad yid bzhin du//sems can mchog gi 'od rnams dang //de bzhin sku'i sprin kyang mthong //de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dag las//mi zad sangs rgyas sprul pa'i rnams//zhing rnams kun tu rab 
85b8(phal chen): sgrog cing /chos kyi rgya mtsho shin tu ston//spu rnams re re kun nas kyang //phyogs bcur 'od gzer dra ba dag/sangs rgyas gsung ni rab 'byin cing //gdul bka' thams cad rnam par 'dul//sangs rgyas 'od gzer 
86a1(phal chen): @#//sgra rnams nas /sgra dbyangs zab mo rtag tu 'byung //sangs rgyas yon tan rgya mtsho ni//byang chub sems dpa' spyod yul lo//sangs rgyas chos kyi 'khor lo dag/mtha' dang dbung med rab tu 
86a2(phal chen): skor /ye shes zab mo shes bya ba//rgya che phyogs su rab tu ston//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad du//rnam par 'phrul ba gzhan dag gzhan//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun bka' zhing //yang dag sangs rgyas 
86a3(phal chen): ston pa mdzad//sku rnams re re kun nas kyang //zhing rnams kun kyi rdul mnyam par//sangs rgyas sems can mnyam phyung zhing //rnam par 'phrul pa rab tu ston//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi rgya mtsho 
86a4(phal chen): rnam par snang ba'i blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//chos kyi dbyings rnams su kun khyab par//sangs rgyas sku ni rnam 
86a5(phal chen): par mdzes//rgya chen gsung ni rang byung ba//zhing rnams rgya mtshor shin tu sgrog/'jig rten thams cad kun 'jug par//'jig rten mgon po shin tu mdzes//'gro ba kun kyi bsam mkhyen cing //byin kyis 
86a6(phal chen): rlabs ni rab tu ston//sangs rgyas 'jig rten kun la mngon//sangs rgyas               bajig rten kun la mngon //sems can thams cad bsam pa bzhin//thams cad gzigs pa sangs rgyas kyi//rnam par 'phrul ba 
86a7(phal chen): thams cad mthong /mtha' yas 'od mnga' sangs rgyas sprin/chos rnaMs rgya mtsho'i 'brug kyang sgrog/ye shes rnam rgyu'i tshul bzhin du//rgyal ba'i sras rnams de la 'jug/'jug pa rnams la 'byung ba med//'byung ba 
86a8(phal chen): yod pa rnam par bstan//gang la sangs rgyas bzhugs pa'i//chos rnams 'byung ba nam mkha' bzhin//'pho dang gnas pa mi mnga' ste//'on kyang rgyal ba kun na snang //sangs rgyas chos kyi dbyings las byung //de phyir 
86b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas rab tu brjod//yul na bzhugs med rang bzhin med//de la 'byung ba mi mnga' ste//sku dang mtshan kyang de bzhin med//rgyal ba gzugs brnyan 'dra ba'o//'gro ba kun tu chos kyi sprin//bsam pa kun la rab 
86b2(phal chen): tu byung //yang dag sangs rgyas rnams kun tu//sems can rgya mtsho 'dul ba mdzad//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad kyi//snying po byang chub kun na snang //'khor ba'i rgya mtsho thams cad na//zhing rnams 
86b3(phal chen): kun kyis rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas kun kyi sku dag la//yon tan rgya mtsho zad mi shes//sangs rgyas yon tan mtha' yas te//de dag yon tan zad pa med//de nas byang chub sems dpa' spos 'od 'phro'i rgyan 
86b4(phal chen): zla'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi byin kyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smraso//bsam gyis mi khyab sangs rgyas sar//byang chub sems dpa' gang zhugs pa//de 
86b5(phal chen): deng de dag sangs rgyas kyi//sangs rgyas rnams 'phrul thams cad mthong //ye shes lus kyis zhugs pa rnams//zhing kun rdul kyi sbubs su yang //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang //bdag nyid lus kyang der 
86b6(phal chen): mthong ngo //gzugs brnyan zhing rnams thams cad dang //sangs rgyas kun kyi spyan sngar 'byung //kun tu byin kyis rlabs rnams dang //rnam par 'phrul ba rab tu ston//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang //spyod pa gang gis 
86b7(phal chen): rnam sbyangs pa//de dag zhing rnams thams cad du//sangs rgyas kun gyi rnam 'phrul mthong //kun tu lung nyam nyid gyur cing //kun tu lus kyis rab gnas te//ye shes kun kyi rjes song na//sangs rgyas 
86b8(phal chen): spyod yul zhugs pa'o//sangs rgyas ye shes zhugs pa'i rnams//chos kyi dbyings la rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas kun kyi spu dag tu//rgya mtsho'i zhing la rab tu 'jug/sangs rgyas kun 
87a1(phal chen): @#//kyi zhugs dag tu//byin gyi rlabs rnams rab bstan pas//gzhan dang gzhan gyi lus dag dang //ming gis kun na rab tu gnas//byang chub snying por rnam 'phrul dang //byang chub mngon par 
87a2(phal chen): 'tshang rgya zhing //chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dag/skad cig sems kyis ston pa byed//zhing rnams 'byam klas thams cad dang //bskal pa bye ba bsam yas dag/byang chub sems dpa' mnyam bzhag rnams//
87a3(phal chen): skad cig sems kyi rnam par ston//sangs rgyas zhing rnams thams cad na//byang chub sems dpa'i sprul pa rnams//sangs rgyas sku la rnams spyad kyang //mtha' ma rnyed par mi rung ngo//
87a4(phal chen): de nas byang chub sems dpa' seng ge 'od gzer snang bas mthus 'byin pa'i blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin kyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras 
87a5(phal chen): so||sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad kyi//chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu 'khor//chos dbyings tshul rnams thams cad kyang //sangs rgyas sprin kyis mngon tu yog/'jig rten khams rnams rgya mtsho 
87a6(phal chen): dag/phyogs bcu kun na gang yod pa//thams cad kun tu 'khor lo 'khor//de ni smon lam rnam 'phrul ba'o//'khor rnams rgya mtsho rgya che ba//gzhan dang gzhan gyi ming dag gis//zhing rnams 
87a7(phal chen): gnas pa kun tu yang //chos kyi 'khor lo kun tu 'khor//rnam par snang mdzad byin gyi rlabs//zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun tu yang //kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dag/rjod cing zhing rnams kun tu 
87a8(phal chen): 'byung //zhing kun rdul la sku'i rnams//thams cad chos rab rab tu 'bebs//'dres pa med cing 'byung ba med//'jig rten dag na rnam par mdzes//bskal pa bye ba grangs med par//zhing kun rdul phran sbugs 
87b1(phal chen): dag du/rnam par snang ba mdzad pa'i gsung //sngon gyi spyod pa rab tu sgrog//zhing rnams thams cad rdul snyed kyi//'od gzer dra ba phyogs bcu na//sangs rgyas 'od gzer kun sgo nas//byang chub mchog phyir
87b2(phal chen): sems can 'dul/kun tu ma 'dres sku dag gis//chos dbyings yongs su rab gsal te//'gro rnams 'dul ba ji bzhin du//gzugs kyi sku yang rab tu mthong //dus gsum zhing rnams
87b3(phal chen): thams cad kyi/mgon po ji snyed bzhugs pa rnams//de dag kun kyi mtshan kyang sgrog//sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul la ltos//da ltar byung ba dang 'das pa dang //sangs rgyas gang rnams ma byon pa
87b4(phal chen): de dag kun kyi bsam yas pa//chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu grag//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi yon tan gyi mdzod mi zad pa'i dpal gyi gzi brjid mar mes sangs rgyas kyi byin kyis rlabs kyis phyogs
87b5(phal chen): bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//gang gis byang chub sems dpa'i//ye shes tshogs 'di kun non cing //ye shes rtogs pa snyed pa'i rnams//de dag 
87b6(phal chen): 'di'i tshul la zhugs//chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam pa'i dbyangs//zhing rnams kun tu rab tu 'byung //sangs rgyas kun kyi spyod yul te//byang chub sems dpa'i ched du brjod 
87b7(phal chen): skad cig re re'i sengs kun la/chos kyi dbyings su rnam par rgyu//de bzhin nyid la gnas shing rten//chos rnams rgya mtsho kun la zhugs//rgyan gyi pha rol phyin pa rnams//bskal ba bye ba bsam yas su/sangs rgyas 
87b8(phal chen): thams cad sku dag du//zhing rnams kun na gnas shing spyod//rdul rnams re re'i steng nas kyang //chos rnams kun ni rab du 'byung //zhing rnams kun kyang khyab par byed//ye shes chags med spyod yul lo//
88a1(phal chen): @##//sangs rgyas thams cad spyod yul du//las la med par kun bkang zhing //rnam par 'phrul pa'i rnam byod pas//sangs rgyas spyod yul rnams la zhugs//sangs rgyas kun kyi rjes snyegs dbyangs//
88a2(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings ni rab sgrog pa//chos dbyings rjes su shes pa'i rnams//dbyangs kyi rgya mtsho rnams la spyod//bskal ba rgya mtshor ma 'dres par//sgra dang dbyangs rnams shin tu sgrog/dus rnams thams cad shes pas 
88a3(phal chen): gsal/dbyangs kyis de dag sal zhugs//sgra'i shes la rmongs pa med//sems can sgra yang spyod yul te//thams cad mkhyen pa kun gyi yang //rnam par 'phrul ba'i dbyangs shes so//sa nas sar yang phyin gyur cing //
88a4(phal chen): stobs kyi sa la rab gnas te//bskal ba bye bar sbyad pas na//ches nyid 'di la rab tu 'jug/de nas byang chub sems dpa' ye shes kyi 'od gzer shes rab kun nas sgron ma'i blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin 
88a5(phal chen): kyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//gang la sangs rgyas bzhugs pa yi//chos rnams de dag mtshan nyid med//ye shes sa ni thob pa'i rnams//'jig 
88a6(phal chen): rten gtso bo rab tu mthong //shes rab snang ba'i kun 'od can//byang chub sems dpa' mnyam bzhag rnams//sangs rgyas thams cad kun kyi yang //sku'i rnam par 'phrul la zhugs//chos kyi dbyings la rnam spyad cing //
88a7(phal chen): lus kyi dngos la rab 'jug ste//zab mo'i chos rnams rtogs gyur nas//smon lam dag gis yang dag byung //bsod nams rgya mtshos byung gyur cing //ye shes sa la rnam par gnas//chos la rnam spyod khong 
88a8(phal chen): du chud//spyod pa'i mchog la rab tu spyod//sangs rgyas zhing rnams thams cad kyi//sangs rgyas kun kyi drung dag tu//chos kyi dbyings la spyad pa san//rang bzhin lus ni rab tu mthong //zhing rnams 'byam klas 
88b1(phal chen): thams cad du/bskal ba bye ba bsam yas su//thams cad gzigs pa'i chos kyi rnams//rgya mtsho kun la rab tu spyod//bdag mthong bas ni lus dang rdul//gcig tu 'dres pa mngon par byas//ma skyes mtshan 
88b2(phal chen): nyid med pa ste//zhing rnams kun tu'ang rnam par mdzes//'jig rten kun gyi bsam mthun par//sems can kun la mngon sum du//lus kyi sprin ni bstan byas shing //byang chub don du sems can 'dul//sangs rgyas 
88b3(phal chen): thams cad mthu yis na//byang chub sems dpa' brjod pa ste//gang gis mgon po mthong ba yi//rgyal ba'i byin rlabs shin tu zab//mgon po ji snyed gang bzhugs pa'i//byin rlabs mtha' dang dbus med de//chos 
88b4(phal chen): dbyings rab tu gang byas nas//byang chub sems dpa'-i rnams 'tshang rgya//de nas byang chub sems dpa' me tog kun nas 'od 'phrog gtsug phud rab tu 'phyang bas/sangs rgyas kyi byin kyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par ltas 
88b5(phal chen): te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhing rnams kun tu sangs rgyas kyi//gsung gi dkyil 'khor sgra 'byin te//chos kyi dbyings rnams gang byas nas//yon tan sprin ni rab tu rjod//yang dag sangs rgyas chos kyi 
88b6(phal chen): sku/rnam par nam mkha' 'dra ba ste//gzugs kyi sku yang rab tun cing //chos nyid 'di la shin tu 'jug/dga' dang mos pa'i shugs rnams dang //sangs rgyas stobs rnams thams cad la//rab tu gnas shing de dag ni//de bzhin 
88b7(phal chen): gshegs pa'i ye shes 'jug/shes rab chu ngus chos 'di dag/kun nas shes par mi nus te//ye shes mig ni rnam sbyangs shing //'di'i tshul la 'jug par 'gyur//rab tu gnas dang byin rlabs dang //chos kun rnam 
88b8(phal chen): par spyad pa dang /rab tu 'jug dang ldang ba'i//mnyam pa nyid la rab tu zhugs//chos rnams mtha' yas klong gi rnams//chos kun mtshan nyid rnams la ste//kun mkhyen nyid la rab zhugs shing //chos kyi rgya mtsho kun la spyod//
89a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas zhing na kun 'khod cing//thams cad kun na rnam par mdzes//'ongs ba med cing 'phos ba med//sangs rgyas chos nyid de 'dra'o//gzugs brnyan 'dra ba'i sku rnams ni//sems can rgya mtsho'i nang na snang //
89a2(phal chen): mos pa ci 'dra yod pa'i rnams//de bzhin mgon po kun gyis mthong//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang//spyod pa mang po sus sbyangs ba//de dag thams cad ba spu la'ang //rnam 'phrul gzhan dang gzhan dag mthong//lus kyi 
89a3(phal chen): rnams ni re res kyang//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun tu khyab//'khor lo kun na 'khor ba ste//chos kyi 'khor lo bsam mi khyab//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'od gzer dang gzi brjid mi zad pa'i rgyal po'i blo gros gis/
89a4(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas sku ni thams cad nas//'od gzer sprin mang phyogs rnams bcur//kha dog mtha' yas
89a5(phal chen): 'bar ba'i 'od/sems can rnam pa kun la 'phro/sangs rgyas zhing rnams thams cad kyi//snying po byang chub kun na bzhugs//bdud rnams thams cad rab btul te//'khor gyi nang na rnam par mdzes//thams cad rdul 
89a6(phal chen): na'ang ma 'dres par//'od gzer dkyil 'khor rgyas par 'gengs/phyogs rnams gzhan dang gzhan lam du//zhing gi rgyud rnams rab tu ston//zhing gi rnams ni rgya mtsho'i klong //phyogs kyi rnams kyang kun tu gnon//
89a7(phal chen): zhing rnams thang chen mnyam zhing dag/in dra ni lar kun zhes gnas//spyi'u tshugs dag dang snel gzhi dang//pad mo'i dbyings dang khyad khyud dang//dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i khyad par du//gzugs rnams kun
89a8(phal chen): gyis rab tu gnas//chos kyi dbyings rnams gar phyin par//thub pa med par gram bdal ba//de dag kun gyi 'khor rnams su//shin tu bstan pa'i chos 'byung ngo//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dbyings na//'khor ba'i
89b1(phal chen): zhing rnams bsam mi khyab//de dag kun tu chos nyid 'di//'jig rten mgon po rnams kyang ston//tha dad med pa'i chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu 'khor//tshig gi tshul gyi rnam pa yis//chos bstan pa ni kun tu 'byung //
89b2(phal chen): sangs rgyas gsung gi dkyil 'khor ni//tshul rnams bsam cing rab tu rjod//dad pa rgya mtsho'i tshul dag gis//mi zad tshul rnams rab tu ston//sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun na yang //rgyal ba rnams ni mig yor 'dra//
89b3(phal chen): snying po byang chub rnams na snang //'byung ba dag kyang yod pa min//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dbyings su 'od 'phro ba'i rnam par snang ba'i 'byung ba'i blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam 
89b4(phal chen): par bltas te/rnam par de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//blta zhing blta zhing sangs rgyas rnams//sku gzugs bsam gyis mi khyab ste//dga' ba rgya mtsho kun kyang 'byung //chos kyi shugs ni rab tu 'phel//mtshan 
89b5(phal chen): rab kun nas sangs rgyas rnams//phyogs bcu kun tu rab tu 'byung //rdul rnams kun tu 'jug pa yis//sangs rgyas sprin kyang de bzhin bstan//skad cig skad cig sems kun la//sangs rgyas 'od ni dpal gyis 'bar//rnam 
89b6(phal chen): 'phrul gzhan dang gzhan dag gis//zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun na snang //byang chub sems dpa' shes rab che//rgya mtsho'i chos la phyung ba rnams//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin rlabs pa//'di'i yul la rnam par spyod//kun tu bzang 
89b7(phal chen): po'i smon lam la//gang rnams shin tu gnas gyur pa//de dang de rnams zhing dag du//sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul kun la lta//ye shes zab mo rnams gnon cing //chos kyi dbyings la gzhol ba ni//mos pa rgya mtsho shes pa ste 
89b8(phal chen): smon lam kun gyis byung ba'o//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku dang gsung //chags pa med la rab tu zhugs//spyod pa gzhan dang gzhan dag gi//spyod yul kun kyang khong du chud//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku gnas shing //ye shes 
90a1(phal chen): @#//spyod yul gyur pa'i rnams//chos kyi dbyings la rab brtson pa//rgyal ba rnams kyis gnon to//skad cig sems kyis zhing gi rnams//sangs rgyas zhing gi rdul mnyam pa//de dag chos nyid ji bzhin du//rdul 
90a2(phal chen): cig steng du rnam par spyod//zhing rnams ji snyed thams cad ni/zhing gi rnam 'phrul gang yod pa//chos nyid 'di la zhugs pa'i rnams//zhing cig tu ni sdud pa byed//de nas byang chub sems dpa' stobs dang brtson 'grus chags 
90a3(phal chen): pa med pa'i blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin kyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par ltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhing rnams rgya mtsho thams cad du//de dag gsung ni zab mo byung /dbyangs kyi yan 
90a4(phal chen): lag kun rgyas pa//chos kyi sprin kyi char 'bebs so//brjod pa rgya mtsho thams cad kyis//sems can kun kyi skad dag tu//sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun tu yang //chos rnams bstan pa rab tu 'byung //zhing rnams 
90a5(phal chen): kun tu ma lus par//sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul mthong zhing //sangs rgyas gsung rnams thos pa dang //byang chub mchog phyir kun kyang 'jug/rdul rnams re re'i steng nas kyang //chos kyi dbyings kyi phyogs tshul 
90a6(phal chen): gyis/sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun bkang zhing //sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul ston pa byed//chos kyi sku ni tha dad min/nam mkha' bzhin te chags pa med//gzugs kyi skur na rnam dmigs pa//mig yor snang ba'i mtshan 
90a7(phal chen): nyid do//mig yor yul la mi gnas ste//nam mkha' bzhin du dngos po med//mnyam pa nyid 'di rtogs gyur nas//ye shes rgya mtsho mthong bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas sku ni ma skyes te/gzung ba med cing byung ba med//nam 
90a8(phal chen): mkha' mnyam pa nyid dang mtshungs//gzung du med la 'di bstan to//zhing rnams kun kyis sangs rgyas rnams//de dag spu gcig rtse la tshogs//ye shes mig dang ldan pa yis//spu rnams kun tu rnam 'phrul mthong //rnam 
90b1(phal chen): par snang ba smon lam ste/chos kyi dbyings ni kun dkang zhing//bla med chos kyi 'khor lo dag/zhing rnams kun na rtag tu 'khor/ji ste bskal pa bsam yas par//spu rtse gcig tu rnam sprul pa//sangs rgyas kun kyis
90b2(phal chen): bshad mdzad kyang//de'i mtha' ni rnyed par bka' ji ltar gling bzhi pa 'dir de bzhin gshegs pas rnam par sprul pa'i snying po byang chub tu phyogs bcu nas byang chub sems dpa' rnams 'dus par gyur pa de bzhin du /'jig
90b3(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i gling pa thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa re rer yang de bzhin gshegs pas rnal bar sprul pa'i snying po byang chung dag tu byang chub sems dpa' phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho bye ba'i rdul
90b4(phal chen): shin tu phra ba snyed kun nas 'dus par gyur to//'di na ji lta ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi snying po byang chub de bzhin gshegs pas rnams par sprul pa dag tu//kun nas 'dus pa'i byang chub sems dpa'
90b5(phal chen): de dag kyang /chos nyid 'di 'khor na rab tu ston to//de bzhin gshegs pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po seng ge'i khri pad
90b6(phal chen): mo'i snying po can de nyid la 'dug ste//de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi mthus ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku'i snying po zhes bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyam pa
90b7(phal chen): nyid kyi rjes su song ba'i rang bzhin chos kyi dbyings kyi gzugs brnyan rnam par brtags pa'i tshogs//nam mkha'i dbyings na rngas pa med cing rgya che pa/khyab par byed pa'i
90b8(phal chen): rang bzhin chos kyi dbyings rgya mtsho'i glong mnyam pa nyid kyi rjes su song zhing shin tu bdal tshogs/chos thams cad kyi sgo'i ting nge 'dzin gyi dkyil 'khor yang dag par 'byung bas rgyas par 'gengs pa//phyogs thams cad du yang dag
91a1(phal chen): @#//par zhugs pa'i chos kyi dbyings kyi lus kyis kun nas yog par byas pa'i rang bzhin gyi snying po can dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i ye shes snang ba//rgya mtsho'i glong phyogs thams cad du sgo kun 
91a2(phal chen): nas tshul rgya mtsho rnam par rig par bya bas mngon tu yang dag par ston pa//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi stobs dang rnam par thar pas byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes kyi lus rgyas par 'gengs pa'i snying po can//zhing 
91a3(phal chen): 'byam klas pa thams cad ma lus par chos kyi dbyings su 'jug pas rgyas par 'gengs pa'i ye shes dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan rgya mtsho thams cad yongs su grub par byed pa//bskal ba thams cad 
91a4(phal chen): du smon lam rgya mtshos rnam par snang bar byed pa'i cod pan//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo ston cing brjod pa'i byin gyi rlabs la mnyam par gzhag par gyur to//ji ltar byang chub sems 
91a5(phal chen): dpa' kun tu bzang pos/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs kyi drung du byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku'i snying po zhes bya ba 'di la mnyam par 
91a6(phal chen): bzhag pa de bzhin du/'jig rten gyi khams 'byam klas pa chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas gtugs pa/phyogs rgya mtsho thams cad kyi khongs su gtogs pa//dus gsum thams cad 
91a7(phal chen): na yod pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byung ba'i khams chos kyi dbyings rgya chen pos/ne rna pa'i ris su gtogs pa/shin tu phra zhing bsgribs pa med pa mang ba dang /phyogs rgya chen po rgya mtsho thams cad snang ba//'jig rten 
91a8(phal chen): gyi khams rgya mtsho'i ris su gtogs pas rnam par gnon pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyan gyis rnam par gzigs pa/sangs rgyas kyi stobs thogs pa med pas chud pa/chos kyi dbyings dang sangs rgyas kyi 
91b1(phal chen): sku thams cad kyi gzugs brnyan rab tu snang ba//chos kyi dbyings kyi rdul ma lus pa kun tu gtogs pa//chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad du//rgyas par gang ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi 
91b2(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba dang mnyam pa dag tu/rdul phra mo re re la'ang //zhing 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed la rab tu zhugs nas zhing re re zhing yang de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi khams kyi 
91b3(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed las rab tu zhugs te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i re re'i zhabs kyi drung du yang lus 'jig rten gyi khams rgya che'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyedakyi//ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas thams cad de bzhin gshegs 
91b4(phal chen): pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku'i snying po zhes bya ba 'di la mnyam par bzhag go/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos//byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas 
91b5(phal chen): thams cad de bzhin gshegasapa rnam par snang mdzad kyis sku'i snying po zhes bya ba de la mnyam par gzhag ba'i rdas la/de'i tshe phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad nas/de bzhin gshegs pa 
91b6(phal chen): thams cad kyis zhal stan to//yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis 'di skad ces kyang bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu khyod de ltar byang chub sems dpa'i ting 'dzin sangs rgyas 
91b7(phal chen): thams chad/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi ska'i snying po 'di la mnyam par gzhog pa la legs so legs so//kye rgyal ba'i bu khyod la phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa 'di dag thams cad kyang //
91b8(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam pa snang mdzad 'di'i sngon gyi smon lam gyi byin gyi rlabs dang /khyod kyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi smon lam dang /spong pa yongs su dag pa'i yang mthus 'di lta ste//
92a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho thams cad kyi rnam par dbye ba dang /chos thams cad kyi tshul gyis phyogs rgya mtsho ma lus par snang bar bya ba dang /
92a2(phal chen): sems can kyi khams thams cad kyi kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par bya ba                ba dang /chags pa med par 'jig rten gyi khams 'jam klas pa thams cad yongs su gnan pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi yul thams 
92a3(phal chen): cad la chags pa med par 'jug par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi 'khor thams cad sgo kun nas rab tu brjod pa dang /chos tshul gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la zhugs pas/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yongs su bzung ba dang /
92a4(phal chen): chos kyi snang ba thams cad kyi tshul rnam par lta ba dang /sems can gyi dbang po rgya mtsho thams cad yongs su shes par bya ba dang /de bzhin gsheg pa thams cad kyi gsung gi yan lag rgya mtsho'i dbyings yongs su bzung 
92a5(phal chen): ba'i phyir byin gyis rlob pa mdzad do//de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po la thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi stobs la rab tu 'jug pa'i ye shes 
92a6(phal chen): kyang nye bar bsgrubs so//chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa la rab tu 'jug pa'i ye shas kyang nye bar bsgrubaso//dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad yang dag par 'byung ba'i ye shes kyang nye 
92a7(phal chen): bar bsgrubs so//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad 'jig pa dang /'du ba'i ye shes kyang nye bar bsgrubs so/chos kyi dbyings 'jam klas pa mtha' dang dbus med pa'i ye shes kyang nye bar bsgrabs so//
92a8(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa zab mo tha mi dad pa dang //ting nge 'dzin thams cad la rnam par gnas pa'i ye shes kyang nye bar pasgrubs so//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi dbang po rgya mtsho la rab tu 'jug pa'-i 
92b1(phal chen): ye shes kyang nye bar bsgrubs so//sems can thams cad kyi skad rgya mtsho dang chos kyi 'khor lo nges pa'i tshig gi ye shes kyang nye bar bsgrub so//'jig rten gyi khams mang po thams cad kyi rjes su song zhing 
92b2(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings thams cad du lus yang dag par gzhol ba'i sems kyang nye bar bsgrubs so//sangs rgyas kyi gsung gi yan lag dang sgra dang dbyangs kyang nye bar bsgrubs so //ji ltar 'jig rten gyi khams
92b3(phal chen): 'di'i bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs kyi drung na 'dug pa la nye bar bsgrubs pa de bzhin du /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad rgyas par bkang ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag dang rdul shin tu phra ba'i thongs na
92b4(phal chen): lus 'khod pa thams cad la yang nye bar bsgrubs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar ting nge 'dzin de nyid kyis nyid nyid thob pa'i phyir ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis/sprul pa'i sangs rgyas bcom
92b5(phal chen): ldan 'das de dag gis/phyag g.yas pa mtshan dang dper byad bzang pos rnam par mdzes par brgyan pa//'od gzer gyi dra ba'i me tog sna tshogs shin tu rgyas pa//'od kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par dag pa'i sprin 'gyed pa//rin
92b6(phal chen): po che 'od 'phro ba thams cad kyi sprin kyis rnam par gsal bar gyur pa/phyogs thams cad du spros 'od 'phro ba'i sprin kyi 'od kyis rnam par snang ba/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsung gi yan lag gi dkyil 'khor gyi rjes su
92b7(phal chen): sgra 'byin pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad gyi rnam par 'phrul ba dang /khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba yang dag par ston pa//byang chub sems dpa' 'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba rnams kyi kun tu bzang po'i smon
92b8(phal chen): lam gyi dkyil 'khor rab tu snang ba rnam par ston pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo'i snang ba yang dag par ston ba/dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi gzugs brnyan 'byung ba
93a1(phal chen): @/rnams brkyang ste/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyi bor bzhag go/'dir ji lta ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad dang rdul shin tu phra ba'i khongs na yod pa thams cad du yang /de bzhin gshegs 
93a2(phal chen): pa'i zhabs kyi drung ngag du byang chub sems dpa' kunatu bzang po'i spyi bo la phyag rgya mtsho'i sprin rnams rab tu bzhag go/byang chub sems dpa' kun du bzang po la sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis//reg ma thag tu donas 
93a3(phal chen): de'i mod la ting nge 'dzin de las ldang ba'i sgo rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed/dus gsum thams cad du/sems kyi skad cig re re la//ting nge 'dzin las ldang ba'i ye shes ma 'dres pa la mkhas 
93a4(phal chen): pa dang ting nge 'dzin las ldang ba'ibyin kyis rlabs kyi sgo chos kyi dbyings dang /dus gsum mnyam pa'i thams cad kyi sgo dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'das ba ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba thams cad kyi sgo dang /sems can 
93a5(phal chen): ma lus pa'i gnas thams cad dang /mnyam zhing sems can thams cad kyi sems rgya mtsho la 'jug pa'i sgo dang /sems can thams cad kyi lus dang /ming gi 'khor lo las ldang ba'i sgo dang /phyogs mtshams thams cad 
93a6(phal chen): du chos kyi dbyings rnam par 'byed pa'i sgo dang /rdul shin tu phra ba thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi sku'i sprin mtha' dang dbus med par 'byam klas pa'i sgo dang /chos kyi tshul rgya mtsho thams cad rab tu bstan pa'i sgo dag 
93a7(phal chen): nas langs par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po ting nge 'dzin de las langs ma thag tu/de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' de dag kyang so so re re zhing ting nge 'dzin rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rten gyi khams 
93a8(phal chen): rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed la rab tu zhugs par gyur to//gzungs rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rtenagyi khams rgya mtsho rdul shin tu phra ba snyedal yang rab tu zhugs par gyur te//chos kyi tshul rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rten
93b1(phal chen): gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed la yang rab tu zhugs par gyur to//tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa'i chu la rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shan tu phra ba snyed la yang rang tu zhugs par gyur to//
93b2(phal chen): spyod pa rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rtenagyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shen tu phra ba snyed la rab tu zhugs par gyur te//chos kyi dbyings kyi phyogs su rnam par snang zhing de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes snang ba yon tan gyi 
93b3(phal chen): snying po can rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rtenagi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shing tu phra ba snyed la yang rabatu zhugs par gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs dang ye shes ma 'dres pa'i tshul rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rtan gyi khams 
93b4(phal chen): rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phrab snyed la yang rab tu zhus par zhugs so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi ba spu'i rtse mo thams cad la zhing gzhan dang gzhan rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rton gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdu shin tu phra ba
93b5(phal chen): snyed la yang rab tu zhugs par gyur to//byang chub sems dpa' re re zhing dga' ldan gyi gnas nas 'pho ba dang 'byung ba dang /byang chub tu rnam par 'tshang rgya ba dang /mya ngan las 'das pa bstan pa rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rted
93b6(phal chen): gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shan tu phra ba snyed la yang rang tu zhugs par gyur to/'dir ji lta ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i thams cad na yang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor rgya mtsho rnams dang/
93b7(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba thams cad kyi khongs na yod pa kun tu yang byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho rnams rab tu zhugs par gyur te//sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i ting nge 'dzin gyi chos nyid
93b8(phal chen): rab tu thob pas/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho thams cad ma lus par bag langs pa tsam du g.yos te//rin po che thams cad kyis rab tu brgyan par gnas shing chos rgya mtsho thams cad kyi sgra yang shin
94a1(phal chen): @#//tu grag par 'gyur te//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor rgya mtsho thams cad du yang /nor bu'i rgyal po chen po bcu'i sprin kyi char mngon par bab par gyur to bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste nor bu'i rgyal po mtshan dang 
94a2(phal chen): ldan pa'i gser dpal gyi rgyal mtshan gyi dpal gyi sprin gyi char mngon par bab po//'di lta ste nor bu'i rgyal bo 'od kyis rnam par gsal bar byed pa'i sprin kyi char mngon pa bab bo//'di lta ste nor bu'i rkyal po 
94a3(phal chen): phyogs bcur rin po che'i 'khor lo rab tu 'grems pa'i sprin kyi char mngon bar bab bo//'di lta ste norabu'i rgyal po byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs brnyan rin po che'i sprin gyi char mngon par pabab bo//'di lta ste nor ba'i 
94a4(phal chen): rgyal po sangs rgyas thams cad kyi mtshan gyi 'khams rab tu sgrog pa'i sprin gyi char mngon bar bab bo/'di lta ste nor bu'i rgyalapo zhing rnam par snang bar byed cing snying po byang chub tu dpal rab tu 'bar ba'i sprin kyi 
94a5(phal chen): char mngon par bab bo//'di lta ste nod bu'i rgyal po phyogs bcur snang ba'i sprul pa 'gyed ba'i sprin gyi char mngon pa bab bo/'di lta ste norabu'i rgyal bo byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi yon tan gyi 'khor lo 
94a6(phal chen): dang mthu rab tu grag par byed pa'i sprin gyi char mngonapar bab bo//'di lta ste/nor bu'i rgyal po nyi ma'i dpal gyi gzi brjid kyi sprin gyi char mngon parabab bo//'di lta ste norbu'i rgyal po rol mo'i sgra yid du 'ong bas 
94a7(phal chen): phyogs thams cad khyab par byad pa'i sprin gyi char mngon parang bab ste/nor bu'i rgyal bo'i sprin gyi char bcu po 'di dag mngon par bab bar gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ba spu'i sgo thams cad dang 'od 
94a8(phal chen): gzer gyi sgo thams cad nas kyang tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag byung bar gyur to//zhing rnams 'byam klas kun na kun tu bzang po/dbang po yid du 'ong la rab tu 'jug pa mthong //kun tu rnam phrul rnam par 
94b1(phal chen): 'di 'dra ston pa byed//ting 'dzin yul la 'jug pa mtha' dang dbus med do//lus rnams thams cad nas kyang kun tu bzang po do//sprul pa rab tu 'gyed cing chos dbyings rtag par 'gengs//ting 'dzin kun gyi tshul 
94b2(phal chen): dang ming dang rnam 'phrul ba//thams cad dbyangs kyis chags pa med par rgya che sgrog/ting 'dzin thams cad gzhan dang gzhan gyi rnam kun gyis/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad zhabs kyi drung dag tu//rgyal ba kun 
94b3(phal chen): gyi spyod yul yangs pa gang byas te//zhing rnams kun tu rnam par 'phrul ba mang po ston//zhing kun rgyal ba kun gyi zhabs drung ji bzhin du//de ltar zhing kun shin tu phra ba'i rdul phran la//ting 'dzin rnam par 
94b4(phal chen): 'phrul ba thams cad ma lus ston//rnam par snang mdzad smon lam dbyings la rab tu zhugs//kun tu bzang po'i lus ni nam mkha'i mtshan nyid do//thams cad zhing la mi gnas de bzhin nyid la gnas//lus kyi sprin 
94b5(phal chen): ni 'gro ba kun dang mnyam par kun bkye zhing //sems can kun la bsam pa bzhin du de dag ston//sangs rgyas rnams kun zhing rnams gang yod pa//mtha' dang dbus med kun la rab tu zhugs pa rnams//
94b6(phal chen): kun tu bzang po'i smon lam gnas pa dag la bstan//rnam par 'phrul pa 'di 'dra dam pa tshad kyang med//'khor rnams rgya mtsho rdul mtha' dbus myed pa//tshad myed thams cad lus kyi nang du kun tshung 
94b7(phal chen): cing /dbyangs la zhing mang dra bar rnam 'tshang rgya ba ston//skad cig sems la bskal pa mang po de bzhin ston//zhing rnams thams cad 'byam klas pa na rnam grags shing //rnam par 'phrul ba dam pa ston par 
94b8(phal chen): rab tu snang //phyogs rnams kun gyi zhing gi dbyangs rnams ma lus spyod//kun tu bzang po de la rnam lta kun kyang mthong //rgyal ba kun gyi yon tan ye shes stobs la zhugs//ting 'dzin sa ni tshul 
95a1(phal chen): @#//rnams kun kyis rab tu mnan//chos rnams 'jam klas gzhan dang gzhan la rab tu zhugs//rang gi byang chub spyod pas sngan cad spyad pa ston//de ltar zhing rnams kun tu bsam yas rnam 'phrul ba//phyogs 
95a2(phal chen): rnams rgya che kun tu bkang ste rab tu bstan//ting 'dzin rnams la 'jug pa rab tu bstan pa'i phyir//rgyal ba'i 'od gzer sprin las yon tan leg sgra 'byung //de nas byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor yod pa thams cad 
95a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi mthus thal mo sbyar te//byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po la lta zhing dbyangs kyi nga ro gcig tu de'i tshe rigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba thams cad chos las skyes te/smon lam kun 
95a4(phal chen): kyis sangs rgyas rab tu byung //de bzhin nyid mnyam nam mkha'i snying po ste//chos kyi sku yis rnam par rab tu mdzes//zhing rnams 'byam klas kun la bzhugs med kyang /kun tu bzang po'i 'khor rnams kun na 
95a5(phal chen): snang /phyogs bcu kun tu yon tan grags pas khyag/ye shes rgya mtsho rab tu snang bar mnga'//rgyal ba thams cad sku thob phyir spyod pa//phyogs bcu'i yon tan rgya mtsho yod pa'i rnams//rdul spubs kun tu 
95a6(phal chen): de dag chud byas shing //shing rnams kun tu rnam par rab tu mdzes//zhing rnams kun gyi rgyal ba'i zhabs drung na//rgyal sras ting nge 'dzin dang spyod pa dang //chos rnams kun la spyod cing gnas pa'i rnams//rdul dang mnyam pa'i 
95a7(phal chen): bskal bar kun kyang snang //chos kyi dbyings rnams lus kyis khyab byas shing //zhing rnams 'byam klas kan la shin tu zhugs//sems can rgya mtsho'i don du de spyod cing //rdul phran kun la chos kyi dbyings su zhugs//
95a8(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings la da ltar zhugs nas kyang //rdul phran kun la mi zad tha mi dad//nam mkha'i dbyings ni lus kyis gang byas shing //rgyal ba'i chos rnams yangs pa rab tu ston//yon tan rgya mtsho 'od kun gzhal du 
95b1(phal chen): med/rnam yangs stobs chen 'od gzer yon tan sprin/'gro ba rgya mtsho'i don tu spyod byed cing //rgyal ba'i spyod lam gzhan du med pa ston//gang gi don du bskal ba rgya mtsho dag/mtha' yas bar ni bzang po'i spyod spyad pa//
95b2(phal chen): de dag chos kyi dbyings rnams gang byas nas//thams cad chos kyi sprin las rab tu brjod//zhing rnams ji ltar 'byung bar 'gyur ba dang //stobs bcu rnams kyang ji ltar 'byung 'gyur dang //sems can rgya mtsho ji ltar 'byung 'gyur 
95b3(phal chen): ba/sems can rnams la sgra dbyangs gtong zhing ston//'khor rnams yangs pa 'di dag thams cad kyang //kun tu bzang po khyod la mngon sum gnas//sangs rgyas kun gyis gnang bar bgyi ba yin//chos kyi 'khor lo de ring 
95b4(phal chen): khyod kyis skor////kun tu bzang po'i ting nge 'dzin dang rnam par 'phrul ba rab tu byung ba zhes bya ba ste//le'u gsum pa//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos//sangs rgyas kyi byin kyis rlabs kyis/
95b5(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad la rnam par bltas//sems can gyi khams rgya mtsho la rnam par bltas chos kyi dbyangs rgya mtsho'i rnam par bltas/sems can thams cad kyi las rgya mtsho la rnam par blta//sems 
95b6(phal chen): can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang dbang po rgya mtsho la rnam par blta sa//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos kyi rgya mtsho la rnam par blta'//dus rgya mtsho la rnam par blta'o//sangs rgyas thams cad kyang yang dag par 'byung ba'i 
95b7(phal chen): smon lam rgya mtsho la rnam par blta'i//sangs rgyas thams cad rnam par 'phrul ba rgya mtsho la rnam par bltar nas//byang chub sems dpa' 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras 
95b8(phal chen): dag sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams thams chad 'du zhing 'jigs pa yongs su dag pa 'byung ba'i ye shes ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sems can 
96a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad kyi las 'byung ba rgya mtsho mkhyen pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul rgya mtsho thams cad la rnam par gnas pa'i ye shes ni bsam gyis 
96a2(phal chen): mi khyab po//sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rgya mtsho thams cad mtha' dang dbus med par bstan pa'i ye shes ni bsam gyi mi khyab po//sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi sems 
96a3(phal chen): can thams cad kyi las rgya mtsho rnam par dgod pa'i ye shes ni bsam gyin mi khyab po//sangs rgyas gcom ldan 'das rnams kyis bsam pa dang /dad pa dang dbang po rgya mtsho la 'jug pa'i ye shes ni bsam gyis
96a4(phal chen): kyi khyab po/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi thugs kyi skad cig gcig la dus thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid thugs su chud pa'i ye shes ni bsam gis spyi khyab po//sangs rgyas btsom ldan 'das rnams
96a5(phal chen): kyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul ba rgya mtsho yang dag par bstan pa'i ye shes bsom gyis spyi khyab po//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba'i ye shes ni bsam
96a6(phal chen): gyis mi khyab po//sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams kyirnam par bgod pa rab tu bstan pa rgya mtsho ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//sangs sargyas bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi sangs rgyas rnams kyi sangs rgyas sku rab tu
96a7(phal chen): bstan pa yongs su dag pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi yon tan rgya mtsho mtha' dang dbus med pas rnam par snang ba ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan
96a8(phal chen): rnams kyi mtshan dang dpe byad bzang pos yongs su dag pa ni bas gyis mi khyab po//sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das rnam kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor yongs su dag pa mtha' yas pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab
96a9(phal chen): 'o//sangs rgyas
96b1(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi 'od gzer rgya mtsho kha dog sna tshogs kyi sprin rab tu dkye ba mdzad pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sangs rgyas kyi dpal rin po che 'phro ba'i rgya mtsho rnam par rgyu ba 
96b2(phal chen): ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi gsung rang dbyangs rgya mtsho rab tu 'grub pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi cho 'phrul rnam pa gsum 
96b3(phal chen): yongs su bstan pas sems can rgya mtsho yongs su smin par mdzad pa dang /gdul ba mdzad pa ni bsam gyi sa mi khyab bo//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams ki gdul ba rgya mtsho thams cad la brtson pa don yod pa 
96b4(phal chen): ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyis la rab tu zhugs pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa bo//sangs rgyas bcom 
96b5(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la 'jug pa ni bsam 
96b6(phal chen): gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi byin kyi rlabs kyi mthu ni bsam gyis mi khyab po //sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
96b7(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyasakyi deng nge 'dzin tha mi dad par spyod pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dos rnams kyi de bzhin gsheg pa'i rdzu 'phrul dang rnam par 'phrul ba ni bsam 
96b8(phal chen): gyis mi khyab po//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'da'as rnamasakyi mda' chen po'i ye shes rnam par dag pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgya kyi chos mi 'phrogs pa na bsam gyis mi khyab ba 
97a1(phal chen): @#//de dag thams cad sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis brlabs kyis byin du rlabs pas//sems can thams cad sangs rgyas kyi ye shes rgya mtsho la gzung ba dang byang chub sems dpA 
97a2(phal chen): thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan rgya mtsho la rab tu dgod pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba thams cad kyis rnam par rgyan pa dang bskal pa rgya mtsho 
97a3(phal chen): thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdung rjes su gsal ba dang /'jig rten gyi kham rgya mtsho thams cad du chos kyi rang bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor rab tu brjod pa dang /sems can thams cad kyi dad pa rgya mtsho tha dad pa j-i 
97a4(phal chen): lta ba bzhin tu rab tu bstan pa dang sems can thams cad kyi dbang po rgya mtsho la sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi tshul kun tu snang ba yang dag par bskyed pa dang /sems can tha mas cad kyi bsam pa rgya mtsho'i sgrib pa'i ri thams cad 
97a5(phal chen): rnam par gzhom pa dang /sems can tham sa cad kyi sems dang spyod pa rgya mtsho la 'byung ba'i lam rnam par spyad pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho thams cad byang chub sems dpa'i spyod lam rgya mtsho la rab tu 
97a6(phal chen): dgong ba'i phyir yang dag par bstan par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho rnams de bas kyang shin tu dpa' bar bya ba dang /chos la dga' ba'i shugs thams 
97a7(phal chen): cad rnam par 'phrel bar bya ba dang /mos pa'i tshul yangs pa rgya mtsho yang dag par 'byung bar bya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin dang snying po sgo kun dang ldan pa rnam par dag par bya ba dang /kun tu bzang po'i 
97a8(phal chen): smon lam rgya mtsho la rab tu dgod par bya ba dang /ye shes gyi mig des bsam gyi rjes su song ba yongs su spyad pa dang /'jig rten thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi yun thams cad rab tu brjod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa 
97b1(phal chen): chos kyi tshul snang ba'i 'od chos kyi dbyings rgya chen po dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la dga' ba'i shugs rgya mtsho rnam par 'phel bar bya ba'i phyir/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so////
97b2(phal chen): sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo////bam pa bdun pa////yon tan rgya mtsho rnams kyi ye shes zab//mtha' dang dbus med des ni zhing rnams khyab//sems can 
97b3(phal chen): bsam la sbyor pa ji bzhin snang //rnam par snang mdzad chos kyi 'khor lo'o//bsam gyis mi khyab rgya mtsho'i zhing yod pa/bskal ba rgya mtshor rgyal bas rnam sbyangs pa//sems can rgya mtsho yongs su smin bya'i 
97b4(phal chen): phyir//sems can nad na rgyal ba rnam par mdzes//sems can rgya mtsho 'byung ba bsam mi khyab//sangs rgyas yul la 'jug pa shin tu zab//srid pa'i gnas can dad pa chung pa yis//sangs rgyas byang chub 
97b5(phal chen): shes par mi nus so//gzhan du gang rnams shin tu sems bstan cing //dge ba'i bshes kyis yongs su bzung ba dang //sangs rgyas kun gyi byin gyis brlabs pa dag/sangs rgyas ye shes 'di la rab tu 'jug/
97b6(phal chen): gang rnams sems dag g.yo med sgyu med cing //snying rje'i bsam can khod nas byams dga' dang //dad pa che zhing rlabs chen de dag ni//'di dag thos shing dga' ba thob par 'gyur//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam sa 
97b7(phal chen): dag dang //chos dbyings nam mkha'i dbyings su khyab pa la//gnas shing spyad pa rab tu sus spyod pa//de dag rgyal ba'i spyod yul de shes so//gang gis rgyal ba'i rnam 'phrul 'di mthong ba'i//byang chub sems dpa' 
97b8(phal chen): thob bya legs pa thob//gzhan gyi yul gis shes par mi nus te//'di ni kun tu bzang pos rtogs pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa kun kyis byin rlabs shing //'gro ba mtha' dang dbus med 'di dag na//kun tu chos kyi 
98a1(phal chen): @#//'khor lo rab tu 'khor//rnam par snang mdzad yul dang mthu yin no//bdag gi lus su zhing rnams thams cad kyang //rab tu zhugs shing de zhugs zhing rnams kun//de dag spu rtse kun tu lta bar gos 
98a2(phal chen): rgyal ba'i spyod yul shin tu bshad par bya//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam spyod pa rnams//mtha' dang dbus med mngon par bsgrub pa dang //kun tu mig gi yul dang lus ldan pa//de dag mgon po'i spyod yul 
98a3(phal chen): 'di nyid te//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag bcu po 'di dag ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho shin tu bstan pa ste//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang da ltar byung ba rnams kyis gsungs so//gsung ngo gsung 
98a4(phal chen): bar 'gyur ro//bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste 'jig rten gyi khams yongs su 'byung ba'i tshogs kyi rgyu rkyen shin tu bstan pa dang /'di lta ste 'jig rten kyi khams rgya mtsho gnas pa shin tu bstan pa dang /'di lta ste/'jig 
98a5(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i dbyings dang rnam par shin tu bstan pa dang //'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i lhun shin tu bstan pa dang 'di lta ste 'jig rten gyi 
98a6(phal chen): khams rgya mtsho yongs su dag par bya ba'i tshul shin tu bstan pa dang //'di lta ste 'jig rten dang /khams rgya mtsho dag tu sangs rgyas 'byung ba shin tu bstan pa dang 'dir lta ste 'jig rten gyi 
98a7(phal chen): kham rgya mtsho'i bskal pa'i byin gyi brlabs shin tu bstan pa dang /
98b1(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang da ltar byung ba rnams kyis gsungs so//gsung ngo gsung bar 'gyur ro/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad ni rgyu dang rkyen la dmigs 
98b2(phal chen): pa'i sgo 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed phrag bcus byung ngo//byung ngo byung bar 'gyur ro//bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin kyi rlabs dang /chos nyid rab tu thob pa dang /sems 
98b3(phal chen): can rnams kyi las kyi tshogs dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yang dag par sgrub pa'i chos nyid dang /sems can rnams kyis kyang byang chub sems dpa' dag dang /bsod nams kyi rtsa ba skal 
98b4(phal chen): pa 'thun pa bsags pa dang /byang chub sems dba' rnams kyi zhing yongs su dag pa'i smon lam gyi khang par dang /byang chub sems dang rnams kyi spyod pas rnam par bsgrubs pa'i smon lam gyis mngon par sgrubs 
98b5(phal chen): pa dang /byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi mos pa'i dbang rnam par dag pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba 'jig rten las 'das pa dang /rgyu 'thun pas mngon par 'tshang rgya zhing sangs rgyas 
98b6(phal chen): kyi rnam par 'phrul ba khyu mchog gi mthu byung ba dang /byang chub sems dma' kun tu bzang po rnams kyi smon lam gyis rnam par 'phrul ba'i sgo'i//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de ltar rgyu dang rkyen la dmigs pa'i sgo bcu po 'di 
98b7(phal chen): dag las stsogs pa rgyu dang rkyen la dmigs pa/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho thams cad byung ngo//'byung ngo 'byung bar 'gyur ro//de nas byang chub sems 
98b8(phal chen): dpa' kun tu bzang pos 'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'tsho 'byung ba shin tu bstan pa nyid gsal bar brjod pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin kyis rlabs kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas ye shes 
99a1(phal chen): @#//yul ni bsam yas pas/byin rlabs rnam 'phrul de dag 'di lta bu/zhing rnams rgya mtsho bstan pa mtha' dbus med//rnam par snang mdzad chos kyis yongs su sbyangs//byang chub sems ni kun kyi 
99a2(phal chen): bsam pa bzhin//smon lam rgya mtsho bsam yas rnam sbyangs shing /sems can las rnams rgya mtsho bsam yas pas//phyogs kun zhing rnams rgya mtsho thams cad 'byung //byung chub sems dpa' kun kyi rnam 
99a3(phal chen): 'phrul dang //thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la 'jug pa'i sgos//smon lam rgya mtsho thams cad mngon sgrubs shing //nam mkha'i dbyings la 'byam klas zhing 'byung ngo//spyod pa rgya mtsho mtha' dang dbus med rnams//spyod 
99a4(phal chen): cing tshad med bde gshegs yul la 'jug/phyogs kyi zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun spyod zhing //bskal pa mtha' yas zhing rnams re rer spyod//sems can bsam dbyangs bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i//las kyi zhing 
99a5(phal chen): rnams rgya mtsho thams cad byung //nyon mongs las kyis de dag nyon mongs bcas//mos dang bsam pas rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs//zhing rnams rgya mtsho rnam dag rgyan can rnams/rin cen rgyus byung sna tshogs dri med pa/dang ba 
99a6(phal chen): rgya mtsho rnam par spel ba'i phyir//rgyal sras rnams ni phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams spyod//chos dbyings rdul gyi khongs su rgyal sras rnams//kun tu bzang po spyod pa spyod pa dag/rdul gyi khongs su mtha' yas zhing ston te//

99a8(phal chen): skad cig sems kyis dus gsum byung ba yi//zhing 'byung tshul dang 'jug pa kun kyang ston//sangs rgyas rnams kyis dus gsum zhing stan pa//rnam par snang mdzad rgya mtsho'i zhing mi zad//tshul gyi khyan par mtha' yas sna tshogs pas

99b2(phal chen): zhing 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtshe na'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed de 'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'tsho la la ni rgyan thams cad kyi rgya mtsho kun la rab tu gnas so//'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho la la ni nam mkha'i byings kyi gnas la

99b5(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la ni sgyu ma lta bu'i las kyis byung ba lha ma yin dang mthu bo che lta bu'i lag na rdo rje rgya mtsho la rab tu gnas sa//'jig rten gyi khams brgya mtsho la la ni//'jig rten gyi dbang po thams cad kyi lus kyi

104b6(phal chen): yas zhing rnams byung /bsod nams sbrin ni nam mkha' mnyam bskyed cing//'gro ba kun phan bdo don spyod sbyang de//bsod nams rgya mtsho sems can kun bstan bas//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho kun kyang 'byung //

104b8(phal chen): med dang /chos rnams thams cad yangs pa yongs sbyangs te//sna tshogs thabs sgos sems can sbyangs bas na//'di 'dra yid 'ong rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams 'byung //rgyan rnams kun kyi tshul dang sa sbyangs shing //
105a1(phal chen): @#//rgyal ba'i yon tan rgya mtsho kun zhugs nas//'gro ba kun gyi sdug bsngal mtsho bskams pa//zhing gi mtsho yangs 'di kun dga' bar 'gyur//stobs rnams rgya mtsho gzhan mang yongs sbyangs 
105a2(phal chen): shing /'gro ba 'di 'dra tshad med rnams sbyangs te//sangs rgyas rgya mtsho yongs kun bsnyen bkur pas//zhing rnams rgya mtsho rnam mang rnam par dag/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho re rer 
105a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i tshul rgya mtsho ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya ste//'di ltar sangs rgyas 'byung ba la la ni sku chu ngur kun tu ston to//'di lta ste sangs rgyas 'byung pa la 
105a4(phal chen): la ni chos kyi dbyings thams cad gzugs kyi skus gang bar ston to//sangs rgyas 'byung ba la ni sku tshe'i tshad mi ring ngo//sangs rgyas 'byung ba las la ni sku tshe'i tshad ring ngo//sangs rgyas 'byud ba la la ni sangs rgyas 
105a5(phal chen): kyi zhing gcig yongs su dag par byin kyis rlob bo//sangs rgyas 'byung ba lal ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgyamatsho thams cad yongs su dag par byin gyis rlob po//sangs rgyas 'byung ba lam ni tshul gcig gis bstan pa'i 
105a6(phal chen): chos kyi 'khor lo byin gyis rlob po//sadas rgyas 'byung ba la la ni/tshul bsam gyis mi khyab par bstan pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo byin kyis rlob po//sangs rgyas byung ba la la ni sems can gyi khams nyung du rnam par 
105a7(phal chen): gdul ba byin gyis rlob po//sang rgyas 'byung ba la la ni sems cad rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa rnam par gdul ba byin gyis rlob ste/de dag las stsogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho re rer sangs rgyas 'byung 
105a8(phal chen): ba'i tshul rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i tshul rgya mtsho'i phyogs 'di nyid yongs su 
105b1(phal chen): bstan pa'i byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so/rgyal ba 'byung thabs rnams pa sna tshogs kyi/sgo nas zhing rnams rgya mtshor rab tu 'byung //de dag sems 
105b2(phal chen): can bsam pa ji bzhin mkhyen//sangs rgyas thabs kyi sgo mang rgya mtsho'o//sangs rgyas chos skur bsam gyis mi khyab ste//mi g.yor ma yin mtshan nyid med pa'i gzugs//gzugs kyi khyad par 'gro ba 'di dag du//sems 
105b3(phal chen): can bsam pa'i phyogs bzhin ston la ltos//sku tshe thung par la la rnam par ston//la la skal ba bsam yas byin kyis rlob//chos kyi sku ni phyogs rnams kun kyi sgo//rab tu mdzes par 'gro ba 'di ni bzhugs 
105b4(phal chen): la la zhing rnams rgya mtsho bsam yas dag/sbyong zhing 'byam klas phyogs bcu kun na yang //la la zhing gcig rnam par sbyong ba mdzad//smon lam ma lus kun kyang tshul gcig go/la la bsam gyis mi khyab 
105b5(phal chen): theg pa'i rgyan//sems can yid bzhin sangs rgyas ston pa mdzad//la la theg pa gcig kyang rab ston te//rgyal ba'i thabs ni gcig kyang mtha' yas so//rang byung la la byang chub sangs rgyas nas//sems can mi 
105b6(phal chen): mang lam la gzhog pa mdzad/la la skad cig gcig gis sems can rnams//rgya mtsho bsam yas byang chub don du 'dul//la la spu'i sgo nas bsam yas pa'i//sangs rgyas sprul ba'i sprin rnams rab tu 'gyed 
105b7(phal chen): 'gro ba kun tu de dag mngon bkang zhing //thabs kyi sgo nas sems can 'dul ba mdzad//la la sangs rgyas gsung gis kun bkang zhing //zhing du sems can yid bzhin rab tu sgrog/bsam yas bskal pa de dag 
105b8(phal chen): gsung sgrog cing /sems can rgya mtsho mtha' yas rnam par 'dul//la la zhing rgyan bsam gyis mi khyab ste//'khor rnams yongs dag kun nas 'khor bcas pas//zhing mang rgya mtsho phyogs su gang byas shing //

106b1(phal chen): sna tshogs tshul 'jug rgyan rnams gzhan dang gzhan//phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun na snang //skal pa'i tshul ni rgya mtsho grangs kyang med//bskal pa mi mthun 'gro ba kun dang mnyam//phyogs 
106b2(phal chen): dbyings 'jig rten rgya mtsho'i khams na snang //bskal pa mtha' yas la la ring dang thung //bskal pa sna tshogs rgyal ba'i sgras kyang bsgrags//rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams 'di dag phyogs dbyings snang //
106b3(phal chen): byin rlabs bskal ba zhing rdul snyed dang ldan//la la gzhan rnams bskal ba gcig gis 'jug/la la skal grangs smon lam du mas byung //bskal ba la la dri med nyon mongs can//la la rnam 
106b4(phal chen): dag nyon mongs rnam par dag/bskal ba ming ni smon lam rgya mtsho'i tshul gyis byung //sems can 'du shes rnam pas tha dad tshul la 'jug/bskal ba zhing gi rdul snyed sngan cad spyod spyad pa//'jig rten 
106b5(phal chen): khams rnams rgya mtsho shin tu yangs shing dag/de dag kun nas sangs rgyas yul rgyan tshad kyang med//'di dag rgya chen bskal ba'i 'jig rten rgya mtsho'i khams//bskal ba'i 'od dang 'od gzer rin chen ming sna 
106b6(phal chen): tshogs/bcom ldan dbyangs dang 'od mnyam 'od 'phro'i mig snang ba//snang dang dri med bzang po bskal ba'i ming la 'jug/bskal ba 'di dag rnam dag bskal ba kun las byung //dri med bskal ba la lar sangs 
106b7(phal chen): rgyas gcig skye'o//bskal ba lar lar mi'i gtso bo mtha' yas 'byung //skal ba tshul rnams thams cad sa tshul zad mi shes//smon lam stobs mthus bskal ba sna tshogs 'jug par 'gyur//
106b8(phal chen): bskal ba la la mtha' yas rnams kyang gcig tu 'dus//bskal ba la la gcig kyang bskal ba'i bye ba'i mthu//bskal ba rgya mtsho kun kyang sna tshogs sgo nas 'jug/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten khams 
107a1(phal chen): @#//kyi nang nas sgra yang 'byin//bskal pa la lar sprul pa kun gyi rgyud kyang 'byung //ming gi rgyan rnams rnam par dag pa'ang de la yod//bskal pa la la sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul mthu yis 'byung /bskal pa'i tshul 
107a2(phal chen): rnams kun gyi 'di ni rgyan yangs yin//zhing dang bskal pa rgya mtsho tshul ni gcig gis dag/zhing gi rdul rnams kun kyang tshad med gzhal du med//skad cig yud tsam nyi ma'i grangs kyis 
107a3(phal chen): bskal par 'gyur//'di dag thams cad sems can 'du shes rnam par byung //kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i bskal pa mtha' yas pa'i rgyud kyi tshul rgya mtsho ni 'jig rten gyi khams su rgya 
107a4(phal chen): mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed shes par bya ste/'di ltar chos nyid rab tu thob pas 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho dag bskal pas 'jig cing 'du ba la rab tu 'jug par gnas pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tshor gtogs 
107a5(phal chen): pa'i sems can rnams kyi nyon mongs pas bskal pa mtha' yas pa kun nas gnas pa dang //'di lta ste 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsher gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi bsod nams rgya mtsho rgya chen po bstsogs 
107a6(phal chen): pa rnams par dag pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya matso kun nas nyon mongs la rnam par dag par gnas pa dang //'di ltar ste byang chub sems dpa' mos pa sna tshogs kyi spyod pa rnams skye bas 'jig rten gyi khams rgya
107a7(phal chen): mtsho rnam par dag la kun nas nyon mongs par gnas pa dang /'di lta ste sems can rgya mtsho rgya chen po byang chub kyi sems la gnas pas 'jig rten gyi khams rgyu mtsho shin tu dag par gnas pa dang 'di lta 
107a8(phal chen): ste byang chub sems dpa' rnams 'jig rten gyi khams gzhan dang gzhan du 'dod pas bskal pa rgyan tha dad pa'i tshul du gnas pa dang /'di ltar ste phyogs bcu'i byang chub sems dpa' rnam 'jig rten gyi khams rgya
107b1(phal chen): mtshor 'dus pas bskal pa mtha' yas pa rgyan shin tu mang por kun nas gnas pa dang/'di lta ste de bzhin gshegs pa yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bas 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag gi rgyan nub par 'gyur ba dang//
107b2(phal chen): 'di lta ste sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i tshe 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtshe rnams kyi rgyan kyis brgyan par kun nas gnas pa dang /'di lta ste de bzhin gsheDa pa rnam par 'phrul bas 'jig rten gyi khams rgyu mtsho'i 
107b3(phal chen): bskal pa mtha' yas pas yongs su dag par gnas pa dang//de dag las stsogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i skal pa rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa'i rgyud kyi tshul rgya mtsho dang kun nas gnas pa'i tshul ni 'jig rten 
107b4(phal chen): gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i bskal pa mtha' yas pa'i tshul rgya mtsho 'di nyid yongs su bstan 
107b5(phal chen): pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhing rnams rgya mtsho'i chos nyid 'di dag ltos//chos kyi byung ste ji ltar gnas pa ltar/
107b6(phal chen): de dag 'byung zhing 'jigs par 'gyur ba yang /zhing rnams rgya mtsho thams cad rab tu snang//sems can nyon mongs mi bzad ldan pa dang//las dang nyon mongs ngan pas bsgribs pa'i rnams//de dag bsam la zhing 
107b7(phal chen): gi rgya mtsho rnams//kun nas nyon mongs ldan par gyur pa yin//sems can rnam dag bsam pa ldan rnams dang//bsod nams brtan dang rnam dag spyod yul can//rnam dag yul la nyon mongs ldan gyur rnams
107b8(phal chen): de dag mos pa sna tshogs spyod yul gyi//byang chub sems dpa' skye ba'i dus dag na//bskal pa de ni rnam par dag par snang//sems can nyon mngos ldan par gyur pa yang//de'i chos nyid bsam yas yangs
108a1(phal chen): @#//ba'i/byang chub bsam pa ji ltar bskyed pa dang /de dag bsam pa ji ltar rnam 'phel bas/de bzhin bskal ba rgya mtsho mang po dag/bsam gyis mi khyab zhing rgyan yongs su spyod//bya ba mang po mtha' yas mi 
108a2(phal chen): bzad pa'i/byang chub sems dpa' phyogs bcu kun rgyu yang //rgyan la rnam pa tha dad yod pa min//bskal ba tha dad tshul gyis bye brag snang //rdul rnams re re sbubs su dus kun tu//sangs rgyas zhing gi rdul dang mnyam pa yi//
108a3(phal chen): rgyal sras ji tshul 'dod pa de bzhin du/zhing rnams rgya mtsho de tshul dag par gyur/gal te rgyal ba rnams ni skyes 'gyur na//zhing rnams rgyan kyis shin tu brgyan par 'gyur//bsam pa yongs su dag de bzhin rgyas 'gyur te//
108a4(phal chen): 'di na zhing rgyan phun sum tshogs pa'o//gal te rgyal ba mya ngan 'das gyur pa//de tshe de zhing rgyan rnams 'grib par 'gyur//sems can rnams kyang snod du rung min//zhing gi rgya mtsho de yang nyon mongs ldan//sangs rgyas rdzu 
108a5(phal chen): 'phrul rnam 'phrul yang sa bdag/bsam gyis mi khyab gang che ston pa na//de tshe zhing rnams rgya mtsho mang po dag/kun kyang rgyan kyis rnam par brgyan par 'gyur//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya 
108a6(phal chen): mtsho tha mi dad pa'i tshul ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya ste//de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' rnams 
108a7(phal chen): kyis gsungs so//gsung ngo gsung bar 'gyur ro//de ni 'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho gcig tu 'jig rten gyi khams rgya matso'i rdul shin tu phra bsnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams tha mi dad pa dang /'di lta ste 'jig 
108a8(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag tu sangs rgyas skye zhing 'byung ba tha mi dad pa dang /'dir lta ste/'jig rten gyi kham rgya mtsho dag gi snying po byang chub thams cad nas chos kyi dbyings kyis phyogs rgyas par 'gengs pa tha mi 
108b1(phal chen): dad pa dang /'di lta ste 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag gi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor tha mi dad pa dang //'di lta ste 'jig rten gyi khyams rgya mtsho dag gi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 'od kyi 
108b2(phal chen): dkyil 'khor chos kyi dbyings rgyas par 'gengs pa tha mi dad pa dang /'di lta ste 'jug rten gyi khams rgya mtsho bdag tu sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa'i sprin dang /mtshan gyi 'khor lo tha mi dad pa dang /'di la ste 'jig rten gyi khams 
108b3(phal chen): rgya mtsho dag tu sangs rgyas thams cad kyis dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor bskal pa mtha' yas par brjod cing rgyas par bya ba'i byin gyis rlabs tha mi dad pa dang /'di'i lta ste 'jig rten gyi khamasargya mtsho dag tu chos kyi 'khor 
108b4(phal chen): lo'i tshul tha mi dad pa dang //'di lta ste 'jig rten gyis khams rgya mtsho dag tu rdul shin tu phra ba gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya cho thams cad yongs su 'du ba tha mi dad pa dang //'di lta ste 'jig rten gyi
108b5(phal chen): khams rgya mtsho dag du rdul 'byam klas pa thams cad la dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi yul rgya chen po ston cing brjod pa tha mi dad pa dag ste//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de dag las stsogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams
108b6(phal chen): rgya mtsho tha mi dad pa'i tshul 'jig rten gyi khams rgyal mtsho'i rdul shin tu 'phra ba snyed du shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho tha mi dad pa'i tshu rgya cher

110b3(phal chen): dag/sangs rgyas rnam par snang bas smon lam che mdzad do//'od snang btang ba mkha'i dkyil na gnas pa ni//rlung gi phung pos btab kyang mi bskyod mi g.yo ste//nor bu kun rgyan sangs rgyas gzugs brnyan snying po'o//
110b4(phal chen): me tog mdzod bkye gtor ba rin cen yid 'ong bzhin//sngon gyi smon lam dbang gis mkha'i dbyings la gnas//rgya mtsho'i rgyan rnams thams cad sna tshogs bstan par gnas//phyogs dang phyogs 
110b5(phal chen): mtshams kun tu 'od kyi sprin kyang btong //nor bu thams cad sgo nas byang chub sems dpa'i rnams//byung zhing 'od gzer sprin 'bar phyogs rnams kun tu 'dor//'od gzer me tog tshon dang 'od gzer 
110b6(phal chen): 'khor lo'i tshogs/phyogs rnams ma lus kun gyi chos dbyings khyab par byed//rin chen kun gyis 'od gzer sprin rnams bkye pa ni//phyogs dang phyogs mtshams kun gyi zhing rnams khyab par byed//sdug bsngal thams cad 
110b7(phal chen): bcil zhing byang chub mchog la 'god//'jig rten rgya mtsho thams cad 'od 'bar rab tu 'bar//'gro ba kun dang mnyam pa'i sprul pa sna tshogs rnams//rin chen kun dang spu rnams kun nas rgyal ba 'gyed//brgya byin 
110b8(phal chen): dag dang tshangs pa 'khor lo sgyur 'dra dang //'gro ba kun gyi lus su lus rnams kun kyang gnas//sprul pas 'od bkye chos kyi dbyings kyi chad mnyam pa//'od gzer sgo nas sangs rgyas thams cad mtshan kyang sgrag 
111a1(phal chen): @#//'gro ba lus kun gyi bsam pa tshim byas nas//tshul rnams sna tshogs sgo nas sems can rnam par 'dul//'jig rten rgya mtsho me tog snying po de nang na//rdul phran kun kyi khongs na chos dbyings thams 
111a2(phal chen): cad kyi/rin chen 'od kyi sangs rgyas sprin rnams 'bar bar snang/rnam par snang mdzad chos dbyings rnam 'phrul zhing la ltos/bskal pa rgya mtshor 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal bstsal byas te/smon lam yangs pa'i sprin ni chos kyi 
111a3(phal chen): dbyings mnyam dang//bzang po'i spyod pa rnyes shing ye shes sa ldan pa'i//dgongs pa las ni rgyan mdzes de dag byung ba yin////'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho gzhi dang snying po me tog gi rgyan kyis brgyan pa'i 
111a4(phal chen): yon tan rgya mtsho yongs su dag pas snang ba zhes bya ste/le'u lnga pa'o////kye rgyal ba'i sas dag de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho gzhi dang snying po me toggi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i 'khor yug chen po ni 
111a5(phal chen): nor bu'i rgyal po pad mo nyi ma'i gzhi la rten pa ste//nor bu'i rigs rgya mtsho spos kyi tsan dan thams cad las grub pa//nor bu'i rgyal po rgyan kyi yul thams cad kyis lhun shin tu 'phags shing zil kyis gnon pa

181b3(phal chen): ba de dag ni/sangs rgyas rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi lams rgya mtsho bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba kyis/dus gsum rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi kham rgya mtsho bcu'i 
181b4(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du gsungs te/bstan kyang zad par mi rung ngo//'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho 'jig rten gyi khams rgyam tsho thams cad kyi gnas kyi snying po zhes bya ba de ni 'khor los bsgyur ba'i

181b7(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho re re na yang rin po che rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi khams rgya tsho bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed yod de/'di ltar sangs rgyas kyi spyan rin po che rgya mtsho dang /de bzhin du sangs rgyas 
181b8(phal chen): kyi snyan dang /shangs dang ljags dang sku dang thugs rin po che rgya mtsho dang/sangs rgyas kyi gzugs dang sgra dang dri dang ro dang reg bya dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos rin po che rgya mtsho dang thams cad kyis blta na yid

242a4(phal chen): ba'i zhing rnams kun nas brgyan pa byed//de dag bde gshegs dbu la gdugs kyi mchod//rin chen dra ba sna tshogs rgyan ldan pa//dar gyi lda ldi pa dan yongs sbyangs shing //dril phreng rgyal ba'i gsung sgrog 'dzin 
242a5(phal chen): 'dzin bar byed//de dag lag pa'i mthil nas bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i//mchod pa rgyal ba gcig la byed pa ltar//ma lus rgyan ba rnams la'ang de bzhin te//drang srong ting 'dzin rnam 'phrul de dra'o//
242a6(phal chen): de dag 'gro ba yongs su da ye shes kyis//rnam par 'phrul ba'i ting 'dzin mang na bsgrub shing //bya ba'i tshul dang yon tan thams cad du//thabs mang stong gis sems can 'dul ba byed//la la de bzhin 
242a7(phal chen): gshegs pa mchod sgo dang /sbyin pa bsam gyis mi khyab gtong ba'i sgo//sbyangs pa'i yon tan kun gyi tshul khrims sgo//bzod pa mi zad mi sku sgo dag dang //la la brtul zhugs dka' thub brtson 'grus sgo

242b1(phal chen): sgo ngag dang //bsod nams yongs su bstsags dang ye shes sgo//bden pa brten pa'i rnam thar sgo dag dang //la la stobs dang dbang po lam gyi sgo//nyan thos theg pa rnam grol sgo ngag dang //rang rgyal theg pa rnam par dag

242b4(phal chen): bsam pa ji bzhin sems can 'dul ba byed//de dag mtshan mar bzung bar mi nus te//ting 'dzin rnam 'phrul tshul ni de 'dra'o//de dag dga' ba'i rgyan kyis ting 'dzin mchog/'gro ba thams cad

242b6(phal chen): rnams/thams cad bsam pa rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir//sbyin pa yongs su gtong zhing 'gro don byed//de dag zas mchog skom dang rgyan mchog dang //gos dang dga' ba rin chen sna tshogs dang //rgyal srid nor 
242b7(phal chen): dang bdag stug yongs btang bas//sbyin la mos pa'i 'gro ba 'dul ba byed//de dag 'tshan rab kun gyis lus bstan cing //gos dang rgyan mchog yongs su bgos btang ste//phreng bas rnam rgyan 
242b8(phal chen): spos kyis bskus byas te//gzugs rnams yongs su bstan pas sems can 'dul//mthong bas dga' bde rab tu mos pa la//de dag gzugs mchog gzugs bzang cha byad 
243a1(phal chen): @#//mdzod/gzugs kyi dam pa yongs su ston byed cing //gzugs la mos pa'i sems can 'dul ba byed//de dag ka la bing ka'i sgra snyan dang //ko ko li kun dang pa'i sgra dbyangs dang gang 
243a2(phal chen): dang tshangs pa'i mi'am ci'i sgras/mos pa thams cad tshul bzhin chos kyang ston//de dag stong phrag brgya bcu rtsa bzhi'o//gang gis rgyal ba 'gro ba'i don mdzad pa'i//chos kyi bye brag sgo mo de dag gis//

244a3(phal chen): du yang de dag mkhan por 'gyur//nyi ma'i rjes spyad dka' thub lnga ldan dang //khyim dang ba lang brtul zhugs ri dgas spyod//ge'u can mu stegs bcu gsum spyod pa'i rnams//de dag kun 'ang de dag slob dpon 'gyur//

244b3(phal chen): dag bden pa'i des tshig tshul shes bzhin//de ltar rgyal ba'i chos rnams ma lus pa'i//chos nyid bsam yas tshig lam shes pas ston//de dag ting 'dzin rnams 'phrul de 'dra'o//de dag 'gro ba de
